# Tier 1: government documents (and filings lodged with regulators)

*Added Nov 23, 2026 (UTC): new rows in sections 2 to 5, new sections 2.6 and 3.1 to 3.8, corrections to present rows, and end sections 8 to 11 (documented negatives, saved-but-not-checkable items, read-not-saved items, leads), from the findings of the project's follow-up searches; the working notes behind them are not part of this record.*

*Added Nov 25, 2026 (UTC): new rows in sections 2 to 5, 8, 9 and 11 from a further round of searches (official texts of 2024 to 2026, purchase notices, the carriers' own notices, companies' filings and results calls, spokespeople's answers, the import lists of 2024 to 2026) and corrections to present rows; nine provinces are now counted with a share for home-made computing (the ninth, Zhejiang's, is a target for one sector); the working notes behind them are not part of this record.*

This is the first of two tiers. Tier 2, reporting, is `TIER2_REPORTING.md`. Company filings are kept here under their own headings (sections 3 and 4) and are marked as filings; they are not government documents. The project's owner, Nov 23, 2026 (04:01 UTC), asked whether they should stay here or move to Tier 2: "keep them."

Written Nov 20, 2026 (UTC), after the project owner's question: "are we leaning too heavily on reporting. what about government documents themselves." First written as a local note; now part of the project's public copy.

**Revised later on Nov 20, 2026** after three additions: (1) every one of the 103 lines of the policies list was read against its saved page (97 confirmed, 5 confirmed in part, 1 page unreadable; all 207 quotations found again by script); (2) the Internet Archive was asked for the pages not held; (3) the Commerce Secretary's Senate testimony of Apr 22, 2026 was found and saved. The items of that reading that bear on the argument are folded in below.

**Revised Nov 23, 2026** after the second search (five searches run side by side, 1,216 rows) and about thirty follow-up searches. Added: Nvidia's own wording on China filing by filing; the other chip makers' filings; Chinese companies' annual reports and results calls; the House Foreign Affairs Committee's table of licence applications by status; US reports that read Beijing's acts as leverage; published commentary in state and Party media (new section 2.6); seven more provincial plans, local voucher rules and tenders; the evaluation centre's own lists; official trade statistics. Every quotation added was found by script in the saved copy.

**Rule for this file.** Six kinds of source are used as evidence: (A) texts issued by the Chinese party-state; (B) public statements by named Chinese officials on official sites; (C) United States government documents and officials' recorded words; (D) company filings and published statements; (E) published commentary in state and Party media, and published statements of industry bodies (section 2.6), which are not orders; (F) named people's words on the record as carried by a transcript or a press report, marked as such. Machine captions of a broadcast are marked «machine captions, not checked by ear». Press reports that cite unnamed people appear only in section 6, as the second tier; a press report of a named person's words on the record is kind (F) and is marked as such. Every quotation was read in the saved copy (the id in square brackets names it). English renderings of Chinese are the project's own and are not official translations.

**How much of the first assessment was reporting.** Of the 55 sources the first assessment cites, 19 are official texts or statements, 4 are company filings or statements, 20 are news reports, and the rest analysis and other. So about four in ten of its sources were press.

---

## 1. What the documents establish, in six sentences

1. Since 2016 the Party's leader has said in published speeches that core technology controlled by others is the greatest hidden danger and that home-made products "must be used"; in 2025 the Central Committee wrote "extraordinary measures" for integrated circuits into the five-year plan.
2. That line is in binding and planning documents: Party and government organs at township level and above, the public institutions directly under Party committees and governments at those levels, and the departments' public institutions that provide support to those organs, must when buying desktop and portable computers and general-purpose servers require that the CPU and the operating system pass the security-and-reliability evaluation (joint notices of the finance and industry ministries), and the published lists of processors (2023) and of AI chips (2026) that passed name only entities registered in China (applying is voluntary and open only to such entities); where products made in China and other products compete in one government purchase, those made in China get a 20% price preference in the evaluation; provinces and cities set targets for the share of home-made chips in new computing centres (Jiangsu 70% or more by 2030 and 50% by 2025, both marked indicative; Sichuan aims for over 50% and Guangdong for 70%; Shanghai over 50% by 2025 and over 70% independently controllable; Tianjin over 60% by 2026; Hebei 60% or more of software and hardware; Inner Mongolia 50% or more; Shandong 90% in a newspaper's account). Two limits, both in the documents: every binding purchase rule read is addressed to state bodies or to public money, and in each of the three purchase notices the same sentence adds that other units may leave this requirement out; and every home-made percentage found is provincial or municipal, none national.
3. Regulators acted against Nvidia by name: the market regulator announced on Dec 9, 2024 that it had recently opened an antitrust case and the cyberspace regulator summoned the company over the H20 (Jul 31, 2025).
4. Official Chinese documents also show what the companies used and wanted: a Shanghai government reply says that among the city's computing suppliers internet companies' own clusters run mainly on Nvidia cards while the three big operators mainly buy Huawei's and other home-made intelligent-computing clusters; an official of the state planner's policy research office said at its press conference that it is guiding home-made large models to do more to adapt to home-made chips; a Party-journal investigation says migration is costly and slow.
5. Two companies say in filings that the state stopped purchases: Nvidia ("restricted by the PRC government") and Alibaba ("requested Chinese technology companies to halt purchases of certain chips"). The US Commerce Secretary told the Senate the same on Apr 22, 2026, as his "understanding": the cloud companies "want to buy these chips" and the "Chinese central government has not let them as of yet".
6. The same body of documents holds the other side: every leadership text keeps an "open door" clause; ministers welcomed Nvidia by name; the commerce ministry attacks US controls and smooths the way for Chinese companies' computing centres abroad; local vouchers pay for rented computing that is not home-made too (of 90 rules found by searches that went province by province and city by city, saved and read and counted, not a census of every local government, 79 state no higher rate for home-made computing, 78 of them at one rate for all and one, Hangzhou's, at two rates by a type of computing facility it does not name, and 7 pay other computing at a lower rate; 4, Sichuan's, Ningbo's, Huangyan district's and the Changsha zone's, pay for home-made computing only; and 9 of the 79 also give home-made computing money to build, buy or run its facilities, an award for adapting to it, or a condition on suppliers, by another provision of the same rule: a premium beside a payment that stays open to other computing); two universities publish notices of Nvidia hardware, holding suppliers to the government procurement law's conditions; the 20% preference goes by where a product is made, with foreign-funded firms treated equally; and Baidu and Alibaba tell investors that their own chips are in use or in scaled production. And US government documents read parts of Beijing's conduct, antitrust investigations among them, as leverage or retaliation in trade talks, while the Trade Representative calls the purchase decision China's own (section 4).

**What follows.** On documents alone, the claim "the state's declared policy pulled against what its AI companies were using and buying" is established. The claim "Beijing forbade the purchases" is established only through the two company filings and the US officials in section 4; Beijing itself has published no such instruction and its spokesmen decline to confirm one (section 5).

---

## 2. Chinese party-state documents: the push toward home-made chips

### 2.1 The leadership line

- Xi Jinping, forum on cybersecurity and informatization, Apr 19, 2016: "核心技术受制于人是我们最大的隐患" (core technology being controlled by others is our greatest hidden danger); and of home-made products, "该用的就要用" (what should be used must be used) [cn-xi-20160419]. The same speech speaks of internet companies by kind: "一个互联网企业即便规模再大、市值再高,如果核心元器件严重依赖外国,供应链的“命门”掌握在别人手里,那就好比在别人的墙基上砌房子,再大再漂亮也可能经不起风雨,甚至会不堪一击。" (however large an internet company and however high its market value, if it depends heavily on foreign countries for core components and the "vital gate" of its supply chain is in others' hands, it is like building a house on someone else's foundation: however big and handsome, it may not stand wind and rain, and may fall at a single blow) [cn-policy-xi-coretech-2016]. This is the closest published statement by Xi to the position of the AI companies and Nvidia. It is not an order, and the same speech has the open-innovation passage (section 5). The "must be used" phrase stands in a passage that accepts first flaws: "我们自己推出的新技术新产品,在应用中出现一些问题是自然的。可以在用的过程中继续改进,不断提高质量。如果大家都不用,就是报一个课题完成报告,然后束之高阁,那永远发展不起来。" (it is natural that new technologies and products we bring out ourselves show some problems in use; they can go on being improved as they are used and their quality steadily raised; if nobody uses them, all that comes of it is a project completion report filed and then put away on a high shelf, and they will never develop) [cn-policy-xi-coretech-2016].
- Xi Jinping at an integrated-circuit maker in Wuhan, Apr 2018 (Xinhua's report of Apr 28, 2018, held from People's Daily's database of his speeches [cn-ga03-jhsjk-xi-hubei-20180428] and from the Party members' site): "装备制造业的芯片,相当于人的心脏。心脏不强,体量再大也不算强。要加快在芯片技术上实现重大突破" (chips are to equipment manufacturing what the heart is to a person; if the heart is not strong, no size makes one strong; speed up major breakthroughs in chip technology) [cn-xi-breadth-1-relay1].
- Xi Jinping, Politburo study session on AI, Apr 25, 2025: "集中力量攻克高端芯片、基础软件等核心技术" (concentrate forces to conquer core technologies such as high-end chips and basic software) [cn-xi-round-j-6].
- Central Committee, recommendations for the 15th Five-Year Plan, adopted Oct 23, 2025: "完善新型举国体制,采取超常规措施,全链条推动集成电路、工业母机、高端仪器、基础软件、先进材料、生物制造等重点领域关键核心技术攻关取得决定性突破" (improve the new whole-nation system, take extraordinary measures, and push along the whole chain for decisive breakthroughs in key core technologies in priority fields such as integrated circuits, machine tools, high-end instruments, basic software, advanced materials and biomanufacturing) [cn-policy-15fyp-recommendations]. The plan outline of March 2026 has the sentence from "采取超常规措施" onward (its opening words differ) and ties research to use: "强化以用促攻、攻用结合" (strengthen "use driving the tackling of key problems, tackling joined with use") [cn-policy-15fyp-outline].
- State Council, "AI Plus" opinions, Aug 2025: "支持人工智能芯片攻坚创新与使能软件生态培育" (support breakthrough innovation in AI chips and the growth of the enabling software ecosystem) [cn-policy-ai-plus].
- Eight bodies, "AI Plus Manufacturing" opinions, signed Dec 25, 2025 by the industry ministry, the cyberspace regulator, the state planner, the education and commerce ministries, the state-assets regulator, the market regulator and the data administration: "支持突破高端训练芯片、端侧推理芯片、人工智能服务器、高速互联、智算云操作系统等关键技术" (support breakthroughs in key technologies such as high-end training chips, on-device inference chips, AI servers, high-speed interconnect and intelligent-computing cloud operating systems); aim: "到2027年,我国人工智能关键核心技术实现安全可靠供给" (by 2027 China's key core AI technologies are in secure and reliable supply) [cn-policy-ai-manufacturing-279-wayback, a provincial department's copy in an Internet Archive capture of Jan 14, 2026]. No rule on buying foreign chips; the same document welcomes foreign capital (section 5).
- Cyberspace regulator, action plan for internet and information companies, Aug 21, 2026: "支持企业加强人工智能关键技术研究,研发高端人工智能芯片,突破高性能训练集群关键技术" (support companies in strengthening research on key AI technologies, developing high-end AI chips, and breaking through key technologies of high-performance training clusters) [cn-policy-cac-enterprise-plan-2026]. The regulator that the reporting names as restricting purchases here says it supports companies in developing high-end AI chips. It says nothing on which chips they may buy: the words 国产, 自主可控 and 安全可控 are not in the page (0 places each; "人工智能芯片" is found once, so the search could see a hit).
- State Council, AI development plan, 2017: "突破图形处理器等核心硬件" (make breakthroughs in core hardware such as graphics processors) [cn-policy-ai-plan-2017]. Graphics processors were named eight years before the Nvidia dispute.
- Xi Jinping, New Year address for 2026: "芯片自主研发有了新突破" (independent research and development of chips made new breakthroughs) [cn-policy-xi-newyear-2026].
- **Jan 30 to Oct 2026 · Xi Jinping's words on self-reliance and on the computing network in the window** (two speeches as published and three People's Daily accounts of his words; no foreign firm named). Words: Politburo study of Jan 30, 2026, published May 31: "采取超常规措施，加大重点领域关键核心技术攻关力度，尽快解决制约未来产业发展的“卡脖子”问题" [cn-xi-round-f-3]; Feb 9, 2026, at the national park for information-technology innovation and application (国家信创园): "建设社会主义现代化强国，关键在科技自立自强，北京要发挥自身优势，在这方面作出更大贡献" [cn-le22-jhsjk-xi-beijing-20260209]; Jul 8, 2026: "综合运用财政、货币、科技、产业政策，促进自主研发技术产品推广应用和迭代升级" [cn-xi-science2026]; Sept 2026: "持续做大做强先进制造业，提升产业链自主可控水平" [cn-le22-jhsjk-xi-manufacturing-20260918], an instruction relayed at the national conference on advanced manufacturing, where the Premier said: "技术路线上更加注重高端自主" [cn-le22-jhsjk-xi-manufacturing-20260918]; Oct 2026: "实施“东数西算”工程、构建全国一体化算力网，是党中央一项重要部署" [cn-le22-jhsjk-xi-ulanqab-20261016]. Cuts: FOR on direction.
- **Apr 29 and May 2026 · Ding Xuexiang, vice premier** (a named official on the record). Self-reliance with an open clause: "我國人工智能隻有走自主創新發展之路，才有應對任何遏制打壓的底氣" [cn-factions-ding-both-lines-20260429]; "人工智能的發展，不能筑起封閉的圍牆，而應搭建互利合作的橋梁" [cn-factions-ding-both-lines-20260429]; "推动国产软硬件尽快从“可用”迈向“好用”" [cn-xi-ding-compute2026] (push home-made software and hardware from usable to good to use as soon as possible). Cuts: FOR, with one AGAINST clause.
- Earlier planning: Made in China 2025 (May 2015): "到2025年,70%的核心基础零部件、关键基础材料实现自主保障" (by 2025, 70% of core basic components and key basic materials to be self-supplied) [cn-policy-mic2025]. This clause is about components and materials in general, not chips alone. The 2014 integrated-circuit outline: by 2030 the main links of the industry chain are to reach "国际先进水平" (the international advanced level) [cn-policy-2014-outline].
- **Sept 7, 2026 · Ministry of Industry and Information Technology, plan for the information and communications industry in the 15th five-year period** (a document, 工信部规〔2026〕188号; the ministry's own page and PDF, and three copies on other sites). It tells the industry to deploy 10,000- and 100,000-card clusters and step up adaptation to home-made computing chips, and sets no share. Words: "工信部规〔2026〕188号" [cn-le27-miit-ict-15fyp-2026]; "有序部署万卡、十万卡及以上智算集群，面向场景按需部署推理算力设施，加大力度适配国产算力芯片" [cn-le27-miit-ict-15fyp-2026-pdf]; "加大力度适配国产算力芯片" [cn-le14-miit-ict-15fyp-chinanews] (step up adaptation to home-made computing chips). Cuts: FOR, without a number.
- **2023 to 2026 · Ministry of Industry and Information Technology, its own published texts on computing and telecom** (documents, the ministry's site). They encourage home-made or secure-and-reliable kit and set no share: across 54 saved texts the share words 不低于, 比例 and 占比 occur 56 times and none is near 国产, 本国 or 芯片 (by a script's count: tables/miit_texts_word_counts.tsv). Words: the computing-infrastructure action plan of Oct 8, 2023, "工信部联通信〔2023〕180号" [cn-le27-miit-compute-infra-plan-2023], six bodies including "国务院国有资产监督管理委员会" [cn-le27-miit-compute-infra-plan-2023], "聚焦CPU、GPU、操作系统、存储等关键产品，推动关键技术试点验证" [cn-le27-miit-compute-infra-plan-2023-pdf]; the ministry's general office notice on fostering AI application service providers, dated Aug 27, 2026 (posted Aug 31), "鼓励服务商提升对安全可靠的操作系统、数据库、人工智能训练推理芯片等基础软硬件产品相关集成应用能力" [cn-le27-miit-ai-service-providers-2026] (encourage providers to raise their ability to integrate and apply secure-and-reliable operating systems, databases, AI training and inference chips and other basic software and hardware products); the guide annexed to the IDC notice of Jan 2025, "涉及为党政机关等重点客户提供服务的，鼓励IDC业务经营者使用自主可控的网络与数据安全设备" [cn-le27-miit-idc-data-security-2025-pdf] (network and data-security equipment, not chips); the AI+ICT opinions of Jun 2026, "加速国产AI技术在关键行业的规模化渗透与应用创新" [cn-le27-miit-ai-ict-2026-pdf]. Against or limiting: the compute-foundation call asks DPUs to fit "支持与国内外主流CPU、GPU芯片平台的适配" [cn-le27-miit-compute-base-2025-pdf] (mainstream Chinese and foreign CPU and GPU platforms); the electronics plan for 2025 to 2026: "切实保障外资企业在要素获取、资质许可、标准制定、政府采购方面的国民待遇" [cn-le27-miit-elec-stable-2025-pdf]. Cuts: FOR on direction; AGAINST a published quota.
- **Jun 1, 2026 (posted Jun 18) · Seven bodies, plan for platform-economy firms of all sizes, 2026 to 2028 (工信部联信管〔2026〕119号), full text from the ministry** (a document; the «hard tech» plan of search summaries). 国产 0, 自主可控 0, 安全可靠 0, 芯片 1 (control: 算力 10). Its one chip clause: "加快推进高端芯片、下一代操作系统、下一代智能终端等重点前沿领域技术和产品研发突破" [cn-le27-miit-platform-plan-2026-pdf]; the hard-tech phrase is about investment: "投早投小投长期投硬科技" [cn-le27-miit-platform-plan-2026-pdf]; issuers: "工业和信息化部 中央网信办 国家发展改革委 科技部 商务部 市场监管总局 国家数据局" [cn-le27-miit-platform-plan-2026]. No 75% share (section 8). Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Jul 18, 2024 (posted Aug 6) · State-assets regulator and state planner, guidance on central SOEs' purchasing (国资发改革规〔2024〕53号)** (a document). Central SOEs are to lead in using innovative products in chips and other fields, and may reserve shares of purchases for first-of-kind products on the lists of the industry ministry and other departments; the same text requires equal treatment of all market participants and does not say home-made. Words: "在卫星导航、芯片、高端数控机床、工业机器人、先进医疗设备等科技创新重点领域，充分发挥中央企业采购使用的主力军作用，带头使用创新产品" [cn-le27-sasac-procurement-guidance-2024]; "鼓励企业预留采购份额并先试先用" [cn-le27-sasac-procurement-guidance-2024]; "平等对待中央企业内部和外部各市场参与主体" [cn-le27-sasac-procurement-guidance-2024]. Cuts: FOR on direction, with limits.
- **Jul 21 and Sept 28, 2026 · State-assets regulator: home-made-chip computing offered by central SOEs** (documents and a report on its site). Words: "向中小企业、科研院所免费提供中央企业2000卡规模国产芯片算力" [cn-le27-sasac-reply-00311-20260928] (SMEs and research institutes are given, free of charge, central SOEs' home-made-chip computing on the scale of 2,000 cards); "扩展智算供给至13类，覆盖国产芯片市占率超90%" [cn-le27-sasac-ai-results-20260721]. A service, not a rule. Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Dec 2024 (posted Jan 6, 2025) · State planner, data administration and industry ministry, guide to building national data infrastructure** (a document). It asks for built-in protection against backdoors in chips and other parts; the word 国产 is not in it (0 places). Words: "提供应对芯片、软件、硬件、协议等内置后门、漏洞安全威胁的内生防护能力" [cn-le14-nda-data-infra-guide-20250106-pdf] (provide built-in protection against security threats from backdoors and vulnerabilities built into chips, software, hardware and protocols). Cuts: CONTEXT.
- Xi Jinping at the Chahar high-tech development zone in Ulanqab, Inner Mongolia, Oct 16, 2026 (Xinhua's report): "实施“东数西算”工程、构建全国一体化算力网，是党中央一项重要部署。" [cn-ga31-companies-36] (carrying out the East Data West Computing project and building a nationally integrated computing network is an important arrangement of the Party Centre); to the people of the computing-centre projects, "希望大家认真落实高效、绿色、安全的要求" [cn-ga31-companies-36] (a hope that everyone will earnestly carry out the requirements of efficiency, greenness and security). No chip, maker or purchasing condition is named. Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Dec 10, 2025 to Mar 13, 2026 · The declared line after October 2025: Xi's speech to the Central Economic Work Conference (published in Qiushi 2026 No. 4), the State Council's government work report (delivered by Premier Li Qiang, Mar 5, 2026) and the 15th five-year plan outline (Xinhua's authorised text of Mar 13, 2026)** (documents and the leader's published words). They carry the line of the October 2025 recommendations on in general words: integrated circuits are to be built into an "emerging pillar industry", central and state firms are encouraged to take the lead in opening application scenes, key core technologies are to be tackled "along the whole chain" with the new whole-nation system, very large intelligent-computing clusters are to be built, and the computing plan asks for an "independently controllable" software and hardware ecosystem, with models and chips developed together. The work report counts among 2025's results "new breakthroughs in independent chip research and development". None names a foreign firm, an import rule for chips or a home-made share: in the work report 英伟达 0, 出口管制 0, 自主可控 0, 安全可靠 0 (control: 集成电路 2, 芯片 1); in the outline 英伟达 0 (control: 自主可控 3, 芯片 1); in Xi's excerpt 芯片 0, 英伟达 0 (control: 集成电路 1). The outline also supports meeting computing needs in several ways, among them government purchase of computing services and the leasing of computing power. Words: "3月5日,李强总理代表国务院在十四届全国人大四次会议上作《政府工作报告》" [cn-ga31-workreport-7]; "芯片自主研发有了新突破" [cn-ga31-workreport-7]; "工业机器人、集成电路产量分别增长28%、10.9%" [cn-ga31-workreport-7]; "鼓励央企国企带头开放应用场景,打造集成电路、航空航天、生物医药、低空经济等新兴支柱产业" [cn-ga31-workreport-7]; "发挥新型举国体制优势,全链条推进关键核心技术攻关" [cn-ga31-workreport-7]; "实施超大规模智算集群、算电协同等新基建工程,加强全国一体化算力监测调度" [cn-ga31-workreport-7]; "加快培育自主可控、协同运行的软硬件生态" [cn-ga31-workreport-8] (speed up the growth of an independently controllable, jointly running software and hardware ecosystem); "强化“模芯云用”协同创新" [cn-ga31-workreport-8]; "提升高端芯片、光电子器件、基础软件和工业软件等产业水平" [cn-ga31-workreport-8]; "支持通过政府购买算力服务、算力租赁等多种方式满足算力需求" [cn-ga31-workreport-8]; "打造集成电路、航空航天、生物医药等新兴支柱产业" [cn-ga31-workreport-57]; "※这是习近平总书记2025年12月10日在中央经济工作会议上讲话的一部分。" [cn-ga31-workreport-57]; "来源:《求是》2026/04 作者:习近平" [cn-ga31-workreport-57]. Cuts: FOR on direction (the declared line holds through 2026 at the top), with the limit that none of these texts says what companies may buy; the opening clauses of the same texts are in section 5.
- **Jan 18, 2024 and Apr 25, 2025 · The industry ministry and six other bodies on future industries; and Xi's Politburo study session, the passages beside the one the record holds** (a document; the leader's words in Xinhua's readout). The seven bodies' opinions on future industries ask for faster breakthroughs in GPU chips, low-latency cluster links and heterogeneous resource management, and for very large intelligent-computing centres. At the session where Xi called for conquering high-end chips (held), he also named government procurement among the policy tools for AI and called for broad international cooperation on AI. Neither text names a foreign firm or tells companies what to buy (英伟达 0 in both). Words: "发文字号: 工信部联科〔2024〕12号 成文日期: 2024-01-18" [cn-ga31-early2024-26]; "加快突破GPU芯片、集群低时延互连网络、异构资源管理等技术,建设超大规模智算中心" [cn-ga31-early2024-26]; "要综合运用知识产权、财政税收、政府采购、设施开放等政策" [cn-ga31-early2024-34]; "要广泛开展人工智能国际合作,帮助全球南方国家加强技术能力建设" [cn-ga31-early2024-34]. Cuts: FOR on direction (a published national aim, in January 2024, to speed breakthroughs in GPU chips), with an AGAINST clause (international cooperation).
- **Mar 5, 2026 (Xinhua's account of Mar 12, 2026) · Xi Jinping, with the Jiangsu delegation to the National People's Congress** (a published account of his words). After a deputy from Tongfu Microelectronics (通富微电子) spoke, he said: "中国的科技发展要在国际上开展合作的同时，坚持独立自主、自立自强，更好承担起我们的历史责任" [cn-ga31-counter-10] (China's technological development must, while cooperating internationally, hold to independence, self-reliance and self-strengthening, and better shoulder our historical responsibility). No chip, firm or purchase is named. Cuts: CONTEXT (both halves of the line in one sentence: cooperation abroad and self-reliance).

### 2.2 Purchasing rules that bind state bodies

- Ministry of Finance and Ministry of Industry and Information Technology, notice on desktop computers, signed Dec 16, 2023: "乡镇以上党政机关,以及乡镇以上党委和政府直属事业单位及部门所属为机关提供支持保障的事业单位在采购台式计算机时,应当将CPU、操作系统符合安全可靠测评要求纳入采购需求" (Party and government organs at township level and above, the public institutions directly under Party committees and governments at those levels, and the departments' public institutions that provide support to those organs, shall when buying desktop computers include in the purchase requirement that the CPU and operating system meet the security-and-reliability evaluation) [cn-policy-desktop-2023]. The sentence ends "其他单位可不在采购需求中提出此项要求" (other units may leave this requirement out). The same clause, with the same ending, was published the same day (Dec 26, 2023; corrected Nov 23, 2026 from "issued that day": only the desktop notice's signing date was checked) for portable computers [cn-policy-laptop-procurement-2023] and for general-purpose servers [cn-policy-server-procurement-2023]. All three name the CPU and the operating system only; GPU and 人工智能 are not in any of the three pages (0 places; CPU is found in each). The server standard's annex, read since, words its row as key components «such as» the CPU and the operating system, has GPU 0 times, and has 人工智能 once, in an unstarred row on an optional AI computing unit of which no evaluation is asked (this section's entry on what «article 75» says). Four further standards of the same day, for workstations, all-in-one computers, operating systems and databases, end the same way (this section's row of Dec 16, 2023 on four further purchase standards).
- The evaluation list of Dec 26, 2023 names the processors that passed, in 18 numbered rows by a script's count of the saved table (some rows name more than one model), from HiSilicon, Loongson, the Wuxi institute (Sunway), Phytium, Hygon and Zhaoxin, the entities that submitted them (送测单位), each of which the trial guide then in force required to be registered in China [cn-ga31-evaluation-33]; the strings "Intel", "AMD" and "英特尔" are not in the copy (a provincial procurement site's PDF) [cn-policy-cpu-list-2023].
- **An evaluation list for AI chips now exists.** Announcement 2026 No. 2 of the same two evaluation centres, dated May 26, 2026, "附表一、人工智能训练推理芯片" (table 1, AI training and inference chips): Ascend 310 and 910 (HiSilicon), Zhenwu M530 and M890 (T-Head, "Alibaba’s chip design unit" in Alibaba's release of Sept 25, 2026, which calls the Zhenwu M890 the predecessor of T-Head's new chip [cn-ga31-twelve-35]), and one chip each from Biren, Hygon, Iluvatar CoreX, MetaX and Moore Threads; nine chips, seven makers, all registered in China, all level I, in that announcement [cn-policy-ai-chip-evaluation]. The centres' own pages are now saved: by the announcement of Sept 20, 2026, 11 AI chips from 8 makers have passed, all registered in China, among them "昇腾950 深圳市海思半导体有限公司 Ⅱ级" [cn-le14-itsec-2026-3]; applicants must be "中国境内注册的实体" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727], and applying is voluntary, "由企业自愿向中国信息安全测评中心、国家保密科技测评中心" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]. Two limits remain: applying is voluntary, and no purchase rule read ties AI-chip buying to this list: the three 2023 notices name the CPU and the operating system only.
- The 2014 integrated-circuit outline already had a purchasing clause: "国家扩大内需的各项惠民工程和财政资金支持的重大信息化项目的政府采购部分,应当采购基于安全可靠软硬件的产品。鼓励基础电信和互联网企业采购基于安全可靠软硬件的整机和系统。" (the government-procurement part of the state's public-benefit projects to expand domestic demand and of major informatization projects supported by fiscal funds shall buy products based on secure and reliable software and hardware; basic telecom and internet companies are encouraged to buy complete machines and systems based on secure and reliable software and hardware) [cn-policy-2014-outline]. This is the earliest published text found that reaches internet companies' purchases; for them the word is "encouraged".
- Ministry of Finance, measures on government procurement of imported products, Dec 27, 2007: "第四条 政府采购应当采购本国产品,确需采购进口产品的,实行审核管理。" (article 4: government procurement shall procure domestic products; where imported products are really needed, review applies) [cn-policy-import-procurement-119]. For bodies spending public money, an imported product has needed the finance department's approval since 2007, save in projects involving national security and secrets, which the measures exclude (article 27), and for additions of the items already bought of up to 10% of the original contract's amount (articles 16 and 17); nothing here governs a company spending its own money.
- Cybersecurity Review Measures (in force Feb 15, 2022), among the factors a review weighs: "产品和服务的安全性、开放性、透明性、来源的多样性,供应渠道的可靠性以及因为政治、外交、贸易等因素导致供应中断的风险" (the security, openness, transparency and diversity of sources of the products and services, the reliability of supply channels, and the risk of supply being cut off for political, diplomatic, trade or other reasons) [cn-policy-review-measures]. On paper a chip under another country's export controls carries exactly that risk. No review of Nvidia products under these Measures was found in the pages read.
- Government Procurement Law, article 10: "政府采购应当采购本国货物、工程和服务" (government procurement shall procure domestic goods, works and services), with the exception, among others, of goods that "在中国境内无法获取或者无法以合理的商业条件获取" (cannot be obtained in China or cannot be obtained on reasonable commercial terms) [cn-policy-procurement-law-article10].
- Cybersecurity Law (as amended Oct 28, 2025), article 37: "关键信息基础设施的运营者采购网络产品和服务,可能影响国家安全的,应当通过国家网信部门会同国务院有关部门组织的国家安全审查" (operators of critical information infrastructure that purchase network products and services shall, where the purchase may affect national security, pass a national security review organised by the national cyberspace authority with the relevant departments of the State Council) [cn-policy-cyber-law-amended]. This is the legal footing of the Micron decision below.
- State Council General Office, notice of Sept 28, 2025 on home-made products in government purchasing, where home-made and other products compete in one purchase: "对本国产品的报价给予20%的价格扣除" (the bid price of home-made products is given a 20% deduction in evaluation) [cn-policy-domestic-product-standard-2025]. "Home-made" is defined by place of production, not by the maker's ownership: "产品应当在中国境内生产,即在中华人民共和国关境内实现从原材料、组件到产品的属性改变" (the product shall be produced in China, that is, the change of properties from raw materials and components into the product takes place within the customs territory of the People's Republic of China). The required shares of the cost of components produced in China are to be set, product by product, within five years of the notice taking effect; until a product's share requirement is in force, a product that meets that production test counts as home-made (the notice's equal-treatment clause is in section 5 below).
- **2023 to 2026 · The evaluation centres' own pages (China Information Technology Security Evaluation Center and the national secrecy evaluation centre)** (documents; the project before held only a trade site's copy of the May 2026 list). The security-and-reliability evaluation covers CPUs, AI training and inference chips, operating systems and databases; applicants must be entities registered in China; the rating weighs whether design, development and production were done in China; and applying is voluntary, with firms and users free to use the results or not. Words: "安全可靠测评主要面向计算机终端和服务器搭载的中央处理器（包括含人工智能加速单元的中央处理器）、人工智能训练推理芯片、操作系统、数据库" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; "送测单位应为中国境内注册的实体" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727] (applicants shall be entities registered in China); "设计、开发、生产等关键环节在中国境内完成的情况" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; "由企业自愿向中国信息安全测评中心、国家保密科技测评中心（以下简称“测评机构”）申请产品检测，测评结果由企业和用户自主选择使用。" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727] (firms apply of their own will ... firms and users choose for themselves whether to use the results); the guide is "安全可靠测评工作指南（V4.0）" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]. Nine announcements, Dec 26, 2023 to Sept 20, 2026, are saved; by a script's count 11 AI chips from 8 makers have passed, all registered in China as the guide requires of an applicant; the announcement of Sept 20, 2026 adds "昇腾950 深圳市海思半导体有限公司 Ⅱ级" [cn-le14-itsec-2026-3] and "昆仑芯M100 昆仑芯（北京）科技股份有限公司 I级" [cn-le14-itsec-2026-3]. Table: tables/chip_evaluations.tsv. Cuts: FOR, with the limit that applying is voluntary.
- **Dec 16, 2023 · Finance and industry ministries, four further purchase standards** (documents: workstations, all-in-one computers, operating systems, databases). Each binds the same state bodies and ends the same way; the workstation standard lists AI acceleration as a feature with no evaluation requirement on it. Words: "在采购工作站时，应当将CPU、操作系统符合安全可靠测评要求纳入采购需求，其他单位可不在采购需求中提出此项要求" [cn-le14-mof-workstation-standard-2023]; "在采购一体式计算机时，应当将CPU、操作系统符合安全可靠测评要求纳入采购需求，其他单位可不在采购需求中提出此项要求" [cn-le14-mof-allinone-standard-2023]; "支持 NPU/GPU 等 AI 加速模块" [cn-le14-mof-workstation-standard-2023-pdf]. Cuts: CONTEXT (narrow by their own words).
- **Jun 22, 2026 · Ministry of Finance, Caiku [2026] No. 10** (a document). Government buyers may not buy products made by 46 named American firms, not counting US-funded firms in China; the attached list gives the firms' names only, in Chinese and English, and 英伟达, NVIDIA, Intel, AMD, Micron, 美光, Qualcomm and 芯片 are each found 0 times in it (the control «Lockheed» 7 times). Words: "采购人在政府采购活动中，不得采购46家美国企业（不包括在华美资企业）生产的产品。" [cn-le14-mof-us-firms-measures-20260622]; "财库〔2026〕10号" [cn-le14-mof-us-firms-measures-20260622]; "Lockheed Martin" [cn-le14-mof-us-firms-list-20260622]. When Beijing published a by-name bar on American products for state buyers, none of the chip makers searched for above was on it. Cuts: CONTEXT (supports «guidance, not a published ban»).
- **Nov 7, 2024 and Jul 31, 2026 · Tenders of universities and of a state-funded firm that name home-made AI hardware** (documents: purchase notices). Fudan University sought equipment for a computing cluster, economy and high-performance home-made AI servers among it with storage, network and security equipment, under a ceiling of 54.73 million yuan for the whole; a state-funded firm in Qinghai sought one AI server to build a home-made computing node. Words: "经济型国产AI计算服务器（整机AI算力FP16≥2.2PFlops；FP32≥0.60PFlops）、高性能国产AI计算服务器" [cn-le17-ccgp-fudan-domestic-202411]; "最高限价（如有）：5473.000000 万元（人民币）" [cn-le17-ccgp-fudan-domestic-202411]; Tsinghua's "清华大学国产高性能 AI 服务器购置项目" [cn-o35-tsinghua-atlas-202409]; Sun Yat-sen's "中山大学国产智能计算系统采购项目" [cn-o35-ccgp-sysu-202412]. No tender found refuses Nvidia by name (see section 8). Cuts: FOR.
- **Nov 23 and 24, 2026 · Five buyers' notices on the national procurement site that name or require home-made computing** (documents: four notices in the site's central section, one in its local section; rows 45 to 49 of tables/procurement_records.tsv). The drug regulator's medical-device evaluation centre rented an eight-card home-made AI computing environment for a year and asked for its existing applications to be moved to a home-made AI computing architecture, the original not named (result, Nov 24); Peking University sought eight inference cards, imports not accepted, for work on home-made AI computing (tender, Nov 23); Central China Normal University bought one Huawei Atlas 800I A3 (award, Nov 24); the Institute of Modern Physics of the Chinese Academy of Sciences bought one DCU server, the DCU's maker not written (award, Nov 24); the First Affiliated Hospital of Guilin Medical University sought a platform with one home-made GPU machine (tender, Nov 24). Words: "完成现有应用从原人工智能计算架构向国产人工智能计算架构的适配迁移" [cn-ga13-ccgp-nmpa-cmde-ai-rental-27742397]; "基于国产AI算力开展模型推理验证和算法开发" [cn-ga13-ccgp-pku-inference-card-27734000]; "华为Atlas 800I A3" [cn-ga13-ccgp-ccnu-training-27739829]; "面向等离子体模拟计算DCU服务器" [cn-ga13-ccgp-cas-imp-dcu-27741809]; "国产GPU算力机1台" [cn-ga13-ccgp-df-guilin-med-ai-27746099]. Each is a notice of a tender or an award for one machine, a few cards or a year's rental, not a delivery; none names the maker of a chip (Huawei is named as the brand of a machine). Cuts: FOR.
- **Sept 27, 2024 and 2025 · «No imports» in two universities' notices sits beside Nvidia cards** (documents). The University of the Chinese Academy of Sciences sought to rent servers carrying eight Nvidia A800 or A100 cards under a notice that accepts no imported products or services; the Southern University of Science and Technology awarded the purchase of a server, its listed origin China, with eight Nvidia L40 cards under the line «refuse imports». Words: "本项目不接受进口" [cn-o35-ccgp-ucas-a100-202409]; "是否接受进口 拒绝进口" [cn-o35-sustech-00064]. So the procurement law's preference for domestic goods (section 2.2, the Government Procurement Law) sits, in these two notices, beside servers that hold Nvidia chips, the one bought with its origin listed as China (the project's reading of the two notices). Cuts: AGAINST a reading that state purchase rules shut out Nvidia chips.
- **Jun 19, 2025 · Fudan University, purchase intention for a home-made large-model training and inference server** (a document: the university's procurement centre, the page address dated Jun 19, 2025). One home-made large-model machine on an independently controllable hardware architecture, at least 3 PFlops a node; an intention, which the notice says is a preliminary plan. Words: "大模型国产训练推理服务器" [cn-ga31-early2024-43]; "拟采购拟采购一套国产高性能大模型一体机,该设备基于自主可控的硬件架构与国产化算法框架设计" [cn-ga31-early2024-43] (the page repeats 拟采购). Cuts: FOR.
- **Jun 30, 2026 · The evaluation centres' guide (V4.0), in its own words: who may apply, what is weighed, what stays unpublished** (a document; the China Information Technology Security Evaluation Center's own page, "2026-06-30 来源： 中国信息安全测评中心" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; the words below stand in the saved page's body, not all in its text extract). Only an entity registered in China may apply, and it must meet eight conditions, among them that the product is on open sale or in use and that the applicant holds the product's full development documents, design materials, code and development environment; an applicant may submit at most two products of each kind in one intake period, and the kinds are CPUs (CPUs with AI acceleration units included), AI training and inference chips, operating systems, databases and printer controller chips. GPUs are not named as a kind; a GPU can pass as an AI training and inference chip (Biren's GPGPU passed in that kind: section 3.4, the row on two makers on the evaluation lists). The evaluation includes code review and a review of the development environment; a pass is valid for three years; a change in the applicant's actual controller or controlling stake while the pass is valid must be reported; and the applicant must keep unpublished matters of the evaluation confidential. The nine saved announcements of results list products with their applicants and grades, not who else applied or who failed. The guide's words 外资, 外商, 境外 and 国外 occur 0 times each (control: 中国境内 2), so it neither names foreign makers nor sets a minimum Chinese stake. Words: "送测单位应为中国境内注册的实体，且满足以下条件" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; "送测产品应面向市场公开销售或已投入实际使用" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; "具有送测产品完备的研发文档、设计资料、代码数据和研发环境" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; "同一送测单位一个受理期内最多送测中央处理器、操作系统、数据库、人工智能训练推理芯片、打印机主控芯片产品各两款" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; "测评主要包括材料核验、人员访谈、代码审查、环境审查、现场测试、现场考核、样品测试等环节" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; "测评结果自发布之日起有效期3年" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; "若送测单位出现实控人或控股权发生变化" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; "送测单位应对测评工作涉及的未公开事项承担保密义务，不公开宣传、报道，不向第三方泄露" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]. The centre's application form offers the AI kind for desktops and servers: "人工智能训练推理芯片（□桌面 □服务器）" [cn-ga31-evaluation-30]. What a pass is for: the guide leaves its use to firms and users (section 2.2, the row on the centres' own pages); the state's purchase standards of 2023 require it for the CPU and operating system only. Cuts: CONTEXT (every maker on the lists is registered in China because no other may apply; whether a foreign maker's Chinese-registered unit ever applied cannot be known from the published pages).
- **Jul 2023 to May 2026 · When AI chips became a kind of the evaluation** (documents: the guide's earlier versions as other sites reproduce them, the centres' own pages for the lists). The trial guide, released on Jul 28, 2023 by the centre's account as a software-licensing service centre in Chongqing reproduces it (page of Jul 31, 2023), covers CPUs, operating systems and databases; the third version, as a security news site reproduces it (Jul 8, 2025), adds AI training and inference chips and printer controller chips; the fourth, of Jun 30, 2026, on the centre's own page, keeps them. The second version (reproduced Dec 2, 2024) let a pending application count as the product's intellectual property; the fourth asks for a right already held (a granted patent, a registered trademark, a copyright, a layout design or the like), not one applied for. The lists name the guide they apply: the first, of Dec 26, 2023, the trial guide; that of May 26, 2026 the third version. By a script's count of the nine saved lists (tables/chip_evaluations.tsv), the first table of AI chips is that of May 26, 2026 (nine chips), and the second that of Sept 20, 2026 (two). Words: "2023年7月28日，中国信息安全测评中心发布《安全可靠测评工作指南（试行）》" [cn-ga31-evaluation-33]; "中央处理器（CPU）、操作系统以及数据库等基础软硬件产品" [cn-ga31-evaluation-33]; "人工智能训练推理芯片、操作系统、数据库，以及激光或喷墨打印机搭载的主控芯片" [cn-ga31-evaluation-36]; "集成电路布图设计等知识产权（含申请）之一" [cn-ga31-evaluation-44]; "拥有送测产品相关的授权专利、注册商标、著作权、集成电路布图设计等知识产权之一" [cn-le14-itsec-scope-20230727]; "根据《安全可靠测评工作指南（试行）》要求" [cn-le14-itsec-2023-1]; "根据《安全可靠测评工作指南 V3.0》要求" [cn-le14-itsec-2026-2]; "附表一、人工智能训练推理芯片" [cn-le14-itsec-2026-2]; "附表二、人工智能训练推理芯片" [cn-le14-itsec-2026-3]. The older versions are held only as other sites reproduce them; the centres' own pages for them were not saved. Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Jun 18, Sept 9 and Nov 16, 2026 · Three buyers write the AI-chip evaluation into their purchase terms** (documents: two on the buyers' own sites, one as a payments news site reproduces it). China Post's Shandong branch, in a draft put out for comment on Jun 18, 2026, asks for two AI servers whose CPUs have passed the evaluation, whose accelerator cards or modules must pass too if the centre publishes results for such cards or modules between the tender notice and the bid deadline, and which carry at least eight home-made AI chips of a self-developed architecture. Shanghai Pudong Development Bank, calling for suppliers of four pilot AI servers on Sept 9 to 11, 2026 "to verify the pilot use of home-made AI computing", asks that the GPU chip be in the evaluation's AI training and inference category, with a screenshot of the centre's list as proof (the bank's own portal gave no readable text; the call is held as 移动支付网 reproduces it). A unit of China Southern Power Grid (the tenderer is 南方电网数字电网研究院股份有限公司), in a tender published Nov 16, 2026 for its AI subsidiary's computing clusters, asks that the AI card modules in lot 1 (64.33 million yuan) meet the centre's guide, while the same notice accepts imported key products through their makers' offices in China. Words: "CPU须通过中国信息安全测评中心的安全可靠测评" [cn-ga31-evaluation-35]; "则投标服务器搭载的智能加速卡或模组，须通过安全可靠测评" [cn-ga31-evaluation-35]; "配置不低于8颗国产自研架构AI芯片" [cn-ga31-evaluation-35]; "2026-06-18 来源： 采购平台" [cn-ga31-evaluation-35]; "验证国产人工智能算力试点应用情况" [cn-ga31-evaluation-25]; "投标人所投产品的GPU芯片部件应当符合中国信息安全测评中心发布的安全可靠测评结果中“人工智能训练推理芯片”品类要求" [cn-ga31-evaluation-25]; "证明材料需附安全可靠测评结果公告网站清单页截图" [cn-ga31-evaluation-25]; "征集时间 2026年9月9日－2026年9月11日" [cn-ga31-evaluation-25]; "投标人所投 AI芯片平台 的AI算力卡模块需满足中国信息安全测评中心发布的《安全可靠测评工作指南》的要求" [cn-ga31-evaluation-22]; "标包1：服务器及存储关键零部件 6432.88" [cn-ga31-evaluation-22]; "发布时间： 2026-11-16" [cn-ga31-evaluation-22]; "凡属进口产品，接受产品制造商在国内注册成立的最高办事机构或授权代理商/经销商参与采购活动" [cn-ga31-evaluation-22]. Each is one buyer's notice (two of them small), not a rule; no purchase rule found ties AI-chip buying to the list. Cuts: FOR (once the AI-chip list existed, some buyers made the evaluation a purchase condition, one that only chips of makers registered in China can meet).
- **Mar 11, 2024 · Central government buyers' configuration standards, as Guancha reports them (a press account of a published notice; the notice and its annexes are not saved)** (Guancha, by 吕栋, Mar 26, 2024, carried by Phoenix). The notice 国机采〔2024〕7号, which the central government procurement site published, requires the evaluation for the CPU and operating system in desktop configurations 1 to 6 and laptop configurations 1 to 5, while desktop configuration 7 and laptop configuration 6 specify Intel or AMD processors and a Windows 10 edition, with no evaluation requirement in their key-component security part, buyers choosing them being held to the relevant management rules. The same article reads the guide as leaving foreign firms' products a chance to be listed if they apply and meet its conditions; the guide's own words require the applicant to be an entity registered in China (row above). Words: "国机采〔2024〕7号" [cn-ga31-evaluation-37]; "台式计算机配置7和便携式计算机配置6的CPU品牌为Intel/AMD" [cn-ga31-evaluation-37]; "这一配置在“关键部件安全”部分没有要求满足安全可靠测评，但采购单位选择此项配置应符合相关管理要求" [cn-ga31-evaluation-37]; "英特尔、AMD和微软等外国公司的产品，只要自愿申请安全可靠测评并符合相关测评条件，同样拥有入围安全可靠测评结果的机会" [cn-ga31-evaluation-37]. Cuts: AGAINST, weakly (in 2024 central buyers kept one Intel or AMD option outside the evaluation, under rules not published here); the claim about foreign firms is the outlet's reading.
- **Oct 27, 2026 · Dalian University of Technology: an award for a computer with an RTX PRO 6000D card** (a document: the university's bidding-result page). Project DUTJHT-2610136, a platform for AI-model-assisted software design, awarded at 87,215 yuan, with "RTX PRO 6000D 84G" [cn-ga31-twelve-22] among the technical specifications, the reference brand and model not fixed; "成交金额 ￥87,215.00" [cn-ga31-twelve-22]. - **Apr 2025 to Nov 2026 · Notices of firms, universities, a hospital and a research institute that require or name home-made AI chips** (documents: buyers' sites; for the Zhengzhou contract the Henan government-procurement site; for the Guangzhou notice, which a purchasing agency issued for the buyer, a purchasing platform; one central notice and the bank's notice in reprints; table tables/procurement_notices_sweep.tsv). The Agricultural Development Bank of China's one-year framework for domestic GPU servers, in three lots, "第一包 昇腾GPU服务器 昇腾910B推理服务器 154.5万元/台" [cn-ga31-h20notices-22] (a ceiling price for bids, per server, tax included), with Cambricon 590 and 370 and Hygon K100-AI lots (deadlines May 2025). China Export & Credit Insurance (tender May 27, award Jun 23, 2026): "采购使用海光芯片架构的GPU服务器10台" [cn-ga31-tenders-14], "中标金额：7,435,600.00元人民币" [cn-ga31-tenders-38]. Anhui's provincial rural credit union (award, May 7, 2026): "01包：昇腾芯片服务器12台；02包：昇腾、海光或寒武纪服务器4台" [cn-ga31-tenders-3]. China Tower (Aug 3, 2026), for a project of model adaptation to home-made computing platforms: "1张海光K100AI加速卡,1张百度昆仑芯RG800加速卡" [cn-ga31-tenders-17], beside three GPU workstations whose cards are not named. Guangzhou's transport research institute (Nov 16, 2026), two servers with "国产AI算力卡，单卡64G显存" [cn-ga31-tenders-50] and home-made ARM processors, ceiling 1.64 million yuan. Awards and a contract notice that name the maker, the chip or the server model: Zhengzhou University of Aeronautics, contract of Mar 2, 2026, "通信控制器 寒武纪 国产GPU卡 16 49,000.00" [cn-ga31-tenders-22]; the Second Affiliated Hospital of Xi'an Jiaotong University (central procurement, in a reprint), three servers each "配置8颗昇腾AI处理器" [cn-ga31-tenders-30]; the University of Science and Technology Liaoning, Dec 3, 2025, two Huawei inference servers, "规格型号：Atlas 800I A2" [cn-ga31-tenders-36]. The bank, the insurer, the credit union and China Tower are firms, not government bodies (the notices do not give their ownership); their notices show choices, not a rule. Cuts: FOR.
- **Nov 12 and 20, 2024; Mar and Apr 24, 2025 · Two universities' «home-made only» or «no imports» purchases end in Nvidia cards, the H20 among them** (documents: the national procurement site's award, the Central Government Procurement Center's tender document as a bidding site keeps it, Fudan University's own site; found by a search of Nov 25, 2026, read whole). The University of the Chinese Academy of Sciences, through the Central Government Procurement Center, issued a tender in March 2025 (GC-HGX250062, ceiling 5.7 million yuan) that allowed no imported products, listed its four GPU servers as home-made in the origin column, and asked for eight GPUs a server with at least 96GB of HBM3 memory, NVLink and MIG, without naming a maker; the award of Apr 24, 2025 (5.65 million yuan in all, with storage, two switches and software) includes four Dell PowerEdge XE9680 servers at 1,195,000 yuan each, each with one NVIDIA HGX H20 eight-GPU module. Fudan University's notice of Nov 12, 2024 (ceiling 450,000 yuan) accepted home-made products only, for three cards that had to work with its existing Nvidia A800 cards and CUDA; the award of Nov 20, 2024 is for three Nvidia A800 80G SXM cards at 140,000 yuan each. Words: "是否允许进口产品 9 否" [cn-ga31-h20notices-19] (row 9 of the bidders' table: imported products allowed? no); "GPU 服务器 是 台 4 国产" [cn-ga31-h20notices-19] (GPU servers, core product, 4, origin home-made); "NVLink 总线带宽≥900GB/s" [cn-ga31-h20notices-19]; "1*NVIDIA HGX H20 8-GPU SXM 96GB 500W GPUs模组" [cn-ga31-h20notices-7]; "GPU 服务器 DELL PowerEdge XE9680 DELL PowerEdge XE9680 4 1195000" [cn-ga31-h20notices-7]; "中标（成交）金额： 人民币565.000000 万元" [cn-ga31-h20notices-7]; "2025年04月24日 17:25" [cn-ga31-h20notices-7]; "本项目仅接受国产产品响应，不接受进口产品响应。" [cn-ga31-h20notices-11]; "需与原有服务器的NVIDIA A800显示卡和驱动兼容，与服务器原有CUDA平台兼容" [cn-ga31-h20notices-11]; "高性能图形显示卡 Nvidia A800 80G SXM 140000.00 3 台 420000.00" [cn-ga31-h20notices-4]. The UCAS award was the only award notice held that named the H20 as a word when this search ran (a second award of the same university, published Sept 28, 2025, with six H20 servers beside four Ascend servers, and three more university notices that name the H20 were saved later the same day: section 5): a whole-word script search of the 500 procurement pages saved by then, run on Nov 25, 2026, found it in this award and in one other page, a company's exchange filing (section 3.4); a further search of the same day saved two notices of autumn 2026 that ask for the H20 by name, a university's purchase intention and a leasing company's tender, neither of them an award (section 5); control: the computing-centre page figure 20 uses, cn-tolerate-bit, is found. A listed company's own tender of July 2026 also names it (CFMOTO, section 3.4): "招标范围：①H20 GPU 算力服务器" [cn-ga31-rtxlater-58]. Neither award says where the servers or cards were made; an award is a contract decision, not a delivery. The «home-made» and «no imports» lines are the buyers' words for the products bid, and the products awarded carry Nvidia chips (a reading of the four pages). Cuts: AGAINST a reading that state purchase rules shut out Nvidia chips, the H20 included; fits limit 3 (some purchases made).
- **Apr 2025 · A city government's tender requires home-made chips for the cloud platform of its smart-city project** (document: the tender document of 洛阳市行政审批和政务信息管理局, cover dated April 2025, filing number 洛采公开-2025-55, as the Henan government-procurement site holds it). For phase one (first batch) of Luoyang's smart-city project: "预算金额：122362122.23 元" [cn-ga31-noticecopies-7]; "资金来源：财政资金" [cn-ga31-noticecopies-7]; "是否接受进口产品：否" [cn-ga31-noticecopies-7]. The cloud platform that carries the project must be "满足芯片、服务器、数据库、操作系统、云平台软硬件均为国产自主创新产品，兼容鲲鹏、昇腾、海光、飞腾多种国产化芯片" [cn-ga31-noticecopies-7] (chips, servers, database, operating system and the cloud platform's software and hardware all home-made products of independent innovation, compatible with several kinds of home-made chips, Kunpeng, Ascend, Hygon and Phytium), and "要求云主机、容器服务支持主流国产 AI 智能芯片" [cn-ga31-noticecopies-7] (cloud hosts and container services must support mainstream home-made AI chips). It names home-made chip families and no foreign maker, and it refuses none by name. It is a requirement of one city's tender, dated before the reported rule of November 2025 on state-funded data centres; the chip clause itself cites no rule (the same section cites the security-evaluation lists for the cloud's software and Henan's guide for government clouds). Cuts: FOR (a government body, spending fiscal money, requires home-made chips at project level; one tender, one city).
- **Aug to Sept 2025 · China Pharmaceutical University's computing platform, phase one: a tender that takes no imports, won by a Jiangsu company that bears China Unicom's name with a GPU server whose chip the award does not print** (documents: the tender document on the university's purchasing site, cover dated August 2025; the award as a tender site carries it, listed there on Sept 8, 2025). The award: "供应商名称：联通（江苏）产业互联网有限公司" [cn-ga31-noticecopies-12], with a GPU server listed first of four goods, whose brands and models in that order are "畅快算；橙芯数链；火山引擎；科士达" [cn-ga31-noticecopies-12] and "6810 X2；定制开发；定制开发；FS040FAACEOBE" [cn-ga31-noticecopies-12], at "1300000元；330000元；200000元；135000元" [cn-ga31-noticecopies-12] (so the GPU server is of the brand 畅快算, model 6810 X2, at 1.3 million yuan); no chip is printed.
