- **Dec 16, 2023 · What «article 75» of the server standard says: key components such as the CPU and the operating system shall meet the security-and-reliability evaluation; it names no country of origin, no maker, no 国产 and no 自主可控, and nothing on AI chips** (a document: the annex to the finance and industry ministries' notice on general servers, a PDF on the Ministry of Finance's site, linked from the notice; the copy linked from the ministry's Treasury Department page has the same text from its first line to its last, compared by script [cn-ga31-evaluation-6]). The notice gives its own number, signature and posting day: "财库〔 2023 〕33号" [cn-policy-server-procurement-2023]; "财 政 部 工业和信息化部 2023 年 12 月16 日" [cn-policy-server-procurement-2023]; "发布日期:2023年12月26日" [cn-policy-server-procurement-2023]; the Treasury Department's page of the same notice, "发布日期:2023年12月28日" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-17]. The annex is headed "附件 通用服务器政府采购需求标准（2023 版）" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7] and prints no number, date or issuer of its own. It is one table of numbered rows (序号), not of articles: a tender document that cites its «第75条» (China Telecom's prequalification notice of Jul 5, 2024 does so for the CPU: section 3.6, [cn-ga31-operators-83]) means row 75. Row 75 (class 安全要求, indicator *关键部件安全要求, starred, not to be used as a scoring factor) reads, in its requirement cell, CPU 和操作系统等关键部件应当符合安全可靠测评要求 (key components such as the CPU and the operating system shall meet the security-and-reliability evaluation); the saved text breaks the cell over two lines, so it is checked in two parts: "CPU 和操作系统等关键部件应当符" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7] and "合安全可靠测评要求" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7]. Its use column tells the buyer where to look the results up: on the sites of the China Information Technology Security Evaluation Center and the national secrecy evaluation centre, named by government departments (broken over seven short lines in the server annex; the same cell stands less broken in the desktop annex: "通过政府有关部门指定的" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-9], "站查看安全可靠测评结果" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-9]). The row carries footnote 3, which says who must ask for it: "3)乡镇以上党政机关，以及党委和政府直属事业单位及部门所属为机关提供支持保障的事业单位在采购相关产品时，应当提出此项要求。" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7] (Party and government organs at township level and above, the public institutions directly under Party committees and governments and the departments' public institutions that provide support to the organs shall make this requirement when they buy the relevant products). What the star and the scoring column mean, from the annex's second page: "1)加“*”指标为必须纳入采购需求的指标，未加“*”的指标由采购人根据实际需要自行确定是否包含在采购需求中。" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7] (a starred indicator must go into the purchase requirement; an unstarred one the buyer includes or leaves out according to actual needs); "2)“是否可以作为评分因素”为“是”的指标，采购人可以根据实际需要在采购文件中设为评分项；为“否”的，不能设为评分项。" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7]. The row before it, 74, unstarred: "支持多集群作业管理功能" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7] (support for managing jobs across clusters). The row after it, 76, starred: "支持故障检测功能，可以检测到具" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7] (fault detection for parts such as memory and disks; the cell runs on). What the annex does not hold, by a script's count over its whole text: 国产 0, 自主可控 0, 本国 0, 进口 0, GPU 0, NPU 0, 智算 0, 智能计算 0, 英伟达 0, NVIDIA 0, and 华为, 海光, 飞腾, 龙芯 and 鲲鹏 each 0 (control: CPU 28 times, PCIe 18, 安全可靠测评 2). The word 等 (such as) leaves the list of key components open; the row names two. AI hardware is specified in row 43 alone (row 44, unstarred, names an AI computing unit only as the condition for offering a training-script migration tool), unstarred and with no evaluation asked of it: "若配备 AI 计算单元应符合如下要" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7], "a) 具备人工智能加速处理器，计算" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7] (if an AI computing unit is fitted, it shall have an AI accelerator processor, and so on); row 50, unstarred, lists an accelerator card among cards a buyer may ask for, "卡、显示功能卡、运算加速功能" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-7]. The six sibling standards of the same day carry the same row under other numbers, read in the saved annexes: desktop computers row 183 "CPU 和操作系统等关键部件应当符合" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-9], portable computers row 189 [cn-ga31-serverstd75-10], all-in-one computers row 179 [cn-ga31-serverstd75-11], workstations row 182 [cn-ga31-serverstd75-12]; operating systems rows 160 (desktop) and 165 (server), "操作系统应当符合安全可靠测评要求" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-13]; databases rows 105 (distributed) and 100 (centralized), "数据库应当符合安全可靠测评要求" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-15]. No explanation by either ministry of this row was found (a search of Nov 25, 2026 for the standard's title with 答记者问, 政策解读 and 起草说明 on the two ministries' sites; the same search returns the notice itself, the control). Cuts: CONTEXT (what a tender means by «article 75»: an evaluation condition on the processor and the operating system, which the notice makes binding on Party and government organs from the township level up, on the public institutions directly under Party committees and governments at those levels and on the departments' public institutions that provide support to the organs, and which other units may leave out, in the notice's words (a notice on government purchasing); the limit by origin lies in the evaluation, for which only entities registered in China may apply, as the rows on the evaluation centres' pages say; row 75 of the 2023 standard itself names no AI chip (row 43, which speaks of AI accelerators, asks no evaluation of them), so a buyer who asks the evaluation of an AI chip names a component that the row leaves unnamed).
- **Aug 31 to Sept 21, 2026 · A company under the central bank writes the AI chip into its own tender's version of the server standard's row; a bank's second call asks for home-made cards that are on the evaluation list** (documents: the bid document of 中国金融电子化集团有限公司, a PDF on the financial sector's central purchasing site; the bank's second call as the tender site 千里马招标网 carries it, with the bank's name masked in the notice's body and printed in the page's title). The company (a financial-computerization group; the page gives its name in Chinese only): "招标人:中国金融电子化集团有限公司" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25]; "项目编号:JSTCC26025206627" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25]; cover "二〇二六 年 九 月" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25]; documents on offer "获取时间:2026 年 08 月 31 日起至 2026 年 09 月 07 日止" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25], bids due Sept 21, 2026. It tenders two lots of two intelligent-computing servers each for a large-model platform of its own, "资金来源:自筹资金" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25] (funds it raises itself), "包 1(类型一智算服务器采购)人民币 6,100,000 元,包 2(类型二智算服务器采购)人 民币 6,600,000 元" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25]; the purpose it gives is "补齐集团国产化智算基础设施底座" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25] (to fill in the group's home-made intelligent-computing base), with two kinds of server side by side "为规避单一技术路线依赖风险,项目配置两类差异化技术路 线智算服务器并行部署" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25] (to avoid depending on a single technical route); its purchase table marks both lots as closed to imported products. Its requirement table has the 2023 server standard's row titles round this point (多集群作业管理 at row 80, *关键部件安全要求 at row 81, *故障检测 at row 82), and where the standard's row 75 names the CPU and the operating system, row 81 here, starred for both lots, adds the AI chip: CPU、人工智能训练推理芯片和操作系统应当符合安全可靠测评要求 (the CPU, the AI training and inference chip and the operating system shall meet the security-and-reliability evaluation). The saved text breaks the cell over lines, so it is checked in two overlapping parts: "CPU、人工智能训练推理芯片和操作系统应当" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25], "统应当符合安全可靠测评要求" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25]. The proof asked is a screenshot of the evaluation centre's results page, and a bid scores for the chip maker's authorization: "所投产品关键芯片(人工智能训练推理芯片)的生产厂商对本项目的芯 片授权书原件扫描件" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25]. The document names no chip, card or maker (GPU 0, 英伟达 0, NVIDIA 0, 昇腾 0, 海光 0 in the saved text, a script's count; control: CPU 48, 国产 49). It describes itself: "本项目是央行直属企业践行服务履职使命、响应金融行业智能化趋势的关键举措" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-25] (this project is a key measure by which an enterprise directly under the central bank carries out its mission of service and duty and answers the financial sector's trend towards intelligent technology; the sentence runs on). Shanghai Pudong Development Bank's call for suppliers of four pilot AI servers (this section, the entry on three buyers: first put out for Sept 9 to 11, 2026) was put out a second time: "上海浦东发展银行总行2026年人工智能算力服务器扩容建设项目之试点算力服务器采购供应商征集公告(第二次)" [cn-ga31-inti-spdb-second-call-qianlima], "征集时间 2026年9月16日-2026年9月18日" [cn-ga31-inti-spdb-second-call-qianlima], signed "二〇二六年九月十五日" [cn-ga31-inti-spdb-second-call-qianlima]. This copy prints the hardware table that the copy of the first call does not: "配置国产化GPU 加速卡>=8张" [cn-ga31-inti-spdb-second-call-qianlima] (at least eight home-made GPU accelerator cards a server) and "GPU芯片需在安全可靠测评结果公告人工智能训练推理芯片目录中。" [cn-ga31-inti-spdb-second-call-qianlima] (the GPU chip must be on the AI training and inference list of the evaluation results). Why the call was repeated the page does not say. Each is one buyer's document, not a rule: the 2023 standard's row names no AI chip (the entry on what «article 75» says). Cuts: FOR (two buyers of the financial sector, a company and a bank, make a pass in the AI-chip evaluation a condition of their own purchases; only chips of makers registered in China can hold one, by the evaluation guide's words in the rows above).
- **Nov 2025 to Nov 2026 · Buyers the notice does not name use the server standard as they choose: two universities in Henan and a Beijing hospital except the evaluation; a Beijing university keeps it and names the CPU** (documents: two purchase documents on the Henan government-procurement site, an award on the national procurement site, an award on the university's own purchasing site). Henan Normal University: "采 购 人:河南师范大学" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-37]; "1.项目编号:豫财招标采购-2025-1397" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-37]; documents on offer "1.时间: 2025 年 11 月 10 日至 2025 年 11 月 18 日" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-37]; "4.预算金额:9310300.00 元" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-37]; "8.是否接受进口产品:否" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-37] (imported products not accepted). Its second lot is "包 2:AI 计算服务器 4 台" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-37] with other items, each server with "★6.GPU 显卡:配置 8 张≥48GB 全高" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-37] (eight GPU cards of at least 48 GB), and the rest of the configuration held to the standard's substantive minimum with the evaluation excepted: "性指标的最低要求(安全可靠测评除" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-37] (the cell is broken over lines; the exception stands three times in the document, a script's count). Yellow River Conservancy Technical University: "采 购 人:黄河水利职业技术大学" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-18]; "项目编号:豫财磋商采购-2025-1261" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-18]; documents on offer "1.时间:2025 年 12 月 10 日 至 2025 年 12 月 17 日" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-18]; "8、是否接受进口产品:否" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-18]. It asks for one server with "3、配置 8 张高性能 GPU 加速卡,单卡搭载 ≥ 141GB 高速显存" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-18] (eight GPU accelerator cards, each with at least 141 GB of memory) and writes: "*13.供应商提供的货物配置不能低于(财库 〔2023〕33 号)发布的《通用服务器政府采购 需求标准(2023 版)》实质性指标的最低要求 (安全可靠测评除外)。" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-18] (the goods may not fall below the minimum of the substantive indicators of the 2023 server standard, the security-and-reliability evaluation excepted). Neither document names a maker of the cards (英伟达 0 and NVIDIA 0 in each, a script's count; control: GPU 9 and 4 times). Peking Union Medical College Hospital's award of Apr 3, 2026 writes the same exception for each of its three server items, one of which carries eight NVIDIA L20 cards (section 5, the entry of that date). The University of the Chinese Academy of Social Sciences keeps the evaluation and names the processor as the key component: its award, "发布时间:2026-11-13" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-19], "1.项目编号: GC-HGX261204" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-19], buyer "名 称:中国社会科学院大学(中国社会科学院研究生院)" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-19], is for three GPU servers, "1 GPU计算服务器 新华三 H3C UniServer R5500 3 1565000" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-19] (three at 1,565,000 yuan each), of which it says "(13)符合财政部、工业和信息化部印发的《通用服务器政府采购需求标准(2023)年版)》中所有*指标要求,产品关键部件(CPU)通过政府有关部门指定的中国信息安全测评中心和国家保密科技测评中心网站近三年发布的安全可靠测评结果。" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-19] (they meet every starred indicator of the standard, and the key component, the CPU, is in the evaluation results of the last three years); the eight accelerator cards of each server are set by figures, with no maker and no evaluation asked of them, "(6)配置8块OAM形态GPGPU加速卡" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-19]. The notice allows all of this: buyers other than those it names (Party and government organs from the township level up, the public institutions directly under Party committees and governments at those levels and the departments' public institutions that provide support to the organs) may leave the requirement out (this section's first row). Cuts: CONTEXT, and AGAINST reading the standard as a general bar: outside the bodies the notice names the evaluation is the buyer's choice; three buyers of AI servers drop it in terms, two of them in purchases closed to imported products, and one keeps it for the CPU while leaving the accelerator free.
- **Jul 1, 2025, as a listed company's filed record dates it · The day the evaluation guide's third version took in AI chips** (a filing: the investor-relations record of Ninestar, "证券代码:SZ:002180 证券简称:纳思达" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-34], "活动时间 2025 年 10 月 30 日" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-34], on the disclosure site cninfo.com.cn). The record above on when AI chips became a kind of the evaluation holds the third version only as a security news site reproduced it on Jul 8, 2025. The company gives the days: "2025 年 7 月 1 日，中国信息安全测评中心更新了《安全可靠测评工作指南（V3.0）》" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-34]; "相较于 2024 年 11 月 26 日发布的 2.0 版本" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-34]; "本次更新将激光或喷墨打印机搭载的主控芯片及人工智能训练推理芯片纳入到产品测评品类" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-34] (on Jul 1, 2025 the centre updated the guide to V3.0; against the 2.0 version issued on Nov 26, 2024, the update brought the main controller chips of laser or inkjet printers and AI training and inference chips into the kinds evaluated). It expects the same course for printers and GPUs: "预计打印机和 GPU 也将遵循类似的测评机制和推进流程" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-34] (printers and GPUs are expected to follow a like evaluation mechanism and course): a company's expectation, not a rule. The dates are the company's account; the centre's own page for the third version is not held. Cuts: CONTEXT (a date for the step, by a company's account: Jul 1, 2025).
- **Dec 16, 2023 to Mar 4, 2024 · How the server standard is to be applied: buyers may add indicators of their own, a supplier's undertaking counts at the bid stage, and a Shanghai district tells all its units to ask for the evaluation** (documents: the ministries' notice on general servers, already cited in this section; a work note of a district finance bureau on the district government's site; an explanation credited to a city's procurement centre on that city's government-services site). The notice, paragraph 3, after the sentence on who must ask for the evaluation: "采购人在采购需求中,可以对《需求标准》中的指标提出更高要求,也可以根据实际需要增加《需求标准》以外的指标,但不得超出实际需要。" [cn-policy-server-procurement-2023] (a buyer may set higher requirements than the standard's and may add indicators the standard lacks, though not beyond actual need): the ground on which a buyer can ask the evaluation of an AI chip, which the standard's row does not name. Paragraph 4: "四、供应商在投标、响应环节出具关于所提供通用服务器满足采购文件要求承诺函的，即视为相关产品符合要求。" [cn-policy-server-procurement-2023] (where a supplier at the bid or response stage gives a letter undertaking that the servers offered meet the purchase document's requirements, the products are taken as meeting them). Huangshi's explanation, "来源:采购中心 时间:2024-01-11" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-33], puts it so: "采购人在供应商投标、响应环节不得要求对7个类目软硬件产品进行检测、认证，也不得要求供应商提供检测报告、认证报告。" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-33] (at the bid or response stage buyers may not require the seven kinds of software and hardware products to be tested or certified, nor require suppliers to provide test or certification reports). The finance bureau of Shanghai's Putuo district, in a note addressed "各委、办、局、街道镇:" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-8] (to every commission, office, bureau, subdistrict and town of the district), signed "上海市普陀区财政局 2024年2月28日" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-8] and posted "2024年03月04日 来源: 上海普陀" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-8], passes the seven standards down without the notice's sentence on other units: "请各单位严格遵照执行。在采购上述产品时，应当将CPU、操作系统、数据库等关键部件符合安全可靠测评要求纳入采购需求。" [cn-ga31-serverstd75-8] (all units are to comply strictly; when buying these products they shall put into the purchase requirement that key components such as the CPU, the operating system and the database meet the evaluation). Its addressees are the district's commissions, offices, bureaus, subdistricts and towns, which the notice binds in any case on the project's reading (the notice names Party and government organs from the township level up and does not name subdistricts). Cuts: CONTEXT (the rule as applied: binding on government bodies down to subdistricts and towns; open to additions by any buyer; taken as met at the bid stage on the supplier's own undertaking).

### 2.3 Targets for computing centres

- Action plan for computing infrastructure, 工信部联通信〔2023〕180号, Oct 8, 2023, six bodies, the state-assets regulator among them (the ministry's own page: "工信部联通信〔2023〕180号" [cn-le27-miit-compute-infra-plan-2023]): "逐步形 成自主可控解决方案" (gradually form independently controllable solutions) and "鼓励算力基础设施采用安全可信的基础软硬件进行建设,保障供应链安全" (computing infrastructure is encouraged to be built with secure and trusted basic software and hardware, to safeguard supply-chain security) [cn-policy-compute-plan-pdf]. No share and no deadline.
- National computing-network opinions, Dec 25, 2023 (the state planner and four other bodies): "算力网关键核心技术基本实现安全可靠" (among the goals for the end of 2025: the key core technologies of the computing network to be in the main secure and reliable) [cn-policy-computing-network-1779]. No home-made share: 国产 and 自主可控 are not in the page (0 places each; "安全可靠" is found 4 times). **Every home-made percentage found so far is provincial or municipal; none is national.**
- Shanghai, "Computing Pujiang" plan for 2024 to 2025, of the city communications administration and ten other bodies ("上海市通信管理局等11个部门联合印发" [cn-ga31-early2024-49], a trade site's full copy; a government site of another province, the Shandong Big Data Bureau's, carries the plan with its issuing notice on a page dated 2024-04-01 [cn-ga31-unsaved3-plantexts6-http]; the issuer's own page not reached), as carried by Yicai, Mar 22, 2024: "到2025年,本市新建智算中心国产算力芯片使用占比超过50%,国产存储使用占比超过50%" (by 2025 home-made computing chips make up over 50% of use in the city's newly built intelligent computing centres, and home-made storage over 50%) [cn-policy-shanghai-domestic-target].
- Guizhou, provincial press conference as reported by People's Daily, Mar 24, 2026: "贵州算力总规模中,智算占比、国产化率、区域集中度均超过90%" (of Guizhou's total computing capacity, the share of intelligent computing, the home-made rate and the regional concentration are each above 90%) [cn-policy-guizhou-localization-2026]. The province's own figure; how the rate is counted is not given.
- Jiangsu, four-agency plan (苏通联〔2024〕7号, signed Apr 22, 2024 by the provincial communications administration, development and reform commission, industry department and data bureau with the provincial government's consent, posted Apr 25, 2024: "苏通联〔2024〕7号", "2024年4月22日" [cn-ga31-inth2-js-compute-plan-notice-2024]; the saved PDF, the notice's attachment, carries no date of its own): "新建算力中心国产算力芯片使用占比达 70%以上" [cn-factions-jiangsu-plan] (home-made computing chips to make up 70% or more of use in newly built computing centres). The plan's own table of indicators gives the row "新建算力中心国产算力芯片使用占比(%)" the values 50 for 2025 and 70 for 2030 and the label "预期性" (expected, that is, indicative); in that part of the table only the energy-efficiency row is labelled "约束性" (binding). So Jiangsu's 70% is a target for 2030, 50% is the target for 2025, and the plan itself marks both as indicative. (Corrected Nov 23, 2026: this line had said "Apr 2024" as if the page gave it, and left the years and the label to a search's account. A later reading noticed the missing date; the table in the PDF was then read.)
- Gansu, provincial government document 甘政发〔2026〕1号 of Jan 6, 2026, an "AI plus" action plan (hand-saved Nov 23, 2026; read whole): no share for home-made chips. It supports home-made computing in words: "支持国产化算力集群和全栈国产框架平台建设" (support the building of home-made computing clusters and full-stack home-made framework platforms) [man-cn-o39-gansu-ai-plus-2026]. Its two percentages are targets for 2027: about 70% for the spread of new-generation smart devices and agents, and more than 60% for the share of government services handled «intelligently» (智能办).
- Hunan (plan for green intelligent computing, 2025 to 2030, issued Feb 23, 2025) and Qinghai (two documents, of Feb 2024 and Aug 2026) were hand-saved on Nov 23, 2026 [man-cn-o39-hunan-green-compute-plan-2025] [man-cn-o39-qinghai-green-compute-2024] [man-cn-o39-qinghai-compute-15fyp-2026]. Their PDFs' text layers are damaged; the pages were read as images (transcriptions are kept beside the damaged copies, and the provinces' own HTML pages were read in Archive captures): none sets a share for home-made hardware. Hunan's table has nine goals, the PUE goal marked binding; Qinghai's 2026 table has seven indicators; Hunan's 95% is the present rate for chip-making equipment. Hunan's guiding opinion of Sept 30, 2024 (湘政办发〔2024〕34号), read whole on the provincial portal, sets no share either (this section's row of Sept 30, 2024).
- Sichuan, Nov 15, 2024, the provincial DRC with the economy and IT department, the communications administration and the energy bureau (川发改数据〔2024〕569号; copies on two government sites, the investment-project platform whose site the State Information Center runs and Shanghai's digital-transformation centre; the issuer's own page did not open): "力争国产化算力设备占比超过50%" (strive for home-made computing equipment to exceed 50%) [cn-factions-sichuan50] [cn-ga31-provtexts-1]; "文号：川发改数据〔2024〕569号" [cn-factions-sichuan50]. An aim for the province's computing equipment, with no share for each centre and no condition on funding.
- Shanghai, Mar 2025: "智算规模力争达到200EFLOPS,其中自主可控算力占比超70%" (intelligent computing to reach 200 EFLOPS, of which independently controllable computing over 70%) [cn-factions-shanghai-cloud-20250324].
- Beijing, Apr 2024: "坚定算力自主可控路线" (hold firmly to the route of independently controllable computing power) [cn-factions-beijing-plan-20240424]. The same plan sets a capability by 2027, not a share of use: "具备100%自主可控智算中心建设能力" (have the capability to build intelligent computing centres that are 100% independently controllable). (Added Nov 23, 2026; this file had said the plan gives a route and no number. A later reading found the sentence.)
- **Mar 7, 2024 · Guangdong Communications Administration and eight other provincial bodies, «Yue Suan» plan for computing infrastructure, 2024 to 2025 (粤通联〔2024〕3号)** (a document, on the Administration's site). It aims (力争) for home-made computing to be 70% of what is added by the end of 2025. Words: "力争到2025年底，新增国产化算力占比达到70%" [cn-o39b-guangdong-yuesuan-2024]; "广东省通信管理局等九部门" [cn-o39b-guangdong-yuesuan-2024]; "2024年3月7日" [cn-o39b-guangdong-yuesuan-2024]. Cuts: FOR.
- **Jul 1, 2024 · General Office of the Tianjin Municipal People's Government, plan for the computing industry, 2024 to 2026** (a document, saved official page). Home-made computing chips to exceed 60% of use in the city's computing centres by 2026. Words: "到 2026 年，全市算力中心国产算力芯片使用占比超过 60％" [cn-o39-tianjin-plan-2024]. Cuts: FOR.
- **May 8, 2024 · General Office of the Hebei Provincial People's Government, opinions on computing and the AI industry (冀政办字〔2024〕34号)** (a document: an Archive capture of the provincial industry department's page of May 23, 2024, and a copy on the Xiongan site). 60% or more (以上) of newly added basic software and hardware for computing to be independently controllable; the share counts software and hardware together, not chips alone, and carries no binding-or-expected label. Words: "新增算力基础软硬件设施自主可控比例60%以上" [cn-o39-hebei-opinion-2024-wayback]. Cuts: FOR.
- **Oct 10, 2023 · General Office of the Inner Mongolia regional government, work plan for the digital economy, 2023 to 2025** (a document: Archive capture of the official PDF). Home-made computing to reach 50% or more. Words: "国产化算力占比达50%以上" [cn-o39-nmg-plan-pdf-wayback]. Cuts: FOR.
- **Aug 1, 2022 · Shandong, action plan for a unified computing network, 2022 to 2025, as reported by the provincial daily on the provincial government's site** (accounts of a plan: the provincial daily, and Xinhua and People's Daily Online, which give the provincial communications administration director's account; the plan's text is not held). Home-made computing to reach 90%, a target: as Xinhua tells it, the plan's first task includes striving (争取) for it. Words: "国产化算力将达90%" [cn-o39-shandong-network-2022]; "据山东省通信管理局局长张洪溢介绍" [cn-ga31-provtexts-12]; "争取到2025年国产化算力占比达到90%以上" [cn-ga31-provtexts-12] [cn-ga31-provtexts-4]. Cuts: FOR, weakly (an account, not the text).
- **Dec 28, 2024 · General Office of the Guizhou Provincial People's Government, plan for AI development, 2025 to 2027 (黔府办函〔2024〕69号)** (a document: Archive capture of the provincial page). It aims for a rank, not a share: home-made intelligent computing among the nation's leaders by 2027. Words: "到2027年，全省算力规模达到210Eflops，国产智算能力位居全国前列" [cn-o39-guizhou-ai-plan-2025-wayback]. Guizhou's over-90% (section 2.3, the press conference of Mar 24, 2026) is a reported rate, not this plan's target. Cuts: FOR on direction.
- **Apr 17, 2025 (in force from May 31, 2025) · Zhejiang Provincial People's Government, measures to support AI innovation (浙政发〔2025〕10号: the government's own signed PDF, "浙政发〔2025〕10号" [cn-ga31-plantexts-1], "2025年4月17日" [cn-ga31-plantexts-1], and a full copy on another site, which shows no number)** (a document). It advocates that government departments, computing centres and businesses buy or use home-made software and hardware, and supports localities that have the means in issuing computing vouchers, which may cover up to 30% of the amount actually executed under a contract for independent computing services against up to 10% for others; it sets no share. Words: "倡导政府部门、算力中心、经营主体采购或应用国产软硬件" [cn-le25-zhejiang-ai-measures-2025-smartcity]; "使用自主算力服务的，可 按照不高于合同实际执行金额的30%给予补助" [cn-le25-zhejiang-ai-measures-2025-smartcity]; "采用非自主算力服务的，可按照不高于合同实际执行金额的10%，给予最高800万元补助" [cn-le25-zhejiang-ai-measures-2025-smartcity]; "本措施自2025年5月31日起实施，有效期至2027年12月31日" [cn-le25-zhejiang-ai-measures-2025-smartcity]. Cuts: FOR on direction (a preference and advocacy, not a share); it also allows a smaller subsidy for computing services that are not «independent» (非自主算力服务).
- **Sept 30, 2024 · General Office of the Hunan Provincial People's Government, guiding opinion on green intelligent computing (湘政办发〔2024〕34号)** (a document, the provincial portal; read whole, control word 算力 found 12 times). Its only figures in digits, beside dates and the numbers of the document and the page, are for the industry's size and for how many industry clusters and parks are to be formed (in words it also names two computing clusters to build up); 占比, 比例, 国产化 and 自主可控 are each found 0 times; it asks state bodies and state firms to try new products first and supports foreign-invested firms' part in the industry. Words: "文号：湘政办发〔2024〕34号" [cn-le26-hunan-opinion-2024-34]; "推动政府机关、事业单位、国有企业先行先试创新型产品和应用场景，加速新技术新产品推广。" [cn-le26-hunan-opinion-2024-34]; "落实外商投资法及配套法规，支持外资企业参与全省绿色智能计算产业发展。" [cn-le26-hunan-opinion-2024-34]. Cuts: CONTEXT (no share), and AGAINST a closed-door reading, weakly.
- **Jun 25, 2024 and May 2025 · Shanxi and Ningxia: rewards for data centres 90% or more home-made** (documents). Shanxi pays a one-off reward of up to 5 million yuan to new or rebuilt data centres whose basic software and hardware are 90% or more home-made; Ningxia's industry department describes a reward of up to 10 million yuan on the same test. Words: "对基础软硬件实现国产化90%以上的新建、改建数据中心，给予最高不超过500万元的一次性奖励" [cn-o39b-shanxi-measures-2024-jincheng]; "对基础软硬件实现国产化率90%以上的数据中心，给予企业最高不超过1000万元奖励" [cn-o39-ningxia-reply-2023]. Shanxi's and Ningxia's computing vouchers carry no chip tier (see the voucher table, section 5). Cuts: FOR (a reward, not a target).
- **2023 to 2026 · Provincial plans read that set no share** (documents). Henan encourages intelligent-computing and supercomputing centres to take independently controllable routes such as Ascend and Hygon; Hubei, Fujian, Liaoning, Jilin, Heilongjiang, Yunnan and Anhui speak of raising home-made use without a number; Shaanxi's AI plan (陕发改高技〔2025〕1665号, now held whole on two other sites, cn-ga31-unsaved-shaanxi-aiplus-cpem and -yanqitong), Hainan's (琼府办〔2026〕6号, published abridged: "（此件公开发布，此文有删减）" [cn-ga31-provtexts-21], so a clause left out of it cannot be excluded) and Xinjiang's regional pages read have no share, among them the General Office's services measures of 2026 (新政办发〔2026〕22号, also published abridged: "（本文有删减）" [cn-ga31-cndocs-1]), whose computing items set a target for the end of 2028, "智能算力规模超20万PFLOPS" [cn-ga31-provtexts-9] (item 2, on building computing), and a subsidy, with no word on chips, for those who use Xinjiang's intelligent-computing resources (item 3), "按不超过实际结算金额的20%补贴，最高不超过50万元" [cn-ga31-cndocs-1]; the region's computing plans that its DRC names, "印发《绿色低碳的算力网建设实施方案（2025—2027年）》《自治区绿色算力中心布局方案》" [cn-ga31-cndocs-15], were not reached; Guangxi's 35% is the share of intelligent computing, not of home-made hardware; Chongqing's computing-hub plan of Jun 15, 2026 (渝府办发〔2026〕26号; an Internet Archive capture of Jun 24, 2026, now saved) asks for a 10,000-card cluster on independently controllable chips and sets no share for home-made hardware; its only share is for green power. Words: "鼓励智算、超算中心采用昇腾、海光等自主可控技术路线" [cn-o39-henan-plan-2024-wayback]; "推动智算中心提升国产化率" [cn-o39-fujian-measures-2025]; "以基础要素突破为支撑点，构建自主可控算力底座，建设高质量行业数据集" [cn-le16-hainan-ai-plus-2026-smartcity]; "算力规模达到5EFLOPS，存力规模达到20EB，智能算力占比达35%" [cn-o39-guangxi-digital-plan-2024-wayback]; Chongqing: "探索建设集自主可控芯片、通信网络、训练框架于一体的万卡级算力集群" [cn-o39-chongqing-hub-plan-2026-wayback], and its one share, "确保新建数据中心绿电占比超过80%" [cn-o39-chongqing-hub-plan-2026-wayback]. Cuts: CONTEXT; AGAINST a uniform national push of the same strength.
- **Count of the 31 provincial-level governments, as of Nov 25, 2026.** 9 set a share for home-made computing hardware (Jiangsu, Shanghai, Sichuan, Guangdong, Tianjin, Hebei, Inner Mongolia, Shandong, Zhejiang; Shandong's only in press accounts of a plan whose text is not held; Zhejiang's, found on Nov 25, 2026, is a project target for the computing of one sector, the province's information and communications industry, by 2030, in a plan posted Jul 29, 2026: this section's row of that date); 20 have a plan read that sets none (the 19 of the first count, with Chongqing moved there by a later reading; Zhejiang, moved there by the same reading, now sets a share); 1 is known only from accounts of its plan, the issuer's own news item among them (Tibet, whose 60% is the share of intelligent computing, not of home-made hardware); 1 was not reached (Xinjiang: no regional computing or AI plan text found; the regional pages read set no share). The count is of what was found. The plans of the kind that holds Zhejiang's share (the provincial communications administrations' five-year plans for the information and communications industry) were searched as a class on Nov 25, 2026 for the other 30 units: the plan for 2026 to 2030 was read in full for ten (Beijing, Shanghai, Anhui, Fujian, Jiangxi, Shandong, Henan, Chongqing, Yunnan, Qinghai; Shanghai's and Shandong's in trade sites' copies, their own PDFs being scans) and as a consultation draft for two (Heilongjiang, Guangdong), only the plan for 2021 to 2025 for seven more (Inner Mongolia, Jiangsu, Sichuan, Ningxia, Gansu, Shaanxi, Hainan), and for eleven no plan text was found, only a release or an account (Tianjin, Hebei, Shanxi, Liaoning, Jilin, Hubei, Hunan, Guangxi, Guizhou, Tibet, Xinjiang). No text read sets a number for a home-made share, so Zhejiang's is the only share found in plans of this kind (this section's entry on the class search; table: tables/provincial_ict_plans.tsv). Table: tables/provinces_31.tsv. Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Jul 17, 2026 · Shanghai Xuhui District, where, as the page has it, StepFun's Step models and MiniMax's abab model were born** (a Jiefang Daily article as the district government's site carries it). It is pushing a 50,000-card single home-made cluster into use and supports Shanghai INESA's national AI training ground: "徐汇正全力推进5万卡国产单集群的规模化落地应用，支持上海仪电建设国家人工智能训练场" [cn-ga31-smalllabs-44]; the same page lists "阶跃星辰Step系列大模型" among the five base models born in the district [cn-ga31-smalllabs-44]. A promotion, not a rule for any company. Beside it, Biren's own page on its joint laboratory with INESA's computing arm and StepFun (opened Feb 2, by the page): the lab "积极响应国家“自主可控、安全可靠”的人工智能发展战略" [cn-ga31-smalllabs-40] (actively answers the national strategy for AI development that is «independent and controllable, secure and reliable»), and a cluster of about 1,000 of Biren's cards was deployed at INESA for it, "采用壁仞科技壁砺™ 166系列产品的千卡智算集群在上海仪电进行部署" [cn-ga31-smalllabs-40] (the chip maker's words, not StepFun's). Cuts: FOR (a local government and a partner it supports steer a smaller lab toward home-made chips), CONTEXT (no instruction to the lab).
- **Jul 2024 to Jun 7, 2025 · The state counts home-made chips in computing centres: a national computing survey (2025), its domestic-rate field, and a draft standard that defines the rate** (documents: the Shanghai economy commission's notice on the city's portal; a draft of the national data standardisation committee on the data administration's site; the state planner's plan for green data centres, an annex PDF). Shanghai's notice of Apr 18, 2025 carries out a national survey of computing ordered by the general offices of the state planner and the industry ministry and the general department (综合司) of the data administration, and asks firms to report, among other fields, the number of chips and the home-made rate for computing built, being built or planned, as of Mar 31, 2025. The draft of Jun 7, 2025 for assessing computing centres defines the "AI accelerator card home-made rate" as home-made accelerator chips over all accelerator chips, counted by card; it sets no minimum. The plan for green data centres (Jul 2024) requires new and enlarged data centres to use servers at or above set energy-saving grades, a test of energy, not of origin. Words: "印发日期:2025-04-18" [cn-ga31-early2024-38]; "沪经信基〔2025〕235 号" [cn-ga31-early2024-38]; "根据《国家发改委办公厅、工业和信息化部办公厅、国家数据局综合司关于开展全国算力摸底工作的通知》要求" [cn-ga31-early2024-38]; "芯片数量及国产化率" [cn-ga31-early2024-38]; "(征求意见稿) 2025 年 6 月 7 日" [cn-ga31-early2024-39]; "全国数据标准化技术委员会 发布" [cn-ga31-early2024-39]; "计算国产加速卡芯片数量占总体加速卡芯片数量的比值" [cn-ga31-early2024-39] (the ratio of home-made accelerator chips to all accelerator chips); "规定的节能水平及以上服务器产品" [cn-factions-ndrc-energy-2024]. The national survey notice itself was not reached. This is the published trace of the survey of early 2025 that a named industry person ties to the spring window guidance (Tier 2, context row C5). Cuts: FOR on direction (the state asks for the home-made rate in a national survey of computing, seen here as Shanghai carries it out for general and intelligent computing built, being built or planned in the city), CONTEXT (a count and a definition, not a quota).
- **Nov 2026 · Provincial and city texts of the month: Jiangsu's plan for new information infrastructure (苏工信信基〔2026〕329号, posted Nov 19), Fujian's AI Plus plan, Beijing's embodied-robotics plan, and a Jiangsu state institute's tender** (documents on official sites). Jiangsu's five bodies make "home-made substitution" of computing chips and other core components a priority and back centres in Nanjing and Suzhou to verify adaptation to home-made computing; the plan's only percentages are for green power and network coverage (its PUE limit is a ratio, 1.2), not for home-made chips (国产化率 0, 英伟达 0; control: 芯片 7). Fujian lays out next-generation AI application chips; Beijing supports research on RISC-V intelligent-computing chips; neither sets a share. The Jiangsu Water Resources Research Institute's amended tender of Nov 23 keeps at least eight home-made GPU cards per economy server. Words: "苏工信信基〔 2026〕329号" [cn-ga31-livecn-4]; "江苏省工信厅 gxt.jiangsu.gov.cn 日期:2026-11-19" [cn-ga31-livecn-4]; "重点突破算力芯片、光通信芯片、卫星芯片、射频芯片等核心器件国产化替代" [cn-ga31-livecn-4] (focus on breaking through home-made substitution of core components such as computing chips, optical-communication chips, satellite chips and radio-frequency chips); "支持南京、苏州建设国产算力适配验证中心,推动全栈国产化应用与适配" [cn-ga31-livecn-4]; "布局新一代人工智能应用芯片和具身智能" [cn-ga31-livecn-61]; "基于RISC-V的系统级智算芯片" [cn-ga31-livecn-48]; "配置不少于8张国产GPU卡" [cn-ga31-livecn-42]. Cuts: FOR on direction (Jiangsu's word is substitution), without a share: Jiangsu's 70% target of 2024 (held) is not repeated in this plan.
- **Jul 7, 2025 and Jun to Jul 2026 · Pingdingshan, Liaoning and Wuhu, in replies to two deputies, a member and a party's provincial committee: a state-funded centre designed for 80% or more home-made AI chips, and "window guidance" in officials' own words** (documents on the bodies' own sites). Pingdingshan's administrative-approval and government-information bureau (平审信〔2025〕13号 and 15号, both posted Jul 7, 2025) says the city's public AI computing centre at Baigui Lake, approved on Jan 5, 2024 and to be paid for with fiscal funds and special government bonds, is designed with home-made AI chips at 80% or more. The same replies give the bureau's account of the "window guidance" that national and provincial bodies issued in April 2025: all computing-centre projects fall under it; in principle no new large centre outside the national hub nodes; in principle local governments and state firms are not to fund small facilities; facilities whose building is paid for by other «social funds» (其他社会资金) stay outside it and are filed. Liaoning's data bureau (Jun 24, 2026) says that under the current national window guidance its projects are mainly building work, with computing equipment bought later as demand comes. Wuhu's data bureau (posted Jul 23, 2026, to the deputy Wei Jiansong) says it monitors the chip localisation rate among the key indicators of projects in its cluster, those below standard to be put right in time or to leave; it gives no number. Words: "平审信〔2025〕13号" [cn-ga31-carriers-34]; "资金来源为财政资金及政府专项债" [cn-ga31-carriers-34]; "其中国产化智算芯片国产化率80%以上" [cn-ga31-carriers-34]; "平审信〔2025〕15号" [cn-ga31-twelve-14]; "2025年4月，国家、省相继下发新政策，要求对算力中心建设实施“窗口指导”" [cn-ga31-twelve-14]; "国家枢纽节点外原则上不得新建大型算力中心项目" [cn-ga31-twelve-14]; "原则上地方政府和国有企业不得出资建设小型算力设施" [cn-ga31-twelve-14]; "算力设施建设由其 他社会资金出资建设的不纳入“窗口指导”范围，采用项目备案制度" [cn-ga31-twelve-14]; "按照现行国家“窗口指导”政策，我省现有算力项目以土建为主，算力设备按需滞后投资" [cn-ga31-replies-6]; "芯片国产化率等关键指标实行动态监测和履约评价" [cn-ga31-replies-18]; "对不达标项目依规限期整改或有序退出" [cn-ga31-replies-18]; "发布日期： 2026-07-23" [cn-ga31-replies-18]. As these officials describe it, the window guidance of 2025 governs where and how large centres are built and who pays; neither says it governs whose chips. The 80% is one project's design, not a provincial rule. Cuts: FOR (a centre to be built with public money, designed for home-made chips; a city tracking chip localisation), CONTEXT (window guidance on building, not on chips).
- **Jan 2025 · Shanghai Commission of Economy and Informatization, guide for building intelligent computing centres, 2025 edition (上海市智算中心建设导则（2025年版）), full text** (a document: the 67-page PDF as Invest Shanghai's site holds it, uploaded Jan 15, 2025 by its address; the commission's own copies answer 404). Organised by the commission and written by the China Academy of Information and Communications Technology, it applies in principle to every intelligent computing centre built in the city, for own use or hosted. What it calls the entry requirement (准入要求) for a new centre is energy efficiency (baseline PUE), beside general requirements, among them siting, the operator's licence, rack power, self-assessment and energy use; its post-launch assessment counts load, PUE, water, carbon, green, availability, security and computing grades; it sets no share of home-made chips and no tiers by one: 国产 occurs twice and 自主可控 twice in the whole text, all in two recommendations (宜, should), one for the AI hardware layer and one for edge centres, which are encouraged to use home-made chips. The software layer must support adaptation to several mainstream kinds of AI accelerator. A city bureau dates its release to Jan 10, 2025, and the commission says it set conditions for being allowed to build, assessment standards and procedures. Words: "上海市智算中心建设导则 （2025 年版）" [cn-ga31-replies-22]; "2025 年 1 月" [cn-ga31-replies-22]; "本导则由上海市经济和信息化委员会组织编制，中国信息通信研究院具体承担。解释权归上海市经济和信息化委员会。" [cn-ga31-replies-22]; "在本市范围内建设智算中心，包含资产自用和托管两种业务场景，原则上应按本导则要求执行" [cn-ga31-replies-22]; "综合 PUE 准入值和先进值分别不高于 1.22 和 1.18" [cn-ga31-replies-22]; "衡量智算中心算力与算效水平的算力与算效等级" [cn-ga31-replies-22]; "宜采用自主可控的 AI 训练及推理加速卡、AI 服务器、高性能存储和智算网络等相关设备" [cn-ga31-replies-22]; "智算设备宜自主可控，鼓励使用国产算力芯片及国产存储" [cn-ga31-replies-22]; "应支持多种主流 AI 加速芯片硬件适配，以人工智能算力为主（如 GPU、FPGA、ASIC 等）" [cn-ga31-replies-22]; "组织编制《上海市智算中心建设导则（2025年版）》并于2025年1月10日发布" [cn-ga31-replies-17]; "今年一季度，我委编制发布《上海市智算中心建设导则（ 2025 年版）》，明确准建条件、评估标准、工作流程等要求" [cn-ga31-replies-5]. That this copy is the commission's own text is not proven by bytes (the commission's file did not load); its foreword names the commission as organiser. Cuts: FOR in direction (a recommendation, not a share), AGAINST the company list's «tiered entry conditions on the chip localisation rate» (none in the text).

Words: "海淀区建设的北京人工智能公共算力中心2025年完成扩容" [cn-ga31-replies-37]; "算力规模达1200P，国产算力占比超80%" [cn-ga31-replies-37] [cn-ga31-replies-38]. How the share is counted is not given. - **Jun 26, 2025 (posted Jul 7, 2025) · Pingdingshan Municipal Administration of Administrative Approval and Government Information, with the city's development and reform commission and science bureau, reply to CPPCC proposal 254 (平审信〔2025〕15号)** (a document, on the bureau's site). A public AI computing centre in Baigui Lake, approved on Jan 5, 2024 and to be paid for with public money and special government bonds, is designed on home-made CPUs and AI chips, for the network security of the centre, with at least 80% home-made AI chips: "资金来源为财政资金及政府专项债" [cn-ga31-twelve-14]; "本项目选取自主可控产品，采用国产CPU、国产AI芯片、国产自主软件系统" [cn-ga31-twelve-14] (the project chooses independently controllable products, using home-made CPUs, home-made AI chips and home-made independent software systems); "其中国产化智算芯片国产化率80%以上" [cn-ga31-twelve-14] (of which the home-made rate of the intelligent-computing chips is 80% or more). The same city's reply 平审信〔2025〕13号 of the same date gives the same 80% [cn-ga31-carriers-34]. A design figure for one project, set before construction (to start in 2025 once the bond money arrives), not a rule for other centres. Cuts: FOR (a state-funded centre, approved in 2024, planned on home-made chips by the reply of June 2025); CONTEXT for the Reuters report of Nov 5, 2025 on state-funded data centres: an official text of the same kind for one city, not the national guidance.

No imports, which is not the same thing (section 2.2): the network emergency centre's Beijing branch, "国家计算机网络与信息安全管理中心北京分中心算力服务器采购项目", "本项目采购预算为：95万元", "本项目不接受进口产品参加谈判，进口产品是指“通过中国海关报关验放进入中国境内且产自关境外的产品”" [cn-ga31-localrule-50] (Jul 23, 2026; one server). - **Jun 18, 2025 to Oct 17, 2026 · The spring 2025 «window guidance» in officials' words, a news item on a district's site and a filing: its title and number, its issuers, a rule on where and how centres are built** (documents and one filing). Tongliao's city government, "文号通政发〔2025〕115号成文日期2025-06-18" [cn-ga31-cndocs-58], in its plan for green computing, orders strict compliance with "《关于对算力基础设施实施“窗口指导”的通知》（发改高技〔2025〕320号）" [cn-ga31-cndocs-58], beside the State Council General Office's notice forwarding a plan of the state planner and other departments on energy-intensive, high-emission projects ("严格执行《国务院办公厅关于转发国家发展改革委等部门〈有力有效管控高耗能高排放项目总体方案〉的通知》（国办函〔2025〕22号）" [cn-ga31-cndocs-58]), in a paragraph on energy-efficient servers: the only official text found that gives the notice's title and number (a number of the state planner's high-technology series). Who stands behind it, in a city bureau's words and a news item on a district's site: Fuzhou's data bureau Party group, "发布时间：2026-02-26" [cn-ga31-cndocs-10]: "根据国家发展改革委、数据局、工信部联合对算力中心实施“窗口指导”的要求，我局已向上推送多个算力基础设施项目申请“窗口指导”" [cn-ga31-cndocs-10]; Guangzhou's Huangpu district, on its own site (a news item credited to 黄埔融媒, dated Apr 10, 2026): "2025年4月，国家发改委牵头印发算力基础设施项目窗口指导相关文件" and "2026年2月，4个项目成功通过国家算力基础设施建设审批" [cn-ga31-localrule-25], one of them "云下科技汇云数据中心是民营企业项目，采用华为910B芯片" [cn-ga31-localrule-25]. What it asks, in officials' words: Ulanqab's "乌兰察布市大数据管理局局长郭晓玲" [cn-ga31-localrule-23] (Inner Mongolia Daily's account, reposted by the national data administration on Oct 17, 2026): "按照国家‘窗口指导’要求，八大枢纽节点内算力中心项目电能利用效率（PUE值）不高于1.2" [cn-ga31-localrule-23]; and a listed builder, 晶科科技 (601778), "公告编号：2026-022" [cn-ga31-cndocs-40], citing public information: "根据公开信息，2025年4月，国家发展改革委、工业和信息化部、国家数据局联合发文明确，大型及以上算力基础设施项目须由国家评估论证后方可开工建设" [cn-ga31-cndocs-40]. None of these says the guidance governs whose chips a centre uses (an industry association's report of 2025 sums it up with a point on chips, a duty to state their type, number and home-made rate, with no share set: this section, the entry on that report); with Pingdingshan's and Liaoning's replies (section 2.3, above) it governs approval, siting, power efficiency and who pays. The notice's own text is not published; the paper Leiphone reported for October 2025 (section 8) has no official trace. Cuts: CONTEXT (central review of large centres before they are built, as officials and a filer describe it; not a chip rule), FOR weakly (a lever over building that reports say was used for chips).
- **Aug 29, 2025 · National data standardisation committee, technical document TC609-6-2025-09 on assessing computing centres' capability** (a document; a technical paper, not a binding standard). Among its measures of a centre's network security it counts the home-made rate of AI accelerators by chips, and sets no minimum: "TC609-6-2025-09全国一体化算力网算力中心能力评估要求" [cn-ga31-cndocs-77]; "算网安全：评估算力中心国产化率和安全服务方面" [cn-ga31-cndocs-77]; "AI加速卡国产化率=国产加速卡芯片数量（颗）/总体加速卡芯片数量（颗）" [cn-ga31-cndocs-77]. So the national committee's framework for assessing computing centres has a measure, by count of chips, for how home-made their accelerators are. Cuts: FOR on direction (the state counts it), CONTEXT (a measure, not a quota).
- **Jun to Nov 2026 · The window guidance at work, in a province's reply and a builder's filing (and, as a lead, a carrier's purchase notice of Nov 2025): a bar on local subsidies for building, an opinion still awaited in November 2026; again no word on chips in what they say of the guidance** (a document on the body's own site; a filing; a purchase notice as a tender site carries it. Adds to the two entries above on the spring 2025 guidance). Liaoning's data bureau, in the reply already cited (Jun 24, 2026), also says computing centres are now classed as high-energy, high-emission projects and are strictly bound by the state planner's window guidance, which bars local governments from any fiscal subsidy for building them: "当前算力中心已被列为“两高”项目并受国家发改委“窗口指导”政策严格约束，明确规定地方政府不得对算力建设环节进行任何财政补贴政策" [cn-ga31-replies-6]. A purchase notice of China Telecom's Zhanjiang branch for an energy-saving report and a «two highs» (high-energy, high-emission) assessment report on an infrastructure project that its title does not name is held as a tender site carries it, listed there on Nov 21, 2025, "湛江电信某基础设施项目节能报告及“两高”项目论证报告编制服务采购询比公告" [cn-ga31-nameddocs-18]; what the page says of an application for window guidance stands only in parts of the form that a reader is not shown, so it is not quoted here: a lead. "晶科电力科技股份有限公司" [cn-ga31-nameddocs-6] (Jinko Power, 601778), answering the Shanghai exchange again in November 2026 on its 1 GW computing centre at Zhongwei, says the project's six-month run of permits starts only from the guidance opinion, which had not come: "截至本回复出具日，公司尚未取得窗口指导正式意见。" [cn-ga31-nameddocs-6]; "中卫项目后续工期以“取得窗口指导意见”作为启动前提，目前该前置审批事项仍在主管部门审核流程中" [cn-ga31-nameddocs-6]; "获得窗口指导意见后的6个月内" [cn-ga31-nameddocs-6]. In these the guidance is a condition for starting a centre's further works and bars local fiscal subsidies for building. None names a chip, a home-made share or the paper that the press reported for about October 2025 on state-funded centres and home-made chips; of that paper no number, title, citing document or official's words was found (section 8). Cuts: CONTEXT (a working central gate on large centres, through which a chip condition could be passed without being published; no text shows that it was).
- **2021 and May to Nov 2026 · The provincial communications administrations' five-year plans for the information and communications industry, searched as a class for a home-made share** (documents: the administrations' own pages and PDFs, two trade sites' copies, releases; table: tables/provincial_ict_plans.tsv). Zhejiang's share of Jul 29, 2026 stands in a kind of plan each provincial communications administration writes. For the other 30 provincial-level units the plan for 2026 to 2030 was read in full for ten (Beijing, Shanghai, Anhui, Fujian, Jiangxi, Shandong, Henan, Chongqing, Yunnan, Qinghai; Shanghai's and Shandong's in trade sites' copies, their own PDFs being scans) and as a consultation draft for two (Heilongjiang, Guangdong); for seven more only the plan for 2021 to 2025 was read (Inner Mongolia, Jiangsu, Sichuan, Ningxia, Gansu, Shaanxi, Hainan); for eleven nothing but a release or an account was found (Tianjin, Hebei, Shanxi, Liaoning, Jilin, Hubei, Hunan, Guangxi, Guizhou, Tibet, Xinjiang), their texts not being on the administrations' file lists. No text read sets a number for a home-made share of computing, chips or equipment: every percentage in the 23 plan texts read (25 saved copies) was checked by script for a home-made word beside it, and the same check finds Zhejiang's 60%; one more plan text read since, Henan's for 2021 to 2025 in the administration's own PDF, has none either (20 percentages, none beside a home-made word; 20 indicators marked expected and 2 binding). What the 2026 plans say without a number: Beijing (京信管发〔2026〕7号), "有序部署万卡及以上智算集群，加大力度适配国产算力芯片" [cn-ga31-inth2-bj15-plan-pdf], the national plan's own phrase (section 2.1), which Fujian, "加大力度适配国产算力芯片" [cn-ga31-ictplans-15], and Yunnan (云通信局发〔2026〕35号), "加大力度适配国产算力芯片，探索异构智算资源池规模化建设" [cn-ga31-ictplans-26], repeat; Shanghai (沪通信管发〔2026〕28号, as a trade site copies it) asks to raise a rate and gives none, "支持新型算力超节点建设，提升自主AI芯片部署率" [cn-ga31-ictplans-16]; Anhui names a home-made cluster, "高质量推进芜湖国家级数据中心集群及合肥国产化万卡智算集群建设" [cn-ga31-inth2-ah-attach-1-pdf]; Chongqing's home-made words are about the industrial internet's identifier system and a video-network protocol, not computing chips: "支持国产自主可控的视联网协议技术创新应用" [cn-ga31-inth2-cq15-plan-pdf]; Shandong's, Henan's, Jiangxi's and Qinghai's plans do not speak of home-made computing chips at all (Shandong's computing item: "支持重点城市按需部署智算集群及省内企业建设智算中心" [cn-ga31-inth2-sd15-lmtw]; Qinghai, 青通局〔2026〕97号: "有序部署万卡及以上智算集群，打造以智算为主，通算、超算为辅的多元算力供给体系" [cn-ga31-inth2-qh15-plan-pdf2]). Guangdong's draft of July 2026 does not repeat the 70% of its 2024 plan; its home-made words are "支持基于华为等国产自主创新生态，抢抓全球人工智能发展机遇" [cn-ga31-inth2-gd15-draft-pdf] (a draft, not the plan). Yunnan's "智算规模占比超95%" [cn-ga31-ictplans-26] is the share of intelligent computing, not of home-made hardware. Issuers and dates as the pages give them: "京信管发〔2026〕7号" [cn-ga31-ictplans-1]; "沪通信管发〔2026〕28号" [cn-ga31-ictplans-16]; "云通信局发〔2026〕35号" [cn-ga31-inth2-yn15-notice]; "青通局〔2026〕97号" [cn-ga31-inth2-qh15-notice]. So among the plans of this kind whose text could be read, Zhejiang's is the only one that sets a home-made share; eleven units' plans were issued and not published in full where the search looked, Inner Mongolia and Shaanxi show only a call for comment on a plan for 2026 to 2030, Hainan's administration said in January 2026 that it was drafting one and on Jul 23, 2026 that the drafting was to be sped up, Shaanxi's still spoke on Nov 4, 2026 of drawing its plan up and carrying it out, and Gansu's, which shows no plan, told the operators in February 2026 that the planning for 2026 to 2030 was to be drawn up and carried out. Cuts: CONTEXT (the count of nine now rests on a searched class, with eleven texts unread); AGAINST a uniform push (in 2026 the sector's provincial plans, Shanghai's and Shandong's among them, set no home-made share).

The opinions that set independently controllable computing at over 70% of the city's intelligent computing by 2027, as an aim (力争), also tell the commission and the city communications administration to bring overseas cloud providers to Shanghai, and aim to attract leading cloud providers, Chinese and foreign, to invest more in the city. The same page names Anhui's plan for the information and communications industry for 2021 to 2025, without naming its issuer, whose text was not found: "及《安徽省信息通信业“十四五”发展规划》等要求" [cn-ga31-ictplansmissing-29]. Cuts: FOR, weakly (in 2024 a provincial regulator of the state operators asks for a rising home-made share of their new computing, GPUs named first); CONTEXT for the count of provinces (no number, so Anhui stays among those whose texts set no share).

- **2025 (the report's own year; the copy's address carries 20251221) · An industry association's report sums up the window guidance of spring 2025 with a line on chips: a computing centre must state the type, number and home-made rate of the chips it uses** (an industry body's summary, not the guidance's text: the report 《全球重点区域算力竞争态势分析报告(2025年)》 of the data-centre committee of the China Communications Industry Association, compiled with companies and others, "编制单位 中国通信工业协会数据中心委员会" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-nameddocs15-dfcfw], as a Chinese financial site's file host carries it). In its table of national policies the row dated 2025/03, issuers given as the state planner, the industry ministry and the data administration, is named for the window guidance of computing infrastructure projects and has four points. Words: "指导对象为已建成、在建、拟建算力中心" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-nameddocs15-dfcfw] (it applies to centres built, being built and planned); "算力中心需明确投资金额构成及出资方" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-nameddocs15-dfcfw] (a centre must state how its investment is made up and who pays); "算力中心需明确采用芯片的类型、数量、国产化率等指标" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-nameddocs15-dfcfw] (a centre must state such indicators as the type, number and home-made rate of the chips it uses). As summarised it is a duty to declare, not a share to be met: the row sets no percentage and bars no chip. Beside a note of spring 2025 from an institution its site does not name (未知机构), which says the guidance encouraged home-made computing (Tier 2, context row C4), it is a second account that gives that guidance a point on chips, and the point matches what the national computing survey of spring 2025 asked firms to report, the number of chips and the home-made rate (this section, the entry on that survey); the officials' own words on the guidance (this section) speak of approval, siting, power efficiency and who pays, and the notice itself is not published. A press report of Oct 31, 2025 describes a later paper on window guidance with rules on chips, of which no copy was found (Tier 2). The report gives as the sources of its table "资料来源：中共中央、国务院、工信部、国家数据局等" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-nameddocs15-dfcfw] and cites no document for the row. Cuts: FOR, weakly (limit 2: by an industry body's account the unpublished guidance has the state ask every centre what share of its chips is home-made; an account, and of a duty to declare); CONTEXT.

### 2.4 Regulators' acts against foreign chips

- Market regulator, notice dated Dec 9, 2024 ("发布时间：2024-12-09 19:30" [cn-policy-samr-open-original]), saying it had recently (近日) opened the case: "因英伟达公司涉嫌违反《中华人民共和国反垄断法》... 市场监管总局依法对英伟达公司开展立案调查" (because Nvidia is suspected of violating the Anti-Monopoly Law ... the regulator has, according to law, opened an investigation of Nvidia) [cn-policy-samr-open-original].
- Market regulator, Sept 15, 2025: "经初步调查,英伟达公司违反《中华人民共和国反垄断法》和《市场监管总局关于附加限制性条件批准英伟达公司收购迈络思科技有限公司股权案反垄断审查决定的公告》,市场监管总局依法决定对其实施进一步调查" (a preliminary investigation found that Nvidia violated the Anti-Monopoly Law and the regulator's decision approving its purchase of Mellanox's equity with conditions; the regulator has decided in accordance with the law to investigate further) [cn-policy-samr-further-original].
- Cyberspace regulator, Jul 31, 2025: it "约谈了英伟达公司,要求英伟达公司就对华销售的H20算力芯片漏洞后门安全风险问题进行说明并提交相关证明材料" (summoned Nvidia and required it to explain the security risks of vulnerabilities and backdoors in the H20 chips sold to China and to submit supporting material) [cn-policy-cac-h20-original].
- The precedent: the Cybersecurity Review Office on Micron, published on the cyberspace regulator's site on May 21, 2023 (the office sits in that regulator: "网络安全审查办公室设在国家互联网信息办公室" [cn-policy-review-measures]; corrected Nov 23, 2026: this line had named the regulator itself as the issuer): its products "存在较严重网络安全问题隐患,对我国关键信息基础设施供应链造成重大安全风险" (carry fairly serious hidden dangers of cybersecurity problems and pose a major security risk to the supply chain of China's critical information infrastructure) [cn-policy-micron-decision]. Its operative sentence: "我国内关键信息基础设施的运营者应停止采购美光公司产品" (operators of critical information infrastructure in China shall stop buying Micron's products). The page calls it the review office's conclusion that the products do not pass the review: "网络安全审查办公室依法作出不予通过网络安全审查的结论" [cn-policy-micron-decision]. So when the review office barred a foreign chip maker's products for a class of buyers, the conclusion was published. For Nvidia the pages read hold a summons and no decision. That cuts both ways: Beijing can publish a ban and has not; or its method for Nvidia was unpublished guidance, as the two company filings say.
- Customs Law, article 24: "国家限制进出口的货物,没有进出口许可证件的,不予放行" (goods whose import or export the state restricts shall not be released without licence documents); article 28: "进出口货物应当接受海关查验" (import and export goods shall be subject to customs inspection) [cn-policy-customs-law-wayback, a finance-ministry page in an Internet Archive capture of Apr 10, 2026]. Customs may inspect any goods or articles entering or leaving the country and must not release restricted goods that lack a licence. None of China's published lists of restricted or banned imports that could be read names these chips (the licence lists of 2024 to 2026 and the banned-import lists in force, read whole by script: section 8), so the reported customs holds (Tier 2) may rest on the general inspection power, on a temporary decision outside the lists (the Foreign Trade Law, below) that was not found published, or on something else unpublished.
- The measures Beijing published in these three notices are on the export side: export licences for gallium and germanium ("决定对镓、锗相关物项实施出口管制") [cn-policy-gallium-germanium]; a near-ban on exports to the United States of dual-use items related to four materials, Dec 2024 ("原则上不予许可镓、锗、锑、超硬材料相关两用物项对美国出口") [cn-policy-us-minerals-2024]; and that ban's suspension "自即日起至2026年11月27日" (from today until Nov 27, 2026) [cn-policy-us-minerals-suspension]. No published restriction on importing AI chips was found in the pages read or in the import lists of 2024 to 2026 (section 8); the one published import measure against American chips found is the anti-dumping investigation of analog chips below.
- Ministry of State Security, as reproduced by Xinhua, Jul 21, 2025, headline: "境外生产芯片可能故意留“后门”" [cn-acts-mss-xinhua-20250721] (chips produced abroad may have backdoors deliberately left in them); the body says «some»: "一些境外生产的芯片、智能设备或者软件可能在设计制造阶段就被故意预埋了“后门”" [cn-acts-mss-xinhua-20250721] (some chips, smart devices or software made abroad may have had backdoors planted on purpose at the design and manufacturing stage), and says key posts that handle secrets can use independently controllable chips and home-made operating systems: "重点涉密岗位可通过采用自主可控芯片和国产操作系统，避免境外软硬件后门风险" [cn-acts-mss-xinhua-20250721]; "来源：国家安全部微信公众号" [cn-acts-mss-xinhua-20250721].
- **Sept 13, 2025, and Nov 2025 · Ministry of Commerce, anti-dumping investigation of American analog chips, as reported in Microchip's filings** (a US company's filing reporting a Chinese act, and the ministry's own announcement, 商务部公告2025年第27号, now saved). The ministry opened the investigation; by Microchip's 10-Q of Nov 5, 2026 the Chinese government agreed to suspend these investigations under the trade agreement announced on Nov 1, 2025, and may resume the analog-chip investigation at its election. Words: "on September 13, 2025, China's Ministry of Commerce initiated an antidumping investigation of imports into China of analog semiconductors originating in the United States" [cn-le06-mchp-10k-fy2026]; "While we were not a subject of this investigation and the Chinese government agreed to suspend these investigations as part of a trade agreement announced on November 1, 2025" [cn-le06-mchp-10q-2026-09]. The ministry's own words: "商务部决定自2025年9月13日起对原产于美国的进口相关模拟芯片进行反倾销立案调查" [cn-ga31-importcat-23]; the products are "相关模拟芯片中使用40nm及以上工艺制程的通用接口芯片（Commodity Interface IC Chip）和栅极驱动芯片（Gate Driver IC Chip）" [cn-ga31-importcat-23], under one customs number, other goods excluded: "该产品归在《中华人民共和国进出口税则》：85423990。该税则号项下其他产品不在本次调查范围之内" [cn-ga31-importcat-23]. Cuts: CONTEXT (a published import measure against American chips in trade talks; by its own words it does not reach AI chips).
- **Apr 10, 2026 · State Administration for Market Regulation, review of its 2025 merger-control work** (a document). It lists its further investigation of Nvidia among its 2025 enforcement, beside a case opened on Qualcomm; its news list to Nov 23, 2026 shows a published suspension of the DuPont China case and no title ending or suspending the Nvidia case. Words: "对英伟达公司实施进一步调查、对高通公司立案调查，维护法律权威" [cn-le25-samr-2025-merger-review-20260410] (carried out a further investigation of Nvidia and opened a case on Qualcomm, upholding the authority of the law); "市场监管总局暂停对杜邦中国集团有限公司的反垄断调查程序" [cn-le25-samr-dupont-suspend-20250722] (the regulator suspends its antitrust investigation of DuPont China Group). No page found states the present status of the Nvidia case: do not say it was ended, and do not say it was not. Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Jun 5, 2026 (the day of the regulator's notice that it had issued the report 近日, recently) · State Administration for Market Regulation (National Anti-Monopoly Bureau), annual report on antitrust enforcement for 2025** (a document). It lists the further investigation of Nvidia among the year's work, in a sentence that ends "维护公平竞争" (protecting fair competition), and again under its supervision of conditional decisions; it gives no outcome, and 美国 appears 0 times in it. The regulator's lists read on Nov 24, 2026 add nothing: the antitrust bureau's work news (90 entries, Dec 1, 2017 to Nov 24, 2026) names Nvidia only on Dec 9, 2024 and Sept 15, 2025, and the list of conditional decisions (40 entries, Jul 25, 2018 to Oct 10, 2026) has no notice changing or lifting the Mellanox conditions, while it carries notices lifting the conditions of two other deals and changing the party bound by a third. Words: "对英伟达违反《中华人民共和国反垄断法》（以下简称《反垄断法》）实施进一步调查，立案调查高通收购Autotalks违法实施经营者集中，维护公平竞争" [cn-ga15-samr-antitrust-annual-2025-pdf]; "发布时间：2026-06-05" [cn-ga15-samr-antitrust-annual-2025-notice]; "市场监管总局依法决定实施进一步调查" [cn-ga15-samr-fldes-worknews-list-20261124] (from the Sept 15, 2025 title); "市场监管总局关于附加限制性条件批准英伟达公司收购迈络思科技有限公司股权案反垄断审查决定的公告" [cn-ga15-samr-conditional-list-20261124]. Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Jul 21, 2025 · Ministry of State Security, its WeChat article 《当心你身边的“隐形窃密通道”》, read whole** (a published text of a state body; the Tibet regional cyberspace office's reprint of the same day, like Xinhua's copy, runs it under the headline the record cites, keeps the article's own title at the head of its text and names its source; CCTV's copy, which credits the ministry's WeChat account, runs it under its own headline, "国安部发文提示：境外生产芯片可能故意留“后门” 摄像头被远程开启" [cn-factions-mss-cctv-20250721]). Its scope is wider and its advice narrower than the headline the record cites. It names three ways a back door gets in: possibly planted on purpose at design and manufacture in some chips, smart devices or software made abroad; left for after-sales service by a few makers, their origin not said; and planted by criminals through software updates, open-source code or the supply chain. Its advice: the public should be alert; key posts that handle secrets "can" use independently controllable chips and home-made operating systems; and patching, regular updates and checks of logs and traffic can also lower the risk (the sentence names no one who should do them). It names no company, no product and no country (英伟达 0, 美国 0, 采购 0 in the reprint; control: 芯片 4), and says nothing about AI chips, data centres or what companies should buy. Words: "当心你身边的“隐形窃密通道”" [cn-ga31-mssposts-23]; "文章来源: 国家安全部微信公众号" [cn-ga31-mssposts-23]; "一些境外生产的芯片、智能设备或者软件可能在设计制造阶段就被故意预埋了“后门”" [cn-ga31-mssposts-23]; "个别厂家为方便后期维修维护,出厂时设置了允许远程访问的“后门”" [cn-ga31-mssposts-23] (some makers set a back door allowing remote access at the factory, for later repair and maintenance); "个别不法分子可能利用软件更新渠道、污染开源代码库或在供应链环节篡改代码等方式" [cn-ga31-mssposts-23]; "重点涉密岗位可通过采用自主可控芯片和国产操作系统,避免境外软硬件后门风险" [cn-ga31-mssposts-23] (key posts handling secrets can avoid the risk of back doors in foreign hardware and software by using independently controllable chips and home-made operating systems); "还可通过加强技术防护措施,如制定补丁策略、定期进行操作系统更新" [cn-ga31-mssposts-23]. Cuts: FOR (the security side's public warning about chips made abroad, ten days before the cyberspace regulator summoned Nvidia), narrowed: the chip advice is limited to key secret-related posts, is worded "can", and names no maker.
- **Apr 23, 2026 · Ministry of State Security, article 《关键处不能“掉链子”》 on espionage against industrial and supply chains** (a published text of a state body, as Beijing Daily's site carries it at 06:58 Beijing time with "来源: 国家安全部"; also carried by China News Service and on the People's Daily account platform). After three cases (an engineer who leaked a semiconductor firm's process secrets, data theft from an e-commerce platform, and rare-earth secrets sold abroad), it asks for strict hardware management: tight admission of chips, servers and industrial control equipment, security review and tracing of suppliers, so that no one can plant eavesdropping devices or spy firmware. It names no maker and no country (英伟达 0 and 美国 0 in the article; control: 芯片 found), speaks to "the whole of society, especially practitioners in the industries concerned", and sets no rule on buying. Words: "国家安全部:产业链供应链上谍影重重,关键处不能“掉链子”" [cn-ga31-mssposts-5]; "来源: 国家安全部" [cn-ga31-mssposts-5]; "严控芯片、服务器、工控设备等关键硬件准入,实施供应商安全审查与溯源管理" [cn-ga31-mssposts-5] (strictly control the admission of key hardware such as chips, servers and industrial control equipment, and carry out security review and tracing of suppliers); "防范别有用心之人在硬件中植入窃密装置、预置间谍固件" [cn-ga31-mssposts-5]; "全社会特别是相关行业从业者务必提高警惕" [cn-ga31-mssposts-5]; China News Service's copy: "严控芯片、服务器、工控设备等关键硬件准入" [cn-ga31-mssposts-7]. Cuts: CONTEXT; FOR weakly (the security side tells industry to vet chip suppliers), not a rule about Nvidia or about AI chips.
- **Dec 19, 2025 to Nov 18, 2026 · The customs administration in its own words on chip imports: facilitation and totals, nothing on Nvidia** (an official's answers in the State Council Information Office's transcript; a Xinhua report of a customs press conference; a joint notice; a customs district's figures as a newspaper reports them; a customs penalty decision as a trade site reprints it). At the briefing of Oct 14, 2026, deputy head Wang Jun gave integrated-circuit imports for January to September 2026 as up 56.6%; the transcript has 英伟达, H20, H200 and 芯片 0 times (control: 集成电路 3). On Jul 29, 2026 the head of customs' risk-management department, Lin Tengxiao, described moving the inspection of eligible vacuum-packed and other high-tech goods from the ports to premises that qualify, and port handling cut from 3 to 4 days to as little as the same day. The general offices of the commerce ministry and customs opened a pilot for bonded testing of integrated circuits outside the comprehensive bonded zones (notice of Jun 25, posted Jul 2). Shenzhen customs, as a reporter relays it, put the city's integrated-circuit imports for January to October 2026 at 1.08 trillion yuan, up 62.8%. A Ningbo district customs decision records 1,460 used graphics cards declared under 8471800000 (for two of the cards the decision gives the brand AISURIX and the specification RX580 8GB; it names no maker). None of these names Nvidia, the H20 or the H200 (the Ningbo decision gives a card specification, RX580 8GB, for two cards), and none speaks of checks on chip shipments, beyond the Ningbo decision's inspection of the declared used graphics cards, which found two of them to be solid waste. Words: "国务院新闻办公室于2026年10月14日(星期三)上午10时举行新闻发布会,请海关总署新闻发言人、副署长王军" [cn-ga31-customs-3]; "前三季度,我国进口存储部件增长1.5倍,集成电路增长56.6%" [cn-ga31-customs-3]; "海关总署风险管理司司长林腾霄介绍说" [cn-ga31-customs-5]; "原本需要3至4天的口岸作业时间最短压缩为当日办结" [cn-ga31-customs-5]; "【发布文号】商办服贸函〔2026〕355号 【发文日期】2026年06月25日" [cn-ga31-customs-1]; "可试点开展集成电路和消费电子产品的保税检测业务" [cn-ga31-customs-1]; "记者从深圳海关获悉" [cn-ga31-livecn-71]; "进口集成电路1.08万亿元,增长62.8%" [cn-ga31-livecn-71]; "申报商品编号为8471800000,申报数量为1460台" [cn-ga31-customs-9]. The integrated-circuit line is a total not split by maker; it cannot count Nvidia's. Cuts: CONTEXT (customs speaks publicly of easing and growing chip imports; the press reports of port checks, Oct 2025, and of the H200 "not permitted", Jan 2026, have no counterpart in customs' own words found).
- **Dec 30, 2022 text and Dec 27, 2025 revision · Foreign Trade Law: restricting goods that no catalogue lists** (a law: the text as amended on Dec 30, 2022, WIPO Lex's copy, in force through 2024 and 2025; and the revision in force from Mar 1, 2026, on the commerce ministry's site). Both let the State Council's department in charge of foreign trade, alone or with other departments and with the State Council's approval, decide for a time to restrict or ban particular goods outside the catalogues, within the grounds the law lists; national security is one, and building a particular domestic industry is another, in the 2022 text "为建立或者加快建立国内特定产业，需要限制进口的" (where imports must be restricted to build, or speed the building of, a particular domestic industry) [cn-ga31-intf-ftl-2022-wipo] and in the revision "为建立或者加快建立国内特定产业的" [cn-ga31-importcat-22]. Words: the 2022 text, "2022年12月30日第十三届全国人民代表大会常务委员会第三十八次会议" [cn-ga31-intf-ftl-2022-wipo], article 17: "国务院对外贸易主管部门或者由其会同国务院其他有关部门，经国务院批准，可以在本法第十五条和第十六条规定的范围内，临时决定限制或者禁止前款规定目录以外的特定货物、技术的进口或者出口" [cn-ga31-intf-ftl-2022-wipo]; the revision, "自2026年3月1日起施行" [cn-ga31-importcat-22], article 20: "国务院对外贸易主管部门或者由其会同国务院其他有关部门，经国务院批准，可以在本法第十八条和第十九条规定的范围内，临时决定禁止或者限制前款规定目录以外的特定货物、技术的进出口" [cn-ga31-importcat-22]. Neither article says how such a decision is made known; the regulations on goods import and export were not read. No decision under these articles naming these chips was found. So a restriction on importing these chips could exist in law without standing in any catalogue (section 8); only the press reports one (Tier 2). Cuts: CONTEXT (bounds limit 2: «no permit rule was found» is a statement about the published lists, not about every lawful route).
- **2023 and Jan 1, 2026 · Compulsory certification of servers, and the customs declaration of where an integrated circuit's wafer was made** (documents, one through a relay). The compulsory product certification covers servers "适用于额定电流小于等于 6A 的服务器" [cn-ga31-importcat-37] (item 45 of the certification administration's 2023 table; its announcement number is not on the saved annex), and Nvidia's own guide to its DGX H100 and H200 says "No certification is needed for China. The NVIDIA DGX H100/H200 system is a server with power consumption greater than 1.3 kW." [cn-ga31-importcat-35] (a page marked last updated on Oct 17, 2026; the company's word, not the regulator's). From Jan 1, 2026 the customs declaration catalogue asks, for integrated circuits, where the wafer was made: "税目85.42“集成电路”，新增“晶圆制造所在国家（地区），通常为‘流片地’”申报要素" [cn-ga31-importcat-64] (Tianjin Customs' guidance as the trade council's enterprise-service platform carries it; the declaration catalogue itself was not saved). Neither is a permit rule; the second lets customs see where a chip's wafer was made. Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Jun 10, 2026 · Shanghai Customs' enquiry service (12360), an explainer on classifying integrated circuits, as a city trade-promotion committee reprints it: the one customs text found that names Nvidia, and it names it as an ordinary example** (a customs district's public explainer, held through the Tai'an trade-promotion committee's site, which credits Shanghai Customs 12360; the original post was not found). Under processors and controllers of heading 8542 it lists, among foreign brands, one Nvidia part number beside parts of Cirrus Logic, ST and Microchip; a trade site's article of April 2025 gives the same part number as the subject of an advance classification ruling of 2024, as a CPU superchip under 85423119 (the name says CPU, not graphics processor; the ruling itself was not reached). The explainer is about tariff classification and praises home-made chips; it sets no licence, check or limit. Words: "一文读懂集成电路海关归类" [cn-ga31-customstext-48]; "来源：上海海关12360" [cn-ga31-customstext-48]; "日期：2026-06-10" [cn-ga31-customstext-48]; "海关税则归类，是芯片进出口的重要依据" [cn-ga31-customstext-48]; "国外品牌：CIRRUS LOGIC（CS42L77-XWZR/A1）、NVIDIA（6042B0934301）" [cn-ga31-customstext-48]; the trade site: "归类预裁定相关编号：R-2-2200-2024-0164规格型号：6042B0934301，中文名称：CPU超级芯片" [cn-ga31-customstext-51]. Cuts: CONTEXT (customs classifies an Nvidia part as it does any chip; no published customs text treats Nvidia's goods apart).
- **Jun 27, 2026 (the day of the import declaration) · Liantang Customs (Shenzhen), a penalty for used graphics-card modules imported without the used-goods inspection** (a customs penalty notice as a customs-affairs site reprints it; the notice on Shenzhen Customs' own site timed out to the search). A second case beside the Ningbo decision already held [cn-ga31-customs-9]. An importer declared «graphics-card modules» by ordinary trade at the Liantang crossing; on examination three lines showed clear signs of use and had not been declared as used electromechanical goods, which are inspected on import; the goods were valued at about 5.69 million yuan. The passage names no maker and no model, and the saved reprint gives the fine, "罚款人民币 59.7287 万元" [cn-ga31-customs-2] (597,287 yuan), and the decision's number, "深莲关检罚字〔 2026 〕 186 号" [cn-ga31-customs-2]. The rule broken is the inspection of used goods (the 2015 schedule lists used computers and integrated circuits; People's Daily's overseas edition printed customs' guide to it on Apr 6, 2024, "进口旧机电设备通关指南（海关答疑）" [cn-ga31-customstext-117]), not a rule on new chips. Cuts: CONTEXT (the inspection requirement found that reaches graphics cards is the general one for used goods; nothing on Nvidia or on new AI chips).
