## 5. Chinese official documents on the other side

- **The open-door clause.** Xi, Apr 19, 2016, with his condition: "只要有利于提高我国社会生产力水平、有利于改善人民生活，我们都不拒绝" [cn-xi-20160419] (so long as it helps to raise the level of our country's social productive forces and to improve people's lives, we refuse none of it), "我们不拒绝任何新技术" [cn-xi-20160419]; and in the same speech, "市场换不来核心技术，有钱也买不来核心技术" [cn-xi-20160419] (core technology cannot be had for market access, nor bought with money); in the same speech, "我们强调自主创新,不是关起门来搞研发,一定要坚持开放创新" (when we stress independent innovation we do not mean research behind closed doors; we must hold to open innovation), and of the kinds of technology, "哪些是可以引进但必须安全可控的" (which can be brought in but must be secure and controllable) [cn-policy-xi-coretech-2016]: imported technology under security control is inside his stated line. To the Dutch prime minister, Mar 27, 2024: "“脱钩断链”没有出路,开放合作是唯一选择" ("decoupling and breaking chains" leads nowhere; open cooperation is the only choice) [cn-xi-rutte2024]; Jun 24, 2024: "在开放合作中实现自立自强" (achieve self-reliance and self-strengthening through open cooperation) [cn-xi-texts-b-3]. The cyberspace regulator, in its notice of the Micron decision, with its condition: "只要遵守中国法律法规要求，欢迎各国企业、各类平台产品服务进入中国市场" (so long as they comply with the requirements of China's laws and regulations, companies of all countries and platform products and services of all kinds are welcome in the Chinese market) [cn-policy-micron-decision]. The 2014 outline: "充分利用全球资源" (make full use of global resources) [cn-policy-2014-outline].
- **May 14, 2026 · Xi Jinping to American business leaders; and Premier Li Qiang the same day, Nvidia among those present (Xinhua)** (named officials on the record). Xi: China's open door will only open wider and it welcomes more mutually beneficial cooperation with the United States; Li: a stable, open policy environment is the Chinese government's lasting commitment. Words: "中国开放的大门只会越开越大，中方欢迎美国对华加强互利合作" [cn-cnpress-xi-us-business-20260514]; "一个稳定开放的政策环境是中国政府长期不变的承诺" [cn-xi-round-f-6]; "苹果公司、英伟达公司" [cn-xi-round-f-6]. Neither names chips. Cuts: AGAINST (the welcoming side; limit 1).
- **May 14, Jun 24, Sept 24 and Nov 18, 2026 · Xi and Li Qiang to and about foreign business** (named leaders on the record). Words: May 14, with the US executives who came with the President (the readout names none of them; the President's post of May 12 has Huang travelling with them [cn-le22-tt-38445-huang-20260512]): "特朗普逐一向习近平介绍随访企业家" [cn-acts-xi-us-business-20260514]; "中方欢迎美国对华加强互利合作，相信美国企业在华将拥有更广阔前景" [cn-acts-xi-us-business-20260514]; Sept 24, Washington: "中国开放的大门始终敞开，欢迎美国企业来华投资兴业，希望美方公平对待中国企业" [cn-le22-jhsjk-xi-wh-welcome-20260924]; Nov 18, APEC business leaders: "扩大优质产品和服务进口" [cn-xi-apec-ceo2026]; Li Qiang, Jun 24: "全面落实外资企业国民待遇" [cn-le22-mfa-li-davos-business-20260624]. Jul 17, 2026, at the AI conference (names no country; CONTEXT): "应当共同反对在人工智能领域泛化国家安全概念、把本国安全凌驾于他国安全之上的做法" [cn-xi-waic-20260717]. Cuts: AGAINST (the welcoming side; limit 1).
- **Mar 28, 2025 · Xi Jinping to representatives of international business, Beijing (his speech; People's Daily, Mar 29, 2025, p. 2)** (the leader on the record). China will see that products foreign-funded firms make in China take part equally, by law, in government purchasing. Words: "保障外资企业在华生产的产品依法平等参与政府采购" [cn-ga03-jhsjk-xi-intl-business-20250328]; before it, "对于在中国的外资企业，理应保障其国民待遇" [cn-ga03-jhsjk-xi-intl-business-20250328] (foreign-funded firms in China should be assured national treatment). The words cover products made in China, not imported chips. Cuts: AGAINST, with that limit.
- **May 8, 2025 · Joint statement of the People's Republic of China and the Russian Federation, dated Moscow and bearing the names of Xi Jinping and Vladimir Putin (Xinhua)** (a joint statement of the two governments, not a speech of Xi's). The two sides oppose politicising technology and the harmful practice of maliciously damaging the stability of the international AI industry and supply chains, and firmly oppose the abuse of tariffs and of export controls. Words: "双方反对将科技问题政治化，反对恶意破坏人工智能国际产业链供应链稳定的消极做法。" [cn-ga03-xh-cn-ru-joint-statement-20250508]; "双方坚决反对滥施关税和滥用出口管制" [cn-ga03-xh-cn-ru-joint-statement-20250508]; "二〇二五年五月八日于莫斯科" [cn-ga03-xh-cn-ru-joint-statement-20250508]. The passages quoted name no country and no firm. Cuts: CONTEXT (Beijing's stated line against the abuse of export controls).
- **Apr 30 and Nov 6 to 7, 2026 · He Lifeng's calls with the US Treasury Secretary and Trade Representative; Sept 26, 2026, Wang Yi** (official readouts). Words: "中方就近期美对华经贸限制措施表达严正关切" [cn-le21-cn-call-20260430-xinhua]; Wang Yi: "包括建立并推进贸易理事会和投资理事会、设立农业工作组、“300亿美元”对等降税、延期吉隆坡经贸磋商成果等" [cn-le22-mfa-wangyi-briefing-20260926]. Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Jul 18, 2025 · Xinhua, economic essay** (published commentary). Words: "坚定扩大开放、欢迎外资，中国一直态度鲜明、行动坚定" [cn-media-xinhua-welcome-essay]. Cuts: AGAINST.
- **Jul 22, 2025 · People's Daily Overseas Edition, page 9, interview with Zhang Xin, director of a research centre at the University of International Business and Economics, as carried by Guangming** (a scholar's advice printed in a Party paper; not the paper's own advice). Her strategy is two lines at once: actively apply for and stock imported high-end chips, and fill the gap with algorithms and home-made ecosystems; downstream AI firms should for the short term work with home-made chip makers; a cut-off at any time is to be the normal assumption for the next two years; during the three-month ban some AI projects were forced to be postponed. Words: "人民日报记者采访了对外经济贸易大学数字经济与法律创新研究中心主任张欣" [cn-media-pd-h20-gmw]; "国内企业的最优策略是“双线并行”" [cn-media-pd-h20-gmw]; "一边积极申请、储备进口高端芯片" [cn-media-pd-h20-gmw]; "另一边用算法创新和国产生态把算力缺口补回来" [cn-media-pd-h20-gmw]; "下游人工智能企业的短期策略，应聚焦与国产芯片厂商的合作，减少单一来源依赖" [cn-media-pd-h20-gmw]; "“随时断供”是今后两年的常态假设" [cn-media-pd-h20-gmw]; "3个月禁售期里，一些人工智能项目被迫延期" [cn-media-pd-h20-gmw]; "来源: 人民日报海外版 2025-07-22" [cn-media-pd-h20-gmw]. It is not «the opposite advice», and it falls about the day the summer's guidance began by the Financial Times's account. Cuts: AGAINST (a Party paper printed advice to import), with those limits.
- **Aug 17, 2025 · Xinhua commentary by Zeng Yan, as carried by China Daily** (published commentary). Words: "It has shown readiness to resume orderly imports, address genuine security issues through dialogue" [cn-media-xinhua-imports-cd]. Cuts: AGAINST, mixed.
- **Jan 7, 2026 (Global Times English, 21:46) and Jan 8 (Huanqiu) · Wei Shaojun, vice chairman of the China Semiconductor Industry Association** (a named person in state media). Importing is to catch up better; moderate imports can ease demand in some uses in the short term; compliant interaction is welcome; it will not shake the resolve to innovate independently. Words: "引进是为了更好地追赶，追赶终将走向并跑乃至领跑" [cn-media-huanqiu-wei-h200]; "适度引入先进算力产品，可以在短期内缓解部分应用场景的需求压力" [cn-media-huanqiu-wei-h200]; "只要符合双方监管要求，任何有利于技术创新和产业发展的良性互动都值得欢迎。" [cn-media-huanqiu-wei-h200]; "但即便引入英伟达芯片，也不会动摇我们坚持自主创新的决心" [cn-media-huanqiu-wei-h200]. The House Select Committee quotes him ("The purpose of importing is to catch up better" [cn-le03-govinfo-buywhat-pdf]). Cuts: AGAINST, mixed.
- **Mar 13, 2026 · Xinhua, 新华时评** (published commentary). It lists the Nvidia probe beside cases against Alibaba, Meituan, Google and Ctrip and says the series of measures sends a clear signal of treating all kinds of companies alike. Words: "更向外界传递出坚持对各类企业一视同仁的清晰信号" [cn-media-xinhua-antitrust-equal]. Cuts: AGAINST reading the case as aimed at Nvidia's sales.
- **A standing legal duty on Nvidia to keep supplying China.** The market regulator's 2020 decision on the Mellanox purchase, the decision Nvidia is now accused of breaking, obliges it to "依据公平、合理、无歧视原则向中国市场继续供应英伟达GPU加速器、迈络思高速网络互联设备和相关软件、配件" (continue to supply Nvidia GPU accelerators, Mellanox high-speed networking equipment and related software and accessories to the Chinese market on fair, reasonable and non-discriminatory terms) [cn-policy-mellanox-conditions]. The conditions do not lapse by themselves: "未经解除,交易双方和集中后实体应继续履行限制性条件" (until lifted, the parties and the merged entity shall go on performing the restrictive conditions); the decision is dated Apr 16, 2020, and an application to lift them is allowed after six years, which by the project's arithmetic fell in April 2026. Whether Nvidia applied is not in the pages read. And the commerce ministry's Unreliable Entity List rules (2020) allow measures against a foreign entity that "违反正常的市场交易原则，中断与中国企业、其他组织或者个人的正常交易" (against normal market-trading principles, breaks off normal transactions with Chinese enterprises, other organisations or individuals) [cn-policy-uel], where that "严重损害中国企业、其他组织或者个人合法权益" (seriously harms the lawful rights and interests of Chinese enterprises, other organisations or individuals) [cn-policy-uel]. On paper, these two texts provide for action against withholding supply, the first against Nvidia for the products it names and the second against a foreign entity on the conditions quoted; neither makes supplying itself a breach.
- **The 2026 supply-chain security regulation holds both lines.** Article 4: "提升产业链供应链安全可控水平" (raise the level of security and controllability of industrial and supply chains); article 2: "推进高水平对外开放,促进全球产业链供应链稳定畅通" (advance high-level opening up and promote stable and unobstructed global industrial and supply chains) [cn-factions-statecouncil-supplychain].
- **Ministers welcoming Nvidia.** Commerce Minister Wang Wentao to Jensen Huang, Jul 17, 2025: "希望包括英伟达在内的跨国公司为中国客户提供优质可靠的产品和服务" (we hope multinationals including Nvidia will provide Chinese customers with quality, reliable products and services) [cn-policy-wang-nvidia]. Vice Premier He Lifeng, Apr 17, 2025: "欢迎包括英伟达公司在内的更多美资企业深耕中国市场" (more American companies, Nvidia included, are welcome to work the Chinese market in depth) [cn-policy-he-welcomes].
- **The commerce ministry against US controls.** Jul 18, 2025: "美方近日又主动表示将批准对华销售英伟达H20芯片" (the US side has recently also, on its own initiative, said it will approve sales of Nvidia's H20 chips to China) [cn-factions-mofcom-us-initiative-20250718]; it goes on, "美方应摒弃零和思维，继续取消一系列不合理的对华经贸限制措施" (the US should drop zero-sum thinking and go on removing a series of unreasonable economic and trade restrictions on China) [cn-policy-h20-mofcom]. The ministry's published answer to the H20 approval has no word against buying the chip; the cyberspace regulator's summons came thirteen days later. Nov 17, 2026, on the US remote-access rule: "若美方一意孤行,中方将采取反制措施" (if the US persists, China will take countermeasures) [cn-acts-mofcom-20261117].
- **Nov 13 and 17, 2026 · Commerce and foreign ministries on the US remote-access rule and the moves before it** (official spokespersons). They call Chinese firms' cooperation with other countries' firms normal trade and threaten countermeasures. Words: "美方泛化国家安全概念，滥用出口管制，对中国企业与其他国家企业正常贸易合作横加干涉" [cn-acts-mofcom-20261117] (the US stretches the concept of national security, abuses export controls and wantonly interferes in normal trade cooperation between Chinese firms and other countries' firms); Guo Jiakun, Nov 17: "甚至横加干涉中国同其他国家开展正常贸易与科技合作，无理打压中国高新技术产业发展" [cn-acts-mfa-20261117]; "中方将采取必要措施坚决维护自身正当权益" [cn-acts-mfa-20261117]; Mao Ning, Nov 13, asked about moves to stop Chinese firms getting US computing remotely from abroad: "中方一贯反对滥用出口管制措施，扰乱全球产供链稳定，这不符合任何一方利益" [cn-clips-primary-4]. No Chinese statement found tells companies not to train abroad (section 8). Cuts: AGAINST (on computing abroad Beijing's commerce and foreign side backed the companies; limit 1).
- **The purchase rules are narrow by their own words.** In each of the three 2023 notices the same sentence adds "其他单位可不在采购需求中提出此项要求" [cn-policy-desktop-2023] (other units may leave this requirement out); and for indicators not starred, buyers decide for themselves: "对于未加“*”的指标，采购人可以根据实际需要自行确定是否纳入采购需求" [cn-policy-desktop-2023]. The 2025 notice on the 20% preference: "国有企业、民营企业、外资企业等各类经营主体平等享受对本国产品的政府采购支持政策" (state-owned, private, foreign-funded and all other kinds of business enjoy on equal terms the government-procurement support for home-made products), and suppliers may not be treated differently by "所有制形式、组织形式、股权结构、投资者国别" (form of ownership, form of organisation, shareholding structure, investors' country) [cn-policy-domestic-product-standard-2025]. A product made in China by a foreign-funded firm gets the preference; a chip made abroad does not on its own, whoever designed it (the project's reading of the two clauses together).
- **Dec 15, 2025 · Ministry of Finance and Ministry of Industry and Information Technology, Caiku [2025] No. 30** (a document). It puts the home-made product standard into effect in government purchasing from Jan 1, 2026, and protects equal access for foreign-funded firms' products that meet it. Words: "确保于2026年1月1日起在政府采购中落实政策要求" [cn-le14-mof-domestic-standard-impl-20251215]; "保障符合本国产品标准的外资企业产品平等参与政府采购活动" [cn-le14-mof-domestic-standard-impl-20251215]; "财库〔2025〕30号" [cn-le14-mof-domestic-standard-impl-20251215]. Cuts: AGAINST/CONTEXT.
- **Oct 2025 · State planner, measures for central-budget money for energy saving (发改环资规〔2025〕1228号)** (a document). It funds energy-saving retrofits of computing infrastructure and treats all investors equally; it sets no chip condition (国产 0 places). Words: "支持供热、算力等基础设施节能降碳改造" [cn-le14-ndrc-energy-invest-measures-1228-pdf]; "平等对待各类投资主体" [cn-le14-ndrc-energy-invest-measures-1228-pdf]. No published counterpart was found of the reported rule that state-funded data centres use only home-made chips (section 8). Cuts: AGAINST a published rule.
- **National documents that promote chip development also invite the outside in.** State Council "AI Plus" opinions, Aug 2025: "强化算力、数据、人才等领域国际合作" (strengthen international cooperation in computing power, data, talent and other fields) [cn-policy-ai-plus]. The eight-body manufacturing opinions, Dec 25, 2025: "引导外资投向人工智能领域，鼓励外商投资企业开展生成式人工智能技术开发及产品生产等" (guide foreign capital toward AI, and encourage foreign-invested enterprises to develop generative AI technology, make products and the like) [cn-policy-ai-manufacturing-279-wayback]. The State Council's chip and software policies of 2020 apply "不分所有制性质" (whatever the form of ownership) and encourage "国际企业在华建设研发中心" (international companies to build research and development centres in China) [cn-policy-2020-no8]. The 2014 outline: "鼓励国际集成电路企业在国内建设研发、生产和运营中心" (international integrated-circuit companies are encouraged to build research and development, production and operating centres in China) [cn-policy-2014-outline].
- **A roadmap is not an instruction.** Vice Minister Xin Guobin at the launch of the Made in China 2025 technology roadmap, Sept 29, 2015: "路线图不是指令企业怎么做,而是指导性的、参考性的" (the roadmap does not instruct companies what to do; it is for guidance and reference) [cn-policy-roadmap-status, a local office of the ministry carrying a report of the event].
- **Help for computing abroad.** Commerce ministry, on its proposed technology-export list (Oct 2026): for "中国企业在境外自建自营算力中心" (computing centres that Chinese companies build and run abroad) and similar cases, "适用自动许可等便利措施,基本实现“即报即出”" (automatic licensing and other facilitation apply: export on filing, in substance) [cn-tolerate-mof].
- **Oct 16, 2026 · Commerce and science ministries, announcement 2026 No. 48 (a draft for comment, Oct 16 to Nov 15, and a registration before export of the technologies it lists, required from the announcement until the revised catalogue takes effect), and the spokesperson's answers** (documents). The draft adds 23 AI technologies to the catalogue of technologies whose export is prohibited or restricted, and the spokesperson names one AI technology among those to be prohibited, "拟禁止出口技术14项，包括：人工智能驱动的恶意代码生成与变异技术" [cn-tolerate-mof] (14 technologies to be prohibited, among them AI-driven generation and mutation of malicious code); local deployment abroad of a restricted model is a technology export, but on a firm's own cloud abroad automatic licensing may apply; API, app and SaaS service abroad is outside the scope. Words: "此次拟将人工智能大模型、智能体、算力集群、信息处理相关23项关键技术增列至《目录》" [cn-tolerate-mof]; "对于纳入限制类的人工智能技术，开展海外本地化部署，属于技术出口管理范围；如果部署在海外自有云等风险相对可控的情形下，可适用自动许可等便利措施" [cn-tolerate-mof]; "公众可在2026年10月16日-11月15日期间" [cn-tolerate-mof48]. It eases computing centres that Chinese firms build and run themselves abroad and says nothing in so many words about training abroad. (The annex's own words are in section 9.) Cuts: AGAINST (eases computing abroad), and FOR in part (a Chinese licence where a top model is deployed abroad outside a firm's own cloud).
- **Mar 25, 2025 · Shanghai Commission of Economy and Informatization; and the national computing action plan (undated copy)** (documents). Both encourage computing abroad, without naming chips. Words: "支持智算云企业探索海外智算基础设施布局、形成算力部署，与国内算力形成集群联动" [cn-factions-shanghai-cloud-20250324]; "支持我国企业“走出去”，以“一带一路”沿线国家为重点布局海外算力设施，提升全球化服务能力" [cn-policy-compute-plan-pdf]. Cuts: AGAINST, weakly.
- **Subsidies that include foreign chips.** The computing vouchers of Beijing's Economic-Technological Development Area (a development zone's policy of 2024, on the city government's site; corrected Nov 23, 2026: this file had called them Beijing's) offset, under its rule of Mar 28, 2024 (京技管发〔2024〕10号), up to "30%（非国产人工智能算力）或40%（国产人工智能算力）" [cn-factions-etwon-vouchers]; its rule of Nov 24, 2024 (京技管发〔2024〕29号) repealed that rule, "原政策（京技管发〔2024〕10号）同时废止。" [cn-le28-etown-29hao-20241126], and sets one voucher rate with no chip condition, "按照实际使用算力结算费用的30%" [cn-le28-etown-29hao-20241126], keeping a separate subsidy for cloud firms that buy servers with home-made AI chips and offer the service on the zone's computing platform. See the voucher table below. Shanghai's 2026 application templates use A100 and H100 rentals as the example in their sample audit report [cn-tolerate-shtemplate].
- **Local computing vouchers that pay for foreign chips too (2024 to 2026)** (documents unless marked). Every voucher rule found with a home-made tier still pays for foreign computing, and four rules found pay for home-made computing only: Huangyan district's policy of Jul 16, 2025 (黄政发〔2025〕18号), which words the favoured kind 自主 (independent), as the seven that pay more word theirs 自主 or 自主可控, "企业采购自主智能算力服务的，其研发投入（委外除外）中的算力成本部分，按照智算服务实际采购金额的50%给予算力补贴" [cn-ga31-vcityeast-55]; the Changsha Economic and Technological Development Zone's measures of Mar 23, 2026, which pay 10% of what firms spend renting home-made intelligent computing for agent applications, "对租用国产智能算力" [cn-ga31-vcitywest-6]; Ningbo's yearly rules, those for 2026 signed Mar 16, 2026, with the fourth voucher period ending Dec 31, 2026, "合同服务内容需明确为国产算力服务" [cn-ga31-voucherrulesmissing-71], read on a commercial policy platform's page that carries the notice whole, and Sichuan's measures of 2026 (川发改数据〔2026〕338号, written Aug 11 and published Aug 13, 2026, on the provincial commission's own page as the Internet Archive captured it [cn-ga31-unsaved-sc-338hao-fgw-wayback]; the news site Jiemian carries the same text without number or date), "对企业购买国产算力费用给予支持" [cn-ga05-sichuan-compute-net-measures-jiemian]; the premium for home-made is up to 60% against up to 30% in Binjiang, whose rule of May 2025 by its bureau's account raised the home-made rate by ten points and began paying for other computing [cn-cnpress-binjiang-rental-reply-20260720], and up to 30% against up to 10% in Wenzhou, while Hangzhou's plan of 2025 repealed its measures of 2024 from Jul 15, 2025 and pays 30% or 20% "按照算力设施的不同类型" (by the type of computing facility), no longer naming home-made computing [cn-ga31-voucherrulesmissing-40]. A second search (33 rules in 13 more places) found every voucher rule with rates paying for any chip, Zhejiang's the only one with a rate tier by chip; Beijing E-Town's rule No. 29 of Nov 2024 repealed No. 10 and its home-made premium; the home-made-only money it found is separate from the vouchers (purchase and migration subsidies, a building priority, a preference in buying). A third search, of Nov 25, 2026, across the 31 province-level governments and 24 named cities and new areas (districts and other cities only as they turned up), brought the rules found and counted to 38; a fourth round of the same day (four searches that went city by city, a search for the earlier versions of the rules that favour home-made computing, and readings of named rules) and a fifth (the second passes of those searches, and the rulings on the fourth round's judgment calls) brought them to 90, the rules found by searches that went province by province and city by city, saved and read, not a census of every local government, one rule in force for each government: 7 pay more for home-made computing and still pay for other computing (Binjiang, Chengdu, Hubei, Longwan and Wenzhou Bay New Area, Wenzhou, Wuhan and Zhejiang, whose rules word the favoured kind 自主 or 自主可控, independent or independently controllable), 4 pay for home-made computing only (the Changsha Economic and Technological Development Zone, Huangyan, Ningbo and Sichuan), and 79 state no higher rate for home-made computing, 78 of them paying one rate whatever the chip and one, Hangzhou's, 30% or 20% by a type of computing facility that it does not name. Two of the 78 name home-made computing in the rate sentence only at that one rate (Binhaiwan New Area, for adapting to home-made systems and chips; Haidian, as an encouragement), and Hebei district of Tianjin pays, by its words, for one named centre, which Tianjin Daily calls wholly home-made. Shenyang's rule, counted at one rate, names no origin, supplier, centre or list; Liaoning's data bureau describes the vouchers of Shenyang and Dalian in three replies of May to July 2026, in four sentences that do not agree: for local users, "支持本地用户使用算力资源", and, in the same reply, "对使用本地国产算力资源的用户" [cn-ga31-vouchernamedrules-23] (users of local home-made computing); for "省内智能算力资源" [cn-ga31-dcstateeast-36] (intelligent computing inside the province); and "重点支持使用本省国产算力资源" [cn-ga31-sciowhole-132] (mainly supporting the use of the province's home-made computing). Only the second reads as a limit, and the implementing policy the June reply speaks of, "算力券管理实施政策" [cn-ga31-dcstateeast-36], was searched for on Nov 25, 2026 and not found. What is counted is a rule that pays a share of a firm's bill for rented or bought computing: most call the payment a computing voucher (算力券) or a like name, and 38 give it another name, a subsidy, a reward or support (computed from the tables' kind column; the list stands in tables/local_vouchers_classes.tsv). Of the 90, 50 are read on an official page or file of the issuing government or body and 40 in another copy (another body's site, a news or commercial site, or an Internet Archive capture). Beijing city's counted rule, the innovation-voucher measures of its science commission (京科发〔2026〕9号), pays up to 25% of what is spent under a contract and requires in its article 17 that a computing supplier be able to serve on home-made chips with a home-made software stack, "可基于国产芯片并使用国产软件栈提供算力服务" [cn-ga31-voucherrulesmissing-4]: a condition on the supplier, not a higher rate and not a rule that only such service is paid. Jiangsu's 50% is in principle a ceiling for province and city together, inside which each city divided into districts (设区市) may set its own rate, "各设区市可在该上限范围内，结合本地实际确定具体支持比例" [cn-ga31-voucherrulesmissing-11]. Counted other ways (counted / paying more for home-made / home-made only / no higher rate for home-made): with Hangzhou's two tiers read as home-made and other, as its repealed rule had them, 90 / 8 / 4 / 78, and with Hangzhou left out, 89 / 7 / 4 / 78; without the rules of the searches of Nov 25, 2026 that give the payment another name, 57 / 7 / 4 / 46; with the judgment calls the script names taken the narrow way, 53 / 7 / 3 / 43; with Haidian's rule read as paying for home-made computing only, 90 / 7 / 5 / 78; without every city, district and zone rule of the searches that went city by city, 40 / 6 / 2 / 32 (each line computed by the classifier). A second fact about the same rules leaves every class as it is, since a class looks only at the payment counted: of the 79 that state no higher rate for home-made computing, 9 also give home-made computing something by another provision of the same rule, 6 money to build, buy or run home-made computing facilities or equipment (Shanghai [cn-le15-sheitc-8cea38], Beijing E-Town [cn-le28-etown-29hao-20241126], the Lingang Special Area [cn-ga31-voucherrulesmissing-60], Songjiang district [cn-ga31-vcityeast-39], Fuzhou [cn-ga31-vcitysouth-41] and Hebei district of Tianjin [cn-ga31-vcitynorth-10]), 3 an award for adapting to, or building on, home-made chips or computing (Shenzhen [cn-le28-sz-gxj-2024-13hao], Guizhou [cn-ga31-plantexts-13] and Hebei district of Tianjin), and 1 a condition that its computing suppliers be able to serve on home-made chips (Beijing city, by its article 17); taking the two calls the narrow way (Beijing city's condition, and Hebei district's award for research built on Ascend and other independently controllable routes), 8 of the 79; none of the 7 that pay more for home-made computing and none of the 4 that pay for it only gives anything by another provision (computed from tables/local_vouchers_other_provisions.tsv, one row per provision with its own words). That is a premium for home-made computing beside the payment; the payment itself stays open to other computing in those 79. The class of every row, and why a row is not counted, is in tables/local_vouchers_classes.tsv. Table in full: tables/local_vouchers.tsv (20 rows; plus Zhejiang from a later reading) and tables/local_vouchers_more.tsv (217 rows: 12 added on Nov 24, 2026, rows B34 to B45, with the one-rate rules of Wuhu, Suzhou and Tianjin among them; row B46 and then rows B47 to B102, 56 rows, added on Nov 25, 2026, and rows B103 to B216, 114 more, added by later rounds of the same day, and row B217, Dalian's draft AI measures of Apr 2025, read and not counted; of the first 33, 30 below, from «Beijing E-Town (a development zone), rule signed Nov 24, 2024» on; the other 3 are already in this file). Cuts: both: AGAINST (foreign computing still paid), FOR (a premium for home-made).

- **Hong Kong's government pays for the use of Nvidia chips.** (Hong Kong is a special administrative region with its own government and customs territory; this is not a mainland document.) Cyberport's release of Jul 4, 2025: "the HKSAR Government allocated HK$3 billion to launch the" three-year "Artificial Intelligence Subsidy Scheme (AISS) through Cyberport"; the scheme committee's chairman: "The nation is steadily advancing the 'AI+' initiative, and the HKSAR Government is fully committed to aligning with the National Development Strategy by allocating HK$3 billion through Cyberport to launch the AISS"; "the Committee has approved around 10 projects ... with a total subsidy amount of approximately HK$300 million" [cn-tolerate-hkaissawards-wayback, an Internet Archive capture of Jul 12, 2025]. The scheme's technical specifications (v2/2025) list what is subsidised: "Nvidia H800 Tensor Core GPU (8 cards per node)", "One Nvidia H800 Tensor Core GPU Card HK$40,000 per month" [cn-tolerate-hkaisspec-wayback, capture of Aug 13, 2026]. Reuters describes the H800 as one of the chips "Nvidia then developed for the Chinese market but which were also banned last October" (2023) [cn-labs-state-tenders-2024-wayback]. What the Hong Kong scheme does not show: anything about what mainland bodies may buy.
- **2004 to 2026 · Hong Kong Trade and Industry Department, its strategic-trade pages and circulars** (documents of the Hong Kong government, a separate customs territory; found by a search of Nov 25, 2026, read whole). Goods on Schedule 1 of the strategic-commodities regulations need a licence to be imported, exported or transshipped, with a conditional exemption for some air-to-air cargo; a carrier must hold imported strategic goods until an import licence is produced; goods consigned on a through bill of lading or air waybill from one place outside Hong Kong to another still count as transhipment cargo when landed and stored in Hong Kong pending export, by the definition the department's page gives; for a licence the department may ask for an export permit from the country or place where the technology originates or from which the goods are exported. The control list names neural-network integrated circuits (3A001.a.9) and digital computers above 70 Weighted TeraFLOPS (4A003.b), while the department's indicative list of Jan 17, 2025 puts graphics accelerators and coprocessors among items commonly not controlled, under stated limits, and calls itself no classification ruling. No page read names Nvidia or the H200, rules on any consignment or sets a licence for storage alone. Words: "A valid licence is required for import, export and transhipment (except for certain air-to-air transhipment under specific conditions as stated below) of goods that are listed in Schedule 1 to the Regulations." [cn-ga31-legco-5]; "Transhipment of strategic commodities not granted exemption under the Scheme must be covered by an import and export licence." [cn-ga31-legco-4]; "failure on the part of the carrier to retain any imported strategic commodities before an import licence is produced to him;" [cn-ga31-legco-11] (among the offences); "landed in Hong Kong after its importation and stored, pending exportation" [cn-ga31-legco-18]; "export permit(s) issued by the technology originating and/or exporting countries/places of the goods giving expressive approval to the export concerned" [cn-ga31-legco-19]; "(9) Neural network integrated circuits;" [cn-ga31-legco-10]; "“Digital computers” having an “Adjusted Peak Performance” (“APP”) exceeding 70 Weighted TeraFLOPS (WT)" [cn-ga31-legco-15]; "Graphic accelerators and graphics coprocessors, irrespective of three dimensional vector rate" [cn-ga31-legco-16]; "The above list is indicative rather than exhaustive and should not be regarded as official classification rulings." [cn-ga31-legco-16]; the latest change to Schedule 1 "will come into operation on 30 May 2025" [cn-ga31-legco-1]. Whether these rules reach a given Nvidia part, and whether any licence was sought, granted or refused for the stock reported on Nov 5, 2026, no page says; these are Hong Kong's rules, not the mainland's import rules. Cuts: CONTEXT (the twelve documents' item 7).

Sinomach's site, Feb 11, 2025: its Sixth Design and Research Institute won the Chang'an computing-centre project at Xi'an's aerospace base, 3 billion yuan, planned with 1,500 cabinets, which uses Nvidia H100 and H200 chips. A listing of the Xianyou county investment service centre on the Fujian Department of Commerce's investment site, undated: a project for a computing centre and an AI industrial park seeking investors, 4.5 billion yuan in all, the computing servers of its first phase all H100, its second phase bringing in a Huawei technical team's support (the same site's listing 28235, by the same initiator for the same subdistrict and undated, gives 6.2 billion yuan, all of it sought from investors, about 4,000 PFLOPS of sparse computing power in phase one and its servers all H100 [cn-ga31-centreplans-13], the sum Reuters gives, Tier 2 against-row 32; that the two listings are one project is not shown). Wuxi's city portal, Mar 21, 2025, carrying the Wuxi Daily: a combined 960P Nvidia H800 cluster in use in the Wuxi Economic Development Zone. None says when or how the chips were or will be obtained. - **Notices of Oct 13 to Nov 24, 2026 (a reading sample, not a census).** Of 27 notices opened from universities' own lists and the national procurement site's static lists (1,499 central notices of Nov 18 to 24 and 1,459 local notices of Nov 24), 2 name an Nvidia model (Fudan, four RTX Pro6000 cards; Southeast University, two cards of model H100), 5 name or require home-made computing, and 20 name no accelerator maker or model; 8 refuse imported products, which, as one notice defines it, means the product and "而非构成产品的零件或部件" (not the parts that make it up) [cn-ga13-ccgp-jiangnan-cluster-27734913]; 6 attach a declaration of conformity with the home-made product standard (tables/procurement_notices_oct_nov_2026.tsv).

- **Sept 2024 and Sept 2026 · National Technical Committee 260 on Cybersecurity, AI Safety Governance Framework 1.0 and 3.0, published as PDFs on the cyberspace regulator's site** (technical documents of a state standards body; the saved PDFs do not show the release notices; of the regulator's part, 3.0's foreword says the committee worked under its guidance, "在国家互联网信息办公室的指导下" [cn-ga31-mssposts-10], and the saved text of 1.0 does not name the regulator). Both name the risk to chip supply as other countries' export controls cutting supply off, and both answer it with diverse sources and an open ecosystem, not with home-made chips: 1.0 asks for "close attention" to the supply-chain security of chips, and calls for jointly developing AI chips and an open ecosystem with more diverse sources; 3.0 repeats the risk and calls for an open supply-chain ecosystem for AI chips. Neither names a maker or a country (英伟达 0, 美国 0, 国产 0 and 自主可控 0 in both; control: 芯片 3 and 5). Words: "全国网络安全标准化技术委员会 2024年9月" [cn-ga31-mssposts-3]; "但个别国家利用技术垄断和出口管制等单边强制措施制造发展壁垒,恶意阻断全球人工智能供应链,带来突出的芯片、软件、工具断供风险" [cn-ga31-mssposts-3] (some countries use technology monopolies, export controls and other unilateral coercive measures to build barriers to development and maliciously cut the global AI supply chain, creating marked risks that chips, software and tools are cut off); "对于人工智能系统采用的芯片、软件、工具、算力和数据资源,应高度关注供应链安全" [cn-ga31-mssposts-3]; "共同研发人工智能芯片、框架、软件,引导产业界建立开放生态,增强供应链来源多样性" [cn-ga31-mssposts-3]; "全国网络安全标准化技术委员会 National Technical Committee 260 on Cybersecurity of SAC 2026年9月" [cn-ga31-mssposts-10]; "恶意阻断全球人工智能供应链,带来突出的芯片、软件、工具、服务断供风险" [cn-ga31-mssposts-10]; "应关注其供应链安全风险,采取措施提高供应链的多元化、稳定性" [cn-ga31-mssposts-10]; "持续推进人工智能芯片、框架、软件开放供应链生态建设,增强产品服务供应多样性" [cn-ga31-mssposts-10] (keep building an open supply-chain ecosystem for AI chips, frameworks and software, and make the supply of products and services more diverse). - **Dec 10, 2025 to Mar 13, 2026 · The same top-level texts, on imports and foreign firms; and Vice Premier He Lifeng's special address at Davos (January 2026; the saved page gives no delivery date, only that the World Economic Forum's WeChat account published the full text on the 21st, as a Chinese consulate-general carries it)** (documents and named officials on the record). The work report promises to expand imports actively, to guarantee foreign-funded firms national treatment and to open the digital field in order; the outline adds imports of "advanced technology equipment urgently needed at home", steers foreign capital toward high technology, and, before the commerce ministry's draft list of October 2026, already speaks of exploring offshore computing facilities; Xi's excerpt asks for foreign reinvestment and more local production. He Lifeng told Davos that "in many cases China wants to buy and the other side is unwilling to sell", trade being hit by the stretching of security, that China will expand imports harder, and that firms of every country are welcome to deepen innovation cooperation with China. None of these passages names chips (in the whole Davos text 芯片 0, 半导体 0, 英伟达 0, 出口管制 0; control: 进口 3). Words: "积极扩大进口,推进贸易平衡发展" [cn-ga31-workreport-7]; "保障外资企业国民待遇,实施新版鼓励外商投资产业目录,促进外资境内再投资、扩大本地化生产" [cn-ga31-workreport-7]; "有序扩大数字领域开放" [cn-ga31-workreport-7]; "扩大国内急需的先进技术设备、优质农产品、生产性服务等进口" [cn-ga31-workreport-8]; "引导外资更多投向先进制造、现代服务、高新技术、节能环保等领域" [cn-ga31-workreport-8]; "探索建设离岸算力设施、数据跨境流动服务基础设施" [cn-ga31-workreport-8]; "深化外商投资促进体制机制改革,促进外资境内再投资、扩大本地化生产" [cn-ga31-workreport-57]; "21日,世界经济论坛微信公众号发布《中华人民共和国国务院副总理何立峰在世界经济论坛2026年年会上的特别致辞(全文)》" [cn-ga31-workreport-1]; "但很多时候是中国想买,对方不愿卖,贸易受到“泛安全化”影响" [cn-ga31-workreport-1]; "中国愿发挥超大规模市场优势,更大力度扩大进口,加强产业合作共赢" [cn-ga31-workreport-1]; "欢迎各国企业与中国深化创新合作,共享创新驱动发展机遇" [cn-ga31-workreport-1]. Cuts: AGAINST (the welcoming side, limit 1, at the top in 2026); CONTEXT for limit 4 (the vice premier puts the obstacle on sellers who will not sell, in general words); the offshore clause bears on point 9 (computing abroad).
- **Mar 22 to Oct 9, 2026 · Premier Li Qiang, the State Council's executive meetings and the Party centre and State Council's opinions: the rest of 2026's top-level texts on chips, computing and foreign firms** (Xinhua's readouts and authorised texts; the state planner's catalogue). At the China Development Forum (Mar 22) Li promised to import more good foreign goods; at a Summer Davos business round table (Jun 24) he said China's industrial subsidies treat domestic and foreign-funded firms alike. The executive meeting of Jun 29 on AI asked for faster work on key technologies and very large intelligent-computing clusters, and, in its trade item, for more imports of good goods and services; that of Sept 11 on the computing network asked for research and use of key technologies and equipment and for the market to match supply and demand. The opinions of Oct 9 on new productive forces ask for an efficient supply of computing power and encourage pilot free-trade zones to gather advanced factors from home and abroad. The foreign-investment catalogue in force from Feb 1, 2026 encourages foreign investment in chip design and in making digital chips at 28 nm and below. None names a chip maker or sets a rule on which chips companies may buy (英伟达 0 in each). - **Apr 4, 2025 to Nov 9, 2026 · The Customs Tariff Commission and the customs administration: the published import measure that could reach American chips is a duty by origin, and by customs' origin rule as the chip industry association reads it, a chip's origin is where its wafer was made** (documents: the Tariff Commission's announcements on the finance ministry's site, one as the commerce ministry's law database carries it; the customs administration's explanation of its origin rule as the trade-promotion council's legal platform carries it, posted there on Apr 14, 2025; an industry association's notice as a Zhejiang industry newsletter reprints it). From Apr 10, 2025 the Tariff Commission put an additional duty on every import of US origin, raised within days, cut to 10% from May 14, 2025 with 24 points suspended, and kept so since, most lately for two months from Nov 10, 2026. The announcements read list no goods and no customs numbers: origin decides, not the kind of goods (the first spared goods shipped before 12:01 on Apr 10, 2025 and imported by the end of May 13). For integrated circuits the customs administration's explanation applies the general test, a change of four-digit tariff heading; the China Semiconductor Industry Association's «urgent notice» of Apr 11, 2025 states that as the place where the wafer was fabricated and advises declaring it so for packaged and unpackaged chips alike. The association cites a customs order in force since Jan 1, 2005 (No. 122): it is a reading of a standing rule, not a rule of April 2025, and no numbered customs announcement of that month on chip origin was found (from Jan 1, 2026 the declaration catalogue asks for the wafer's place of manufacture: section 2.4). Words: "对原产于美国的所有进口商品，在现行适用关税税率基础上加征34%关税" [cn-ga31-customstext-15] ("自2025年4月10日12时01分起" [cn-ga31-customstext-15]); "由34%提高至84%" [cn-ga31-customstext-16]; "由84%提高至125%" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-customstext17] (from Apr 12, 2025: "税委会公告2025年第6号" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-customstext17]); "由34%调整为10%" [cn-ga31-customstext-24] ("税委会公告2025年第7号" [cn-ga31-customstext-24]); "自2026年11月10日13时01分起" [cn-ga31-customstext-3], "在2个月内继续暂停实施24%的对美加征关税税率，保留10%的对美加征关税税率" [cn-ga31-customstext-3]; "海关总署关于非优惠原产地规则的解读" [cn-ga31-customstext-23]: "集成电路（税目8542）未列入清单，适用四位税号改变标准" [cn-ga31-customstext-23]; "关于半导体产品“原产地”认定规则的紧急通知" [cn-ga31-customstext-1]: "根据海关总署的相关规定，“集成电路”原产地按照四位税则号改变原则认定，即流片地认定为原产地" [cn-ga31-customstext-1]; "建议：“集成电路”无论已封装或未封装，进口报关时的原产地以“晶圆流片工厂”所在地为准进行申报" [cn-ga31-customstext-1]; "中国半导体行业协会2025年4月11日" [cn-ga31-customstext-1]. By that rule a chip designed by an American firm and made at a foundry outside the United States is not of US origin; Caixin said so of the H20 by name, and CNN of Nvidia's chips (Tier 2, context). None of these texts is a licence, a quota or a ban, and none names a chip maker. - **Jul 22, 2024 to May 9, 2026 · Four more university notices that name the H20: an award of September 2025 for six H20 servers beside four Ascend servers, a lease notice of July 2024, a tender of April 2026 that says the first phase runs on H20 servers, and a lease intention of May 2026** (documents: two universities' own purchasing sites, a tender document on a university's purchasing site, and one university's intention notice as a tender site carries it; each read whole in the saved copy, the tables in the page bodies). (1) The University of the Chinese Academy of Sciences, award notice published "发布日期：2025-09-28" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies8-ucas] and signed by the purchasing centre for central state bodies, "中央国家机关政府采购中心 2025年9月26日" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies8-ucas], project "GC-HGX250783" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies8-ucas], by open tender, budget 13.3 million yuan: the winner's list has six servers, "GPU服务器 戴尔 PowerEdge XE9680 6 1288970" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies8-ucas], each with "8块GPU/FPGA/加速卡-NVIDIA HGX H20 8-GPU SXM 96GB 500W GPUs Assembly" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies8-ucas], beside four inference servers, "AI推理服务器 神州鲲泰 KunTai A924 4 1106500" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies8-ucas], each with "8*Ascend 910B4" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies8-ucas]; "中标（成交）金额： 人民币1311.525900 万元" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies8-ucas]. The award page prints no line on imports (进口 0 times) and does not say where the servers were made; the tender document behind it, which would say whether imported products were allowed, was not reached (the purchasing centre's own page for the award closed the connection on Nov 25, 2026). It is the second award of this university held that names the H20 (the first, of Apr 24, 2025, is in section 2.2). It is dated in September 2025, the month in which, by Alibaba's annual report, the cyberspace regulator requested technology companies to halt purchases of certain chips (section 3); that request, as filed and as reported, was to companies, not universities. (2) Soochow University's purchasing centre, notice of competitive negotiation "大显存GPU服务器租赁 竞争性谈判公告" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies5-suda], "发布时间：2024-07-22" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies5-suda]: a cloud service of two servers for three months, "本项目拟采购的云服务需提供 2 台单卡大显存的 GPU 计算服务器，租赁时长为 3 个月" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies5-suda], "2.2GPU ： NVIDIA HGX H20 8-GPU 96GB" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies5-suda]; a notice of July 2024, read as a notice on the buyer's own site. (3) China Pharmaceutical University, tender document for phase two of its computing centre, cover dated April 2026 ("校方编号：DCHPZB005260070" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies10]), paid from fiscal funds, budget "项目预算：879 万元" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies10], imports not accepted, "是否接受进口产品：不接受" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies10]: it says of phase one "中国药科大学超算中心一期项目采用 H20 服务器作为智算核心" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies10] (phase one uses H20 servers as its intelligent-computing core), with models deployed "基于 CUDA 生态" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies10], and requires of phase two "投标产品在整体架构与技术路线上须与一期保证无缝衔接并实现完全兼容" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies10] (in overall architecture and technical approach, the products bid must join phase one seamlessly and be fully compatible with it); its three GPU servers are set by figures, with no maker named for their cards. This bears on what the phase-one award did not print (section 2.2): the university's own later document says phase one uses H20 servers as its core; it does not name the award or the server bought under it. The phase-one tender held, which took no imports, asked for one GPU server for the phase-one project, of eight cards with at least 96GB each [cn-ga31-noticecopies-11]. (4) Shanghai Jiao Tong University, government-procurement intention for July 2026, signed "上海交通大学 2026-05-09" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies4-www], as a tender site carries it: an item named "英伟达 H20 八卡 GPU 算力服务器租赁服务" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies4-www], "租赁 6~8 台英伟达 H20 八卡 GPU 服务器" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies4-www] (to rent six to eight eight-card Nvidia H20 servers), budget 2.261 million yuan; an intention, not yet a tender, in its own words "本次公开的采购意向是本单位政府采购工作的初步安排" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-noticecopies4-www] (the purchase intention made public is a preliminary arrangement of the unit's government purchasing). None of the four says where a server was made or that one was delivered; two concern leases, one of them only an intention. Cuts: AGAINST a ban reading (limit 2: a central purchasing centre awarded H20 servers in late September 2025, and universities asked for the H20 by name in 2024 and 2026); CONTEXT (limit 3; and a university bought Ascend servers in the same award).

---

## 6. What documents cannot show, and what still rests on reporting

No published instruction from Beijing telling companies not to buy Nvidia chips was found in the 82 policy pages read, nor a published rule on importing them in China's import-licence, automatic-licence and dual-use import lists of 2024 to 2026 or in the banned-import lists in force (section 8), and its spokesmen will not confirm one. Guidance of this kind is given orally. So these points, which the first assessment used, rest on press reports from unnamed people and belong in the second tier:

- which bodies gave the instructions and when (the summer of 2025, the RTX Pro 6000D, January 2026);
- the customs holds and the 600,000 chips in Hong Kong;
- the rule for data centres that took state money;
- the conditional approvals of January 2026 and who granted them;
- the split between the commerce ministry and the security agencies, and the state planner's leading part;
- the "traitors" remark attributed to Vice Premier Ding Xuexiang (held since Nov 20 in the Wall Street Journal's own page, from "people familiar with the message": `TIER2_REPORTING.md`, for-row 4);
- DeepSeek's delayed model and the companies' lobbying;
- the report that leaders took offence at the Commerce Secretary's "addicted" remark;
- the 2027 deadline for state enterprises to replace foreign information technology ("Document 79"). The published Document 79 is now saved from the state-assets regulator's own site, "国资发改革〔2022〕79号关于开展对标世界一流企业价值创造行动的通知" [cn-le27-sasac-doc79-value-creation-notice], "文章来源：改革局发布时间：2023-04-27" [cn-le27-sasac-doc79-value-creation-notice]: a notice on value creation, in which 软件, 芯片, 信创, 替代, 2027 and 国外 each occur 0 times (the control 价值创造 68 times). The 2027 deadline is described, never reproduced, in listed companies' filings, in two state firms' purchase notices as tender sites reproduce them (section 3.4), in brokers' notes and trade press from Dec 7, 2022 (Tier 2, Context), and in one state body's own purchase document, the National Natural Science Foundation's tender of May 2025, which dates the instruction to Sept 30, 2022 and gives the scope as office and management systems, among others, and the PCs that run them, naming no chip (section 9). The first two filings found were Yunyong's, which describes a 2022 notice as requiring "确保到 2027 年央企国企 100% 完成信创替代" (making sure that by 2027 central and state enterprises complete 100% substitution) [cn-policy-yunyong-document79-filing], and iSoftStone's placement prospectus, "2022 年 9 月底国资委下发 79 号文" [cn-le08-isoftstone-placement-20251229] and "政策要求到2027年央企国企100%完成信创替代,替换范围涵盖芯片、基础软件、操作系统、中间件等领域" [cn-le08-isoftstone-placement-20251229]; the US Trade Representative says it "reportedly sets out timelines for using only secure and controllable ICT products" [cn-le03-ustr-nte-2026]. A US research centre, which published the public text in a translation by Etcetera Language Group, Inc., says it "does not mention anything about removing foreign software" [cn-policy-doc79-translation]. The instruction the filing describes is not reproduced in any saved page.
- the window-guidance paper of autumn 2025 reported by Leiphone (no issuer, number or date found; the spring 2025 paper is 发改高技〔2025〕320号, by an official city document's citation, and officials' and a filer's words make it a rule on approval, siting, power efficiency and funding, issued by the state planner with the industry ministry and the data administration: section 2.3);
- the draft national plan for an AI data-centre grid with at least 80% home-made technology (Bloomberg, June 2026, a draft: Tier 2);
- the approval step through the state planner and the condition to buy a share of home-made chips with the H200 (Tier 2);
- the January 2026 pause of H200 orders while regulators set how many home-made chips each buyer must take (Tier 2);
- the trade-talks reading of Beijing's acts: American documents state it (section 4), no Chinese document does; 40 saved official pages on the talks, Chinese and American (readouts, joint statements, fact sheets and six notices of a round to come), name neither Nvidia, the H20 nor the H200 (section 8).

The documents bound these reports from both sides without replacing them. They show the policy the reports describe (sections 2 and 3) and two companies' sworn-to accounts that purchases were stopped; they do not show the mechanics.

**What documents now add (Nov 23, 2026).** Nvidia's filings name the Chinese government as a second approver from Nov 2025, and from Aug 2026 say that the licensed sales were restricted by the PRC government; American documents put 55% of the chips in the applications that the Commerce summary of May 11, 2026 records among applications returned without action (the committee that released the summary says it does not show every application in full), and read the antitrust case as leverage; and the local voucher rules pay for computing that is not home-made too, most of them at one rate for all. None of these is a published Chinese instruction.

---

## 7. What changes in the argument when it rests on documents

1. **Stronger.** The claim no longer needs unnamed sources. The Shanghai reply, the state planner's statement, the purchasing rules and the two filings carry it; so does the US Commerce Secretary's testimony of Apr 22, 2026. The full reading of the policy pages adds Xi's 2016 sentence on internet companies that depend on foreign core components, the 2014 clause "encouraging" internet companies to buy secure-and-reliable systems, the cyberspace regulator's 2026 plan telling its companies to develop high-end AI chips, and an official evaluation list of AI chips with only makers registered in China on it.
2. **Narrower.** The documents support "declared policy pulled against the companies' practice, and regulators stopped certain purchases". They do not support "China will not give import permits" as a published fact: no permit rule for these chips was found (China's published import-licence, automatic-licence and dual-use import lists of 2024 to 2026 and the banned-import lists in force name no computer, computer part or chip: section 8; a temporary restriction outside the lists is lawful, and none was found: section 2.4), and the one company inquiry found asks whether a permit is even required. The full reading sharpens this. Every binding purchase rule read is addressed to state bodies or to public money, and says other units need not apply it. Every home-made percentage found is provincial or municipal. The measures Beijing has published in the chip dispute are export controls of its own. When it barred Micron's products it published the decision; for Nvidia it has published a summons and two investigation notices.
3. **More two-sided.** The best evidence against the position is also official: a 2020 regulatory decision that obliges Nvidia to keep supplying the Chinese market, ministers welcoming Nvidia by name, vouchers that pay for foreign chips, facilitation of computing abroad, and universities buying Nvidia hardware in published notices. Added by the full reading: Xi's own "not behind closed doors" and "can be brought in but must be secure and controllable"; equal treatment of foreign-funded firms in the 20% preference; the vice minister's "the roadmap does not instruct companies"; and Baidu's and Alibaba's statements that their own chips are in use, which point to reasons of their own for moving off Nvidia.
4. **Xi.** Herzinger's point is fully documentary, and so is its limit: Xi's texts set the aim and say home-made products must be used; none decides the Nvidia question.
5. **What Beijing meant.** Several American documents read parts of Beijing's conduct as leverage or retaliation in trade talks: the Trade Representative's 2026 estimate, the US-China commission's 2025 report, CRS, the White House's fact sheet of Nov 1, 2025 and Senator Kaine (section 4). The Trade Representative in May and Dec 2025 and May 2026 called the H200 not negotiated and China's decision «sovereign». The regulator's Party group framed the case as a political task (2.6). Dates of the acts and words sit near the talks (7 of 13 restrictive acts and words within 7 days of an in-person meeting, against 26% of days; tables/talks_and_acts.tsv), but dates cannot show motive. The direction of the policy stands under either reading.

---

## 8. What was looked for and not found

Each item says where it was searched and with what control; none is a universal.

- **No published statement by Xi naming Nvidia or telling firms not to buy.** Five searches of the second round found none, each finding Xi's general speeches with the same searches. A later search repeated the test in the People's Daily database of Xi's speeches and activities (jhsjk.people.cn), exact match, all years: 英伟达, Nvidia, 辉达, 黄仁勋, H20, H200, 进口芯片, 国外芯片, 美国芯片, 不要购买, 停止采购, 禁止采购, 不得采购 each gave 0 items; controls on the same search: 高通 8, 微软 38, 英特尔 4, 国产化替代 11, 超级智能 2. What those results are, from the items opened on Nov 24, 2026: of the 8 for 高通 one names Qualcomm, in the Xinhua report of Xi's meeting of Mar 27, 2024 that People's Daily carried, among the American guests who spoke, not in his words, and seven hold the two characters inside 提高通过 or 提高通关; of the 38 for 微软, 17 show Microsoft in their text and 21 hold the word only in a font name, 微软雅黑; all 4 for 英特尔 name Intel; his own words name Microsoft and Intel in his speech of Apr 19, 2016; of the 2 for 超级智能, one is the readout of Nov 20, 2026, where the word is in its account of Trump's remarks, and one names a factory. The database is not complete: thirteen phrases from ten known Xi texts of the window were searched, and nine of the ten texts were returned; the one missing, his meeting with US executives of May 14, 2026, is held from Xinhua (two of its three phrases returned nothing; the third, 更广阔前景, returned three other texts). In a wider test, of 156 Xi items of 2023 to November 2026 taken from Xinhua's lists of his activities and Qiushi's index by chip, computing-power, AI, US-firm or curb words, the database holds 150; the six it lacks are that meeting and five joint statements he signed; of 1,512 further Xinhua items read in full, 124 hold no such word but 人工智能 (AI), and 21 of them were checked (every eighth, and five joint statements; tables/xi_items_database_check.tsv). A pass over the 7,969 saved files, Xi within 300 characters of an Nvidia term, found none of Xi's own words naming Nvidia (controls: He Lifeng near Nvidia 7 ids, Li Qiang 11). Li Qiang's 2026 work report and He Lifeng's Davos address of January 2026 were read whole on Nov 25, 2026: neither names Nvidia or any foreign chip maker (英伟达 0 in both; controls: 集成电路 2 in the report, 进口 3 in the address) [cn-ga31-workreport-7] [cn-ga31-workreport-1]; what they say is in sections 2.1 and 5. Not searched: the US transcripts from Shenzhen (not yet published). Table: tables/xi_searches.tsv.
- **Every Chinese readout of the Xi-Trump meetings and calls, Oct 30, 2025 to Nov 19, 2026, is silent on chips** (Busan; calls of Nov 24, 2025 and Feb 4, 2026; Beijing, May 14 to 15; Washington, Sept 24; Shenzhen, Nov 19): none mentions chips, semiconductors, export controls or Nvidia; AI appears only as a field for competition and cooperation or for safety, e.g. "中美都是人工智能大国，双方有竞争，更可以合作，应该各展所长而不是相互设防" [cn-xi-round-f-4]. The President's own posts on the two calls list trade, farm goods, fentanyl, Taiwan and Iran: "We discussed many topics including Ukraine/Russia, Fentanyl, Soybeans and other Farm Products, etc." [cn-le22-tt-33994-call-20251124] (the call of Nov 24, 2025); "many important subjects were discussed, including Trade, Military" [cn-le22-tt-36449-call-20260204] and "Taiwan, the War between Russia/Ukraine, the current situation with Iran" [cn-le22-tt-36449-call-20260204] (the call of Feb 4, 2026).
- **No official readout of the US-China trade meetings names Nvidia, the H20 or the H200**: one script pass over the 40 official pages named in the meeting and call rows of tables/talks_and_acts.tsv (rerun on Nov 24, 2026 with the President's six posts on calls and rounds, which the rule does not count, and seven further official pages: none names them; counted by script), Chinese and American, May 2025 to Nov 2026; the same pass found the semiconductor and export-control words in the White House fact sheet of Nov 1, 2025 and the Kuala Lumpur readouts (by a written rule and list). Read on Nov 25, 2026, the commerce ministry's readout of the first meeting of the agricultural working group of the US-China Board of Trade (Beijing, Nov 23 to 24, 2026) names none of them either [cn-ga31-official-mofcom-us-agri-wg-20261125].
- **No published text, image, issuer, number or date of the window-guidance paper of Oct 2025**: Leiphone's page and its two reprints give none; the outlet's «No. 320» is a spring 2025 paper, now named in an official city document as "《关于对算力基础设施实施“窗口指导”的通知》（发改高技〔2025〕320号）" [cn-ga31-cndocs-58] (section 2.3; its own text is not published), which a listed company's feasibility report of Sept 2026 describes as tightening new supply in core regions, toward green and compact building, with no word of a chip rule: "2025年3月出台的“320窗口指导”政策进一步收紧核心区域新增供给" [cn-ga15-jingang-pv-feasibility-20260917]; two local governments' replies describe the national window guidance on computing centres of 2025 in their own words, on siting, size and who pays, with no word on chips: "2025年4月，国家、省相继下发新政策，要求对算力中心建设实施“窗口指导”" [cn-ga31-twelve-14]; "按照现行国家“窗口指导”政策，我省现有算力项目以土建为主，算力设备按需滞后投资" [cn-ga31-replies-6] (section 2.3); search words and the control (the outlet's own page found by 雷峰网 算力情报局 窗口指导; the first search on the headline alone did not return it, so search negatives are weak) are kept in the project's working notes.
- **No published document setting 75% home-made in new state-firm computing centres**: the two seven-body plans of 2026 have 国产 0 and 75% 0 (control: 芯片 3 in the September plan); the claim traces to one site's headline behind a login and to market posts under two different plan titles (Tier 2, leads). The earliest full account found, an item on IT时代网 of Jul 15, 2026, ends with the site's own note that a model wrote it, "本回答由AI生成，仅供参考，请仔细甄别，谨慎投资。" [cn-ga31-nameddocs-9], and is not cited as press. A second search of Nov 25, 2026 found no official page with either title or with a 75% purchase share. The one 75% found in a national computing text of 2026 measures something else: the industry ministry's opinions on AI and information and communications aim, by 2028, at "城域算力1毫秒时延圈覆盖率不低于75%" [cn-le27-miit-ai-ict-2026-pdf] (at least 75% coverage of the one-millisecond latency zone for metropolitan computing); nothing found ties that figure to the market claim.
- **No published document of the industry ministry or the state-assets regulator sets a home-made share for chips**: 1,397 MIIT list entries (Jan 3, 2023 to Nov 20, 2026; on Nov 25, 2026 the first page of each of the same twelve columns shows one later entry, of Nov 24, not on chips), 90 MIIT replies to deputies' proposals, the SASAC policy library (17 entries since 2023) and 11 SASAC replies read; share words in 54 saved MIIT texts are never near 国产, 本国 or 芯片 (control: the same count finds 56 share phrases).
- **Xiang Ligang's reported call that buyers must apply to the government was not traced** to his own words or a state outlet; his on-record words in state outlets of Aug and Dec 2025 do not include it; Weibo bars automated agents.
- **No published counterpart of the reported rule that state-funded data centres use only home-made chips**: the state planner's 225 notices of Aug 16, 2023 to Nov 23, 2026 read by title (control: the 1779 title found on page 7); the finance ministry's 58 procurement documents (control: the desktop standard found); the energy-saving measures 1228 set no chip condition. Nor was a provincial or city text found that makes home-made chips a condition of state money for computing centres (a search of Nov 25, 2026: searches of provincial and city development-commission and data-bureau notices, central-budget and special-bond texts and tenders after Nov 2025; control: the same searches found a college's tender requiring "CPU和AI芯片全国产" [cn-ga31-localrule-13], section 2.3): the provincial and city texts found urge a higher home-made rate without a share (Fujian, Jining), and Sichuan's provincial money for large centres sets no chip condition (section 2.3). The national hub launch letter for the Yangtze River Delta, "发改高技〔2022〕211号" [cn-ga31-localrule-29], sets rack use and power efficiency and no chip condition: 芯片, 国产 and 自主可控 occur 0 times in it (control: "数据中心平均上架率不低于65%" [cn-ga31-localrule-29]); the search read the other seven hub letters of 2021 to 2022 as the same, and they are not saved here.
- **No tender found that refuses Nvidia by name** among the 41 procurement records then in the table and 81 Wuhan University notices read (the ten rows added on Nov 24, 2026 were not part of this search), nor among 40 notices and filings of 2024 to Nov 2026 read on Nov 25, 2026 (tables/procurement_notices_sweep.tsv), nor among the 34 saved pages of a further search of that day (a script looked for a refusal word within 40 characters of an Nvidia word and found one window, a price before tax beside 英伟达; controls: a planted refusal and Qingdao University's «非4090D型号» beside an Nvidia card are found; two of those pages, of Sept and Oct 2026, ask for the H20 by name: section 5); six of the 40, Nov 2025 to Nov 2026, require Nvidia hardware by name, one with brand and model fixed (Hubei University, Oct 8, 2026: "是否进口 是否限定品牌型号 计量单位 采购数量 售后服务 信息化设备零部件 显卡 否 是 个 3" [cn-ga31-tenders-35]); «no imports» lines sit beside Nvidia cards (section 2.2).
- **For six of the ten largest award or candidate records that name no chip, no chip was found; for three more, one supplier group names the chip of its own share**: of the ten largest such records in tables/procurement_records.tsv (chosen by a written rule of selection: tables/largest_records_naming_no_chip.tsv), a further search found the chip for one, China Mobile's Hohhot purchase: in part by one of the two products with which FiberHome says its unit Yangtze Computing won lot 1 (FiberHome's own account, as the financial site Tonghuashun carries it), "G940K V2风冷超节点服务器" [cn-le17-10jqka-c679451486], which by the post that launched it runs on home-made NPUs, the maker not named, "依托“鲲鹏+国产NPU”双引擎" [cn-ga13-eastmoney-caifuhao-cjcomputing-g940k-20251125], and for the whole purchase by a news site's sentence (section 3.6, Aug 26, 2026); one names only a product line (Atlas). A search of Nov 25, 2026 then found, for three of the remaining eight, the chip of one supplier group's share in its own news items: Shenzhou Kuntai (Xiamen), a Digital China subsidiary, of China Mobile's 2024-2025 purchase, lot 1 (a 10.53% share, as one of the named candidates), "本次中标的产品为神州鲲泰KunTai A924 系列AI训练服务器" [cn-ga31-tenders-40], and Digital China (China), another subsidiary, of the 2023-2024 trial network, lot 12 (8.48%, as one of the named candidates; the product a Shenzhou Kuntai server), "是基于鲲鹏+昇腾主板打造的AI训练服务器" [cn-ga31-tenders-48] (both items headed Kuntai news, on the DCN site); and, on Digital China Group's own page, Shenzhou Kuntai of the 20,480-card supernode purchase of 2026, "中标金额13.17亿元，中标设备搭载共计2048张昇腾950DT" [cn-ga31-tenders-4]. A share named by its winner is not the lot; five remain not found. A winner of China Telecom Ningxia's Token-factory purchase says it made no separate announcement, the contract being below the exchange's disclosure line [cn-ga13-10jqka-c676815169-token].
- **No report was found that any of the eight provincial shares counted on Nov 24, 2026 was met** (web searches of Nov 24, 2026, and pages read by keyword; not a script over saved pages): no government statement was found, for any of the eight, that its home-made share was met or what the share now is. Zhejiang's share, counted since Nov 25, 2026, is a target for 2030 and was not part of this search. Control: a search of the same form found a reported home-made rate for Guizhou, which sets no share. The searches and the control are kept in the project's working notes.
- **No Chinese government statement found that forbids or discourages training abroad**: the search and its control are kept in the project's working notes. The only Chinese rule touching it is the draft list of Oct 16, 2026, which speaks of deploying models abroad.
- **Among chip filers only Nvidia has «Chinese government» with «discouraged»** (SEC full-text search, read through a web-reading tool, not saved: section 10); Chinese-government purchase restrictions on their own products are not stated in the filings of Super Micro, Dell, TSMC, Samsung, Intel or Broadcom read (controls kept in the project's working notes; Samsung's reports have no «Chinese» at all while «China» appears 174 and 173 times).
- **Of 15 US-listed Chinese companies' 2025 annual reports, only Alibaba's has a sentence on a Chinese authority asking Chinese technology companies to stop buying certain chips**; the Alibaba hit is the control. Caution: the Chinese-language Hong Kong reports of Alibaba, Kingsoft and Li Auto have no full risk-factor sections; Alibaba's Chinese annual report for the year to Mar 31, 2026 gives the risks only as a summary ("風險因素摘要載列如下") and has 停止採購 0, 若干芯片 0 and 美光 0 (control: 網信辦 25) [cn-ga15-alibaba-fy2026-annual-zh], so the 20-F's sentence on the cyberspace regulator stands in the English filing only.

Batches 1, 7, 8 and 9 and the used-goods list, searched the same way, have no computer or chip line; the used-goods list names only monitors and a colour projector, under 8528, made for computers, "可直接连接且设计用于税目 84.71 的自动数据处理设备的彩色液晶监视器" [cn-ga31-importcat-12]. A compliance site's copy carries the announcement with both its annexes, "自 2006 年 1 月 1 日起实施" [cn-ga31-customstext-31]. - **No spokesperson's answer on Nvidia's chips confirms or denies a Chinese restriction, an instruction or an approval, Dec 2024 to Sept 13, 2026** (a census: the foreign ministry's regular press conferences of the period, 411 by the count of the search that read them whole on Nov 25, 2026, of which 45 pages matched its chip words, 英伟达, H20, H200, RTX and others among them, controls the known answers of Aug 13 and Dec 10, 2025; and the commerce ministry's 31 regular briefings of Dec 2024 to Aug 2025 with the written answers that searches find, which have no full list, to which a second search of Nov 25, 2026 added its regular briefings of 2025 and 2026 read at their dated addresses (39 and 28 by that search's count; two exchanges of May and July 2025 that the first reading passed over are among the six it added); every answer saved and its words checked by script; table tables/spokespeople_answers_census.tsv). Of 30 exchanges on Nvidia's chips, on American chips for China or on China buying them (20 foreign ministry, 10 commerce ministry; 9 already in this file), 9 refer the question to the departments in charge, 6 say the spokesperson is not aware, 2 have no information to offer, 4 restate China's position on American chip exports, 1 declines to comment on a company, and 8 answer otherwise (American curbs, four times; asking Washington to lift more; noting a report and praising cooperation; the leaders' agenda; the trade talks in general). Six of the 30, all at the commerce ministry's regular briefings, were added by a second search of Nov 25, 2026 (May 22 and Jul 31, 2025; Jan 22, Mar 19, Jun 4 and Jul 16, 2026: the entry below on press conferences). Two more, at the foreign ministry, were added the same day, when the pages that had matched were read a second time by the written rule, by three readers who were not told the table's rows and who found all 18 exchanges of the first reading: Jan 15, 2025, asked about a planned American rule against selling advanced chips to China, "中方已多次就美方恶意封锁打压中国半导体产业表明严正立场。" [cn-ga31-spokes-66]; and May 16, 2025, asked about an American notice on Huawei's Ascend chips and on training Chinese models with American AI chips, "美方泛化国家安全概念、滥用出口管制和长臂管辖" [cn-ga31-spokes-78]. Outside the rule, and so outside the count: a question of Dec 2, 2024 on American controls aimed at makers of chip-making equipment [cn-ga31-spokes-21], and one of Jan 16, 2025 on American controls of advanced computing semiconductors that names no destination [cn-ga31-spokes-67]. Three more questions that name Nvidia's chips, about alleged evasion of American export controls (Jun 19 and Aug 7, 2025; May 22, 2026), are outside this count; their answers say nothing of a Chinese restriction either [cn-ga31-spokes-81] [cn-ga31-spokes-86] [cn-tolerate-guo20260522]. New words: Jul 15, 2025, asked about Nvidia's announcement that the US government had allowed it to sell China a lower-end AI chip, "我们一般不对企业行为作具体评论。" [cn-ga31-spokes-20]; Jul 31, 2025, asked about the summons, "具体问题请你向中方主管部门进行了解。" [cn-ga31-spokes-10] (in the page's HTML; its text extraction leaves the answer out), and at the commerce ministry, as China Fund News reports it (not in the posted transcript), "关于你提问的问题，目前没有可提供的信息。" [cn-ga31-spokes-77]; Jul 10, 2025, on Huang's reported meeting with the commerce minister, "关于你提到的问题，目前我没有可以提供的信息。谢谢。" [cn-ga31-spokes-48]; Aug 11, 2025, on a report that Nvidia and AMD had agreed to pay the US government 15% of their revenue from chip sales in China in exchange for export licences, "在美输华芯片问题上，中方已经多次表明了立场。" [cn-ga31-spokes-88]; Aug 12, 2025, on a China version of Blackwell, "具体问题请向中方主管部门询问。" [cn-ga31-spokes-90]; Oct 29, 2025, "中方是否欢迎美国在华销售人工智能芯片？" answered "具体问题建议向中方主管部门询问。中方已多次表明在美输华芯片问题上的原则立场，希望美方以实际行动维护全球产供链稳定。" [cn-ga31-spokes-103]; Dec 5, 2025, "中国是否愿意并准备购买上述芯片？" (AMD's MI308), answered with the position and "具体情况建议向中方主管部门询问。" [cn-ga31-spokes-108]; Dec 10, 2025, on the Financial Times's report that China was weighing limits on H200 purchases, "我们昨天已就有关问题作出回应。具体情况建议向中方主管部门询问。", and "中方对购买英伟达芯片有没有安全关切？" answered "我不了解你提到的具体情况。" [cn-ga31-spokes-6]; Jan 15, 2026, on the H200 and the 25% share, "关于美国输华芯片问题，中方已经多次表明立场。" [cn-ga31-spokes-3]; Feb 24, 2026, "中国公司有不违反出口管制使用Blackwell芯片的途径吗？" answered "我不了解具体情况。关于美国输华芯片问题，中方已经多次表明原则立场。" [cn-ga31-spokes-36]; May 15, 2026, "中美元首会晤是否谈及美对华半导体出口管制问题？" answered "中方已多次表明在美输华芯片问题上的原则立场。" [cn-ga31-spokes-51]. And on Dec 5, 2024, asked how it viewed industry associations' call to buy American chips with caution, the commerce ministry answered about American export controls, "12月2日再次发布对华半导体出口管制措施" [cn-ga31-early2024-2]. With the census of Sept 14 to Nov 25, 2026 (above), the foreign ministry's regular press conferences have now been read for Dec 2024 to Nov 25, 2026. Not reached: a full list of the commerce ministry's written answers (searched by words; controls: its Jul 18, 2025 answer on the H20 and its Sept 13, 2025 statements on chips are found). Twelve more of its answers found by the second search, on American export-control measures from January to December 2025 (the January rule on AI exports, design software, the entity and end-user lists, tariffs on Chinese semiconductors), are outside the count: an exchange counts when the question's own words name Nvidia, an American chip, or chips with their export to, sale in or purchase by China; two of the commerce ministry's rows are counted on a wider reading of those words, May 22, 2025 (American export controls on AI chips, with no destination named in the question) and Jul 31, 2025 (high-end chips as one subject of the two countries' trade talks). Cuts: CONTEXT (supports «confirmed nothing» as a census; no answer denies the reports either).

A search of Nov 25, 2026 read whole the announcements of the customs administration, alone or with other bodies, whose titles or annexes touch chips, servers, classification, origin, inspection, tariff exclusions or banned goods, and the Customs Tariff Commission's announcements of 2025 and 2026 on duties on goods of US origin. The pages it read are saved: 131 (the last 17 of them saved afterwards from other addresses: the next entry), of which 97 on government sites (86 of mainland bodies, 8 of Hong Kong's Trade and Industry Department and 3 of the United States: 2 of the White House and 1 of the Federal Register; table tables/customs_and_tariff_texts_read.tsv: each page counted as a visitor sees it, each text once; a reprint on another site of an official text counts as not on a government site). The search's list names by number 35 announcements and orders of the customs administration (31 of them of 2024 to 2026) and 16 announcements of the Customs Tariff Commission among the pages saved; the rest are annexes, explanations, lists and catalogues, and a few press and company pages read beside them. Words that name Nvidia or a model (英伟达, 英偉達, 輝達, Nvidia, H20, H200, H100, H800, RTX) stand on 1 of the 97: the commerce ministry's copy of the white paper of April 2025, which cites Nvidia against the American rules, "美国芯片巨头英伟达表示，新规实际上威胁了全球创新和经济增长，并使其失去中国市场" [cn-ga31-customstext-81]. No announcement of the customs administration or of the Tariff Commission among them names the company, a chip model or a card. Customs numbers under 8471, 8473 or 8542 stand on 15 of the 97, and none of the 15 is a list of goods needing a licence: tax-relief and zero-tariff lists (Hengqin, Hainan, science and teaching imports, border trade), agreement tariff schedules and origin rules (Congo, 20 African countries, Peru), the 2020 list of goods eligible for exclusion from the counter-duties on US goods, the opening of the anti-dumping case on American analog chips (85423990), the list of goods that may be repaired in comprehensive bonded zones, "23 8471800000其他自动数据处理设备的部件" [cn-ga31-customstext-22], and the 2015 schedule of USED goods inspected lot by lot at the port, "国家质量监督检验检疫总局公告2015年第76号" [cn-ga31-customstext-69], which has "3 8471.3010-8471.5090 计算机类设备" and "17 8542.3100-8542.9000 集成电路及微电子组件" [cn-ga31-customstext-69] (used goods only; section 2.4). Besides the lists of goods that may not be imported and that port inspection of used goods (after which goods that fail are destroyed or sent back), the one text found among them that has customs withhold release of goods while a doubt about them is checked is for exports: "出口货物发货人申报出口时，如未向海关交验由商务部颁发的出口许可证件，海关有证据表明出口货物可能属于两用物项出口管制范围的，应当向出口货物发货人提出质疑" [cn-ga31-customstext-86] ("商务部海关总署公告2026年第52号" [cn-ga31-customstext-86], posted Oct 19, 2026). No like text for imports was found. Controls: the same counts find NVIDIA in a customs district's explainer (section 2.4; on a trade-promotion committee's site, so not among the 97) and 8471800000 in the Ningbo decision already held [cn-ga31-customs-9]. What this does not reach, by the search's own account: it is not a census of every numbered announcement (the customs administration's own index and several of its pages timed out to the search; it worked from the E-Port and Zhejiang single-window indexes and from reprints); the Single Window's live lists of goods under inspection were not read; the customs notice behind the tariff exemption of April 2025 that import agents reported (Tier 2) was not found; and five of the saved annexes are cut short in the saved copy, so an absence in them proves nothing (cn-ga31-customstext-25, -41, -75, -110 and cn-o42-hk-tid-circular-2026-03). used goods only); the notice of 2021 of the finance ministry, the customs administration and the tax administration that frees from import duty the own-use equipment of chip makers that meet its line-width conditions, of advanced packaging and testing firms and of the key chip design and software firms the state encourages (goods on three lists excepted) to Dec 31, 2030, "本通知自2020年7月27日至2030年12月31日实施" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-customstext124-http]; and announcement 2024 No. 116 on classification decisions, whose annexes were not reached [cn-ga31-unsaved3-customstext66-mofcom]. None of the nine names a chip model, a graphics card or a server (the schedule for travellers' articles lists a processor and function cards among computer parts, "16010440 ---功能卡 块 300 13%" [cn-ga31-unsaved3-customstext30-newform], with no maker), and none names Nvidia (counted on each page as a visitor sees it: 英伟达, 英偉達, 輝達, Nvidia, H20, H200, H100, H800, RTX 0 on all nine; 芯片, 显卡, 图形处理器 and 服务器 0 on all nine; a customs number under 8471, 8473 or 8542 on one, the used-goods schedule).
