## 9. Saved, but the words are not checkable by cn_has.py

Page images, Word files, HTML stored as body.pdf, Archive captures kept in the project's working folders, raw data. Not yet Tier 1 by this file's rule; they may yet be filed as saved sources, or made readable by cn_has.py.

- **Kessler's written answers on the June 2025 hearing record** (ms-g17-x3-govinfo-5def8e, PDF pages 44, 45 and 50, page images; words from a transcription kept beside the saved copy; pages 44, 45 and 50 read a second time on Nov 25, 2026 from the page images, word for word the same (on p. 45 the same answer sentence stands three times, under 4.d.i, 4.e and 4.f.i); govinfo's HTML text, ms-g17-x10-govinfo-c8fdb0, has the appendix only as "[GRAPHICS NOT AVAILABLE IN TIFF FORMAT]"): US export controls «have motivated China to pursue greater self-sufficiency in key technologies and industries», and selling less advanced chips may serve American aims where it takes market share from Chinese chip makers. Cuts: AGAINST (limit 4).
- **The Trade Representative's 2025 report on China's WTO compliance** (cn-le03-ustr-wto-2025, cover dated March 2026, p. 57, page images; a transcription kept beside the saved copy; p. 57, PDF page 65, read a second time on Nov 25, 2026 from the page image, word for word the same): the same retaliation passage as the 2026 estimate.
- **Beijing E-Town's application guides and Beijing municipal voucher notes** (Word files: cn-le15-bj-etown-202603-doc, cn-le15-bj-etown-202608-doc, cn-le15-bj-gov-202404-doc, cn-le15-bj-jxj-202508-doc; text extracted from the files and checked by script, 16 of 16): E-Town's guides quote one 30% rate; the city's notes 20%.
- **The English annual reports of Baidu, Kingsoft Cloud, GDS, VNET, Li Auto, NIO and XPeng** (stored as body.pdf or body.bin): scripts/cn_has.py now reads these bodies (cn_srclib, since Nov 23) and finds all 15 quotations of these reports (Nov 25, 2026: "19 quotations checked, 19 found"). Baidu, «If we are unable to procure sufficient quantities of chips with advanced semiconductors due to export controls»; Li Auto, «Our current high-end models rely on chips produced by NVIDIA to a great extent». (These can leave section 9.)
- **The commerce ministry's draft list, annex 1 of announcement 2026 No. 48** (a WPS file, cn-le20-mofcom-48-annex1; text extracted from the file and checked by script): restricted are large models that provide services after training on China's important data (except a trace use, at most 0.1%, under conditions, and data that can lawfully be had in public) and the core technology of models of 20,000 hundred-million parameters or more; prohibited, large models that provide services after training on China's core data; listed too, computing-cluster technology for clusters over 10,000 accelerator cards.
- **Northwestern Polytechnical University's notices** (Archive captures, first kept in the project's working folders): an award of Oct 21, 2026 for two NVIDIA H200 141G PCIe cards at 437,350 yuan each, bought from a local firm; a result notice of Jan 28, 2026 for HGX H200 server rental, the service to run from Feb 16 to Sept 16, 2026. The press account is checkable: "是向「陝西博算網絡科技」購買，而非直接向輝達採購" [cn-le12-udn-nwpu-h200-9872002]; and Reuters, against-row 7 of Tier 2. The award notice of Oct 21, 2026 itself and the rental results of Jan 28, 2026 have since been filed as saved ids (Internet Archive captures; the schools' own pages no longer open) and are checkable (cn_has.py finds all three, Nov 25, 2026; this bullet can leave section 9): "NVIDIA H200 141G PCIe显卡2片" [cn-ga13-nwpu-som-88690-wayback]; "NVIDIA HGX H200 8-GPU SXM 141GB" [cn-ga13-nwpu-cyber-17680-wayback] (the argument's point 19; tables/procurement_records.tsv).
- **Singapore's official trade tables** (SingStat API answers kept in the project's working folders with their sha256; the API's terms led the project to keep them out of the saved sources, though one answer of the same API, the table's metadata, was saved through the project's page saver, which sends a browser's name, as cn-le18-sg-T010001-metadata; on Nov 25, 2026 no public page was found that holds the figures: SingStat's page for the October release gives only the headline and points to Enterprise Singapore's release, whose robots file bars scripts [cn-ga31-data-sg-singstat-news-trade-oct-2026; cn-ga31-data-sg-enterprisesg-robots]): re-exports to mainland China under line 84718010 (control and adaptor units) of S$17.97 million in Jan to Jun 2026 and S$3,036.2 million in Jul to Oct 2026 (23,981 units). The line's name is checkable: "CONTROL & ADAPTOR UNITS OF AUTOMATIC DATA PROCESSING MACHINES (NMB)" [cn-le18-sg-T010001-metadata]. The tables name no maker; set beside Nvidia's «$2.4 billion» of licensed China sales it is not a match (different periods, customs value against sales, any maker). Cuts: CONTEXT; AGAINST only a reading that nothing came in.
- **Hygon's investor-meeting record of Oct 23, 2026** (a Word file [cn-ga31-data-hygon-ir-record-20261026-doc], its text extracted with LibreOffice and kept beside the file): revenue of 149.55亿元 in January to September 2026, the fast growth of its results put down to «受益于国内高端芯片需求攀升»; asked whether customers' cost and time of migrating from CUDA had improved, Hygon says the work, tuning time and cost of migrating models «整体呈现持续改善趋势». Cuts: AGAINST (as 3.7) and CONTEXT.
- **China customs, table 14, the computer-parts line (8473.30)**: imports of US$82.2 billion in Jan to Sep 2026 against US$18.9 billion a year earlier, while weight fell; figures by script over the saved issues (cn-o34-gacc-t14-imports-202609, cn-le18-gacc-t14-202509). The row texts are found by cn_has.py itself: "Parts and accessories of automatic data processing machines T 4,460 16,652,479 33,439 82,203,919 5.2 536.9" [cn-o34-gacc-t14-imports-202609]; in the other table the row ends "T 4,247 2,621,216 36,515 18,890,284 9.8 3.9" [cn-le18-gacc-t14-202509] (Nov 25, 2026). No line can count Nvidia chips. Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Voucher rules of the second voucher search held as Word, spreadsheet or .wps files, or in a working folder** (Beijing E-Town's guides of Dec 2024, Aug 2025 and Nov 2025; Longgang's 2025 guide; Shenzhen's 2026 comment draft and its 2025 payout spreadsheet; Xiamen's 厦府办规〔2025〕5号, held here until Nov 25, 2026 as an Archive copy in a working folder, is now a saved source whose words cn_has.py finds [cn-ga31-unsaved-xm-5hao-2025-wayback] and has left this list): words checked by script against text extracted from the files and from Archive copies (rerun on Nov 23, 2026: «38 quotations checked, 38 found, 0 not found; controls behave»), not by cn_has.py; re-checked on Nov 25 against an independent extraction (python zipfile and UTF-16 decoding, no LibreOffice): 58 of 59 Word, zip and spreadsheet quotations of four searches found, the 59th, an annex, differing only by a footnote numeral; their rates are in the voucher table of section 5. Xiamen's 2025 rule may have been repealed from Apr 1, 2026 by 厦府办规〔2026〕3号: a search tool's summary says so; the 2026 text has not been read (lead).
- **May 2025 · National Natural Science Foundation of China, tender for its 2025 cloud-desktop expansion: a state body's own purchase document that cites «Document 79» with a date, a deadline and a scope** (Internet Archive captures of Jul 16, 2026 of the foundation's tender PDF [cn-ga31-inta2-nsfc-tender-pdf-wayback] and of its award notice of Jun 12, 2025 [cn-ga31-inta2-nsfc-award-94593-wayback], which lists that PDF as attachment 2, "基金委云桌面扩容招标文件（最终）.pdf"; the foundation's site timed out when fetched). In its background section (PDF page 73 to 74) the foundation writes that SASAC issued document 〔2022〕79 on Sept 30, 2022, requiring all central firms' information systems to be home-made by end-2027, the replacement covering portals, office, e-mail, archives, Party and mass work, discipline inspection and business management, so that PCs, the clients for these, must be replaced too. Words: "2022 年 9 月 30 日，国资委发布【2022】79 号文件，要求 2027 年底前，实现所有中央企业的信息化系统完成国产替代。"; "整体替换范围包含门户网站、OA、邮件、档案、党群、纪检、经营管理等系统，而 PC 作为承载上述应用的客户端的重要接入载体，国产替代也势在必行。"; "采 购 人：国家自然科学基金委员会"; "2025 年 5 月". This is the only state body's own text found that states the 2027 deadline; it describes SASAC's instruction in its own words and is not that instruction; its date (Sept 30) differs from a vendor's (Sept 28) and the scope it gives includes office and management systems, among others, and the PCs that run them; the passage does not mention data-centre or AI chips. SASAC's own published 〔2022〕79号 is the value-creation notice (section 6). A search named this page; the captures were saved and read on Nov 25, 2026. Cuts: CONTEXT.
- **Apr 24, 2025 · CAS Center for Excellence in Molecular Plant Sciences (中国科学院分子植物科学卓越创新中心), «仪器设备采购活动记录» for a GPU server** (a state research institute's own purchase record, posted among its notices; cn-ga31-intd-sippe-a100-wayback, an Internet Archive capture of Apr 29, 2025 of a scanned two-page PDF with no text layer; read by eye twice from the rendered pages, at 80 and at 110 dots an inch, and transcribed by hand; no OCR run; the transcription is kept beside the scan). The institute compared three servers: a Zhongke Kekong (中科可控) X7850H0 with eight home-made DCU K100 cards, which it says reach over half an A800's performance on AlphaFold3; a server on Huawei's platform with eight Ascend 910B modules, at 0.8 to 1.1 times an A800 on mainstream PyTorch models; and an H3C UniServer R5350 G6 with four A100 80G cards. It chose the H3C server as the best value and the closest fit to the software its researchers use (AlphaFold, RoseTTAFold, Relion, Cryosparc, DeepVariant, Parabricks), and notes that Zhongke Kekong and H3C both have state-capital backgrounds. Words as transcribed: "实测国产 DCU K100 计算卡在运算 AlphaFold3 时性能能达到 A800 计算卡的 50%性能以上"; "4 块 A100 80G GPU 卡"; "选择采购新华三 H3C UniServer R5350 G6"; "更贴近我单位科研用户实际应用中的 AlphaFold、RoseTTAFold、Relion、Cryosparc、DeepVariant、Parabricks 等软件适配需求"; "中科可控与新华三都是国资背景" [cn-ga31-intd-sippe-a100-wayback]. A choice recorded, not a delivery; the form does not say where the cards came from. Cuts: AGAINST a ban reading for state bodies (a CAS institute picks Nvidia over two home-made options), FOR the migration-cost line of point 6 (software fit decides).

## 11. Leads only (seen in a search summary or behind a wall; not facts)

- Sichuan 川发改数据〔2026〕338号 is the computing-network measures already held (tables/local_vouchers.tsv, row 20; cn-ga05-sichuan-compute-net-measures-jiemian): the commission's own page, saved from an Archive capture of Oct 16, 2026 (cn-ga31-unsaved-sc-338hao-fgw-wayback), gives the number, 成文日期 2026-08-11 and 发布日期 2026-08-13, and the same voucher sentence; it names the five signing bodies too (in the page's body; its text extract leaves them out), as does a policy site's copy (cn-ga31-unsaved-sc-338hao-ruichuangshe). The company list's "20 to 40% of investment for projects using home-made chips" is the voucher for buying home-made computing (up to 20%, up to 30% for start-ups in their first three years, up to 40% for one-person AI firms, at most 1 million yuan a unit a year), misdescribed. Shanghai's 智算中心建设导则（2025年版） (Jan 2025; numbered 沪经信基〔2025〕16号 by two summaries, the number not printed in the guide): the commission's own PDF addresses answer 404 (cn-ga31-unsaved-sheitc-pdf-c653e98e; cn-ga31-replies-21), but the full 67-page guide is held as the site of Invest Shanghai carries it (cn-ga31-replies-22; section 2.3): organised by the commission and written by the China Academy of Information and Communications Technology, it sets no home-made share and no tiers by one; what it calls the entry requirement (准入要求) for a new centre is energy efficiency (baseline PUE), beside general requirements; it recommends (宜) independently controllable accelerator cards and, for edge centres, home-made chips. The same commission's census notice of 2025 asks each facility for its number of chips and their home-made rate (沪经信基〔2025〕235号, cn-ga31-unsaved-sheitc-pdf-6e6a7f6f).
- A national intelligent voucher (国家智能券) cited by Shanghai's and Hangzhou's texts: its founding text not found. A national voucher programme is now on the record under the name 人工智能券（算力券）, which the 国家智能券 of those texts appears to be (no page read says so): the state planner said on Aug 29, 2025 that it would keep issuing it, and Zhejiang's development commission, in a notice held as a reprint carries it, says it is paid from ultra-long special treasury bonds and covers all independent (自主) computing and some high-performance computing that is not (section 5). In 2026 Shanghai's development and reform commission uses what appears to be a third name for it (again, no page read says so), in a notice of "2026年05月13日" [cn-ga31-nameddocs-5]: "关于上海2026年度国家人工智能算力服务补贴项目申报情况的公示" [cn-ga31-nameddocs-5] (the national subsidy for AI computing services); and Hangzhou's industry bureau, in a notice as The Paper carries it, makes its own matching money depend on it, "申报主体为获得2025年国家人工智能券补助的企业" [cn-ga31-nameddocs-3]. A second search of Nov 25, 2026 still found no founding text (issuer, number or date) and no list of the card models that local notices say are restricted; it found Shenzhen's and Zhejiang's notices, so the search could see such pages.
- A 2026 Beijing note excluding devices national departments flag as security risks: found and saved (cn-ga04-bj-jxj-voucher-note2026-docx, a Word file): "对于国家有关部门明确声明存在安全隐患或不建议使用的算力设备，本政策不予支持。" (words found in two separate extractions of the file, an earlier one and one of Nov 25, 2026; not yet by cn_has.py).
- The Zhihu page titled 算力窗口指导文件 that may reproduce the Oct 2025 paper (the page answered 403).
- Cyber Security Association of China: (a) its statement of Oct 16, 2024 recommending a cybersecurity review of Intel's products sold in China, held only as a search's account of a reprint (https://www.xkb.com.cn/articleDetail/349798, which answered 403 when fetched on Nov 25, 2026; the association's own page, https://www.cybersac.cn/detail/1846721771810627586, refused the search tool); by the search's reading it names Intel and gives buyers no instruction to stop buying. (b) Its group standard T/CSAC 024—2025 for the security of data-centre AI accelerator chips, published Nov 28, 2025, in force May 28, 2026, which by the search's reading says that taking part in its security assessment is voluntary and lists Alibaba Cloud, T-Head, Meituan, iFlytek and SenseTime among its drafters (https://www.ttbz.org.cn/Home/PdfFileStreamGet/c3QsMTUyMTI5, 504 when fetched on Nov 25, 2026). Leads only: if saved, (a) would show a Chinese security-side body naming an American chip maker for review, and the maker it names is Intel, not Nvidia.
- China Telecom's N-series tender of Nov 2026 in two other tender sites' copies (采招网's page for Beijing's Xicheng district, 110102.bidcenter.com.cn, and 销邦, ixbang.com): a search of Nov 25, 2026 reports that both print clause 2.3.1 with the wider words, products of the PRC or of countries or regions trading normally with it, among masked characters and unfilled template fields. Both pages answered with an access check when fetched and are not saved (the folders cn-ga31-carrierown-1 and cn-ga31-carrierown-4 hold only the check pages), and the carrier's own page gave no readable text. The copy held prints the narrower clause and holds the wider words nowhere, shown or hidden [cn-ga31-operators-132]. If the two copies print the form's unselected options, as the copy of Guangdong's tender does in hidden text (section 3.6), they do not contradict it; if they print the tender as issued, they contradict it. Not settled: until the carrier's own notice or the tender document is read, the PRC-only clause of the N-series tender rests on one tender site's copy.
